Additionally, graphs turn out to be nearly isomorphic for the same set of input states which has potential applications for developing Quantum Algorithms.
  Keywords: Quantum Algorithms, Hadamard Matrices, Unitary Matrix Approach, Topological Structure of Superpositions (TSS), Graph Theory

</details>


### [99] [Why Are We Unrolling? The Importance of Structured Quantum Programs for Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16171)
*Damian Rovara,Daniel Haag,Mark Koch,Josh Izaac,Seyon Sivarajah,Robert Wille,Augustín Borgna,Lukas Burgholzer,Brad Chase,Olivia Di Matteo,David Ittah*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文主张量子编译应超越直线式量子电路表示，转而采用包含经典结构与动态性的结构化程序表示，并以此作为容错量子计算编译的默认形式，同时向社区提出在不展开程序的前提下的编译挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子软件栈扩展以支持未来容错量子硬件与算法，量子编译日益关键。现有栈需支持含中间电路测量前馈、重复直至成功等模式的动态算法，规模可达数百逻辑比特、数十亿次量子操作。当前以直线式电路为主的表示方式难以承载这些经典结构与动态性，因此需要重新思考量子程序的表示方式。

Method: 本文并非提出具体编译算法，而是归纳容错量子应用中的关键模式与算法，指出其具有应被保留的结构化表示；提出在不将结构化程序展开为直线式量子电路的前提下进行编译的挑战；并考察当前量子软件对结构化程序支持的现状。

Result: 识别出容错量子应用中若干可结构化表示的重要模式与算法，论证保留该结构的重要性；梳理了现有量子软件对结构化程序的支持状况，并指出利用经典结构信息可提升量子编译工具的效率。

Conclusion: 量子编译需以包含经典结构与动态性的结构化表示作为默认形式，社区应致力于在不展开的前提下编译此类程序，借助经典结构带来的额外信息提升编译效率。

Abstract: As quantum software stacks scale up to support future fault-tolerant quantum hardware and algorithms, quantum compilation is becoming an increasingly important component of the stack. How do we ensure that our software stacks support dynamic algorithms, including patterns such as mid-circuit measurement feedforward and repeat-until-success, with hundreds of logical qubits and billions of quantum operations? To do so, we must re-think how we represent quantum programs beyond straight-line circuits, to representations that include classical structure and dynamism, and make this the default representation to consider when performing quantum compilation. In this work, we present important patterns and algorithms from fault-tolerant quantum applications which admit a structured representation that we argue is crucial to preserve, and set a challenge to the community to compile such representations without unrolling them into straight-line quantum circuits. We also explore the status quo of structured program support in quantum software, and ask ourselves the rhetorical question: how much more efficient can we make quantum compilation tooling when we take into account the additional information from classical structure?

</details>


### [100] [Strictly Localized Mixed States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17013)
*Sigurd Sørlie Rustad,Jan Gulla,Johannes Skaar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子场论中的严格局域混合态：在某个时空区域外与真空不可区分的态。此类态由Licht映射从真空生成。虽然局域投影测量单个结果对应的态并非严格局域，但其非选择性态是严格局域的，且可分解为严格局域纯态。然而存在不能写成同一区域严格局域纯态混合的严格局域混合态，这类态可由非锐局域测量产生。作者还将Knight定理推广到混合态，证明严格局域玻色混合态包含粒子数任意高的项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子场论中严格局域态的层次结构，特别是混合态能否分解为局域纯态，以及局域性对粒子数含量的限制。这有助于厘清局域测量、局域性与纠缠/粒子激发之间的关系。

Method: 利用Licht映射从真空生成严格局域态；分析局域投影测量的非选择性态及其纯态分解；构造由非锐局域测量产生的不可分解严格局域混合态；将Knight定理推广至混合态情形。

Result: 局域投影测量的非选择性态严格局域并可分解为严格局域纯态；存在不能表示为同一区域严格局域纯态混合的严格局域混合态，可由非锐局域测量产生；严格局域玻色混合态包含粒子数任意高的项（Knight定理的混合态推广）。

Conclusion: 严格局域混合态的结构比纯态情形更丰富：并非所有严格局域混合态都是同一区域严格局域纯态的混合，非锐局域测量可产生此类态；且局域性并不限制玻色混合态的粒子数上界。Knight定理在混合态框架下依然成立。

Abstract: We study strictly localized mixed states in quantum field theory: states that are indistinguishable from vacuum outside some spacetime region. Such states are generated from the vacuum by so-called Licht maps. While the states associated with individual outcomes of local, projective measurements on the vacuum are not strictly localized, the corresponding non-selective states are strictly localized and even admit decompositions into strictly localized pure states. Remarkably, however, there are strictly localized mixed states that cannot be written as mixtures of pure states strictly localized to the same region. Such states can arise from unsharp local measurements. Finally, we extend Knight's theorem to mixed states, showing that strictly localized bosonic mixed states contain terms with arbitrarily high particle numbers.

</details>


### [101] [Geometric View of Iterative Fixed-Node Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16308)
*Pranav Kairon,Bryan K. Clark*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究格点固定节点方法中反复用固定节点哈密顿量基态替换试探态的迭代映射，证明其不动点为哈密顿量本征态、基态不动点稳定、激发态不动点不稳定，并从几何与稳定性角度阐明费米子符号问题的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体基态的符号结构是缓解费米子符号问题的核心。连续电子结构哈密顿量中，正确的符号结构足以求得精确基态性质；但格点固定节点方法还额外依赖试探波函数振幅，现有做法在保持符号结构前提下非最优地改进振幅，其理论基础尚不清晰。

Method: 定义一个迭代映射：反复将固定节点试探态替换为其对应格点固定节点哈密顿量的基态（不考虑实际算法的可实现性）。分析该映射的不动点性质、符号腔内部不动点的唯一性、基态与激发态的稳定性，以及符号腔边界的吸引/排斥方向性。

Result: 该映射的不动点是哈密顿量及约化哈密顿量的本征态；每个符号腔内部至多有一个不动点。基态不动点稳定，且基态符号腔边界具有排斥性；其他符号腔中能量下降受阻，迭代使部分振幅趋于零，产生"支撑坍缩"至腔边界并对应约化哈密顿量。激发态不动点可证不稳定，流向更低能量的边界不动点。符号腔边界具有方向性稳定：某一方向吸引的边界点在符号翻转下变为排斥。

Conclusion: 自洽迭代下，超出正确基态符号结构之外的振幅依赖会消失，从而澄清了固定节点方法的核心基础，并为费米子符号问题的新算法策略提供动机。

Abstract: The sign-structure of a quantum many-body ground state is a central quantity in fixed-node approaches to mitigating the Fermion sign problem. For continuum electronic-structure Hamiltonians, the correct sign-structure is sufficient to compute exact ground state properties; on the other hand, lattice fixed-node approaches retain an additional dependence on trial wave-function amplitudes which are improved upon non-optimally while preserving their sign structure. Here we consider an iterative map obtained by repeatedly replacing the fixed-node trial state with the ground state of its associated lattice fixed-node Hamiltonian (independent of whether and how a practical algorithm could implement this iteration). We show the fixed points of this map are eigenstates of the Hamiltonian and reduced Hamiltonian resulting in at most one fixed point in the interior of each sign chamber. We prove that the ground state fixed point is stable and that the boundary of the ground state sign chamber is repulsive. This shows the amplitude dependence beyond having the correct ground state sign-structure disappears under self-consistent iteration. In other sign chambers, energy descent can be obstructed by the imposed sign structure; this tension leads to the iteration driving select amplitudes to zero producing `support collapse' onto chamber boundaries and corresponding to reduced Hamiltonians. Excited-state fixed points are therefore provably unstable and flow toward lower-energy boundary fixed points. We show sign chamber boundaries have a directional stability; boundary points attractive in one direction are repulsive under a sign flip. By revealing the geometry and stability structure of iterative fixed-node dynamics, our results clarify the foundations of a central approach to the Fermion sign problem and motivate potential new algorithmic strategies.

</details>


### [102] [Utility-Based Path Selection and Configuration in Quantum Networks via Layered Shortest Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16198)
*Leonardo Bacciottini,Subhransu Maji,Don Towsley,Gayane Vardoyan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究量子网络中路径选择与链路配置的联合优化问题，将其建模为分层图上的最短路径计算，单次运行即可获得速率-保真度帕累托前沿，并证明对BB84密钥率等效用函数为全多项式时间近似方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络中路径选择需同时兼顾纠缠分发速率与质量（如保真度），二者组合方式非可加，且受链路级配置（蒸馏轮数、发射器亮度调节）影响，路由与配置相互耦合，传统路由度量不适用，因此需要新的联合优化方法。

Method: 将联合路径选择与配置问题转化为分层图上的最短路径计算，各层跟踪离散化的端到端保真度；单次运行输出完整的速率-保真度帕累托前沿，并据此选择使任意非递减效用函数最大化的路径；同时与基于标量化的路由方法进行对比分析。

Result: 对某些效用函数（含BB84密钥率），该方法是全多项式时间近似方案，能在指定容差内返回近最优路径；标量化路由在效用函数具有凸保真度轮廓时最优，否则可能任意次优（如阶梯型、S形效用），而分层方法在所有情形下均保持可靠。

Conclusion: 分层图方法可统一处理速率与保真度的非可加组合及链路配置耦合问题，适用范围广于标量化路由，为量子网络路由提供具有理论保证的通用框架。

Abstract: A path in a quantum network is a chain of repeaters that distributes entanglement between two users. Selecting a path requires balancing the rate and quality (e.g., fidelity) of the delivered entanglement, but these quantities, unlike standard routing metrics, compose non-additively. The problem is compounded by link-level configuration choices (e.g., distillation rounds or emitter brightness tuning), each trading rate against fidelity, so that a path's performance depends jointly on its route and its per-link settings. We cast this joint path selection and configuration problem as a shortest path computation on a layered graph whose layers track discretized end-to-end fidelity. A single run returns the full rate fidelity Pareto frontier, from which the path maximizing any nondecreasing utility function of rate and fidelity can be selected. We prove that for certain utility functions (including the secret key rate of BB84), the method is a fully polynomial time approximation scheme, returning a near-optimal path within a specified tolerance. We further characterize exactly when cheaper scalarization-based routing suffices: it is optimal for utility functions with convex fidelity profiles, but can be arbitrarily suboptimal otherwise (e.g., for step-like, sigmoidal utilities), whereas the layered method remains reliable in all cases.

</details>


### [103] [Generalised quantum Stein's lemma more robust than ever](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17309)
*Filippo Girardi,Kuan-Yi Lee,Masahito Hayashi,Ludovico Lami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将广义量子Stein引理推广至任意在归一化一阶量子Wasserstein距离下渐近逼近i.i.d.态的源，并由此求解了零假设任意变化情形下的Stein指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义量子Stein引理是量子假设检验与量子资源操控之间的关键桥梁。既有结果仅适用于i.i.d.序列或按Mazzola-Sutter-Renner严格意义下"接近"i.i.d.的源，但许多应用需要更一般的源。

Method: 采用归一化一阶量子Wasserstein距离作为源与i.i.d.态之间"渐近接近"的度量，建立对应版本的广义量子Stein引理。

Result: 证明了一个概念上更简单且更一般的广义量子Stein引理，适用于所有在归一化量子Wasserstein距离（一阶）下渐近接近某i.i.d.态的源。

Conclusion: 作为直接推论，求得零假设任意变化情形下的Stein指数，该指数可由零假设基集凸包中任意态对应的i.i.d. Stein指数表达。

Abstract: The generalised quantum Stein's lemma is a key result in quantum hypothesis testing, and connects this fundamental primitive of quantum information processing with quantum resource manipulation, a task that is central for technological applications. Prior works have proved this statement in the idealised setting of independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) sequences of quantum states, and recent extensions consider also sources that are 'close' to i.i.d., according to the strict notion put forth by Mazzola, Sutter, and Renner. For several applications, however, one would need to consider yet more general sources. We establish a version of the generalised quantum Stein's lemma that is conceptually much simpler and general, as it applies to any source that is asymptotically close to an i.i.d. state with respect to the normalised quantum Wasserstein distance of order 1. As an immediate consequence, we solve the Stein exponent of a scenario where the null hypothesis is arbitrarily varying, expressing it in terms of i.i.d. Stein exponents corresponding to arbitrary states in the convex hull of the null hypothesis base set.

</details>


### [104] [Coupling Structure Determines Communication Regimes in Discrete Quantum Convolutional Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16200)
*Chunhe Xiong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对由可逆 2×2 耦合矩阵与环境态生成的离散量子卷积信道进行分类，并根据耦合矩阵哪些元素为零给出各分支的 Holevo 信息与经典、量子、私密容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子卷积理论新近发展，但离散变量量子卷积信道的结构分类及其通信容量尚缺乏系统刻画；特别是一个零元素的位置如何决定信道通信能力这一问题未明。

Method: 按耦合矩阵零元素分类；在保角色基重标下归一化；对四个单零元素分支求优化单次 Holevo 信息及经典、量子、私密容量；对全非零情形利用 Weyl 协变性约化经典容量问题；对单 qutrit 唯一全非零耦合类推导退极化单参数族的精确 Holevo 公式并确定纠缠破坏阈值。

Result: 四条单零分支容量被完全确定：对角元为零时信道为纠缠破坏的 measure-and-prepare orbit 信道，经典容量等于环境态的相干相对熵，量子与私密容量为零；非对角元为零时为可退化广义退相位信道，一个正交基无误差传输，量子与私密容量由共轭基退相位后环境态的熵亏损决定。全非零情形信道不可约 Weyl 协变，经典容量问题约化为正则化最小输出熵问题。

Conclusion: 单个零元素的位置选择了两类本质不同的通信机制；全非零情形由 Weyl 协变性刻画；单 qutrit 全非零耦合类获得精确 Holevo 公式及纠缠破坏阈值。

Abstract: We study the recently developed theory of quantum convolution for discrete-variable quantum systems. We classify the discrete quantum convolutional channels generated by an invertible $2\times2$ coupling matrix and a fixed environmental state according to which entries of the coupling ma- trix vanish. Under role-preserving basis relabelings, matrices with all four entries nonzero form $d-2$ canonical cross-ratio classes, whereas matrices with exactly one vanishing entry split into four inequivalent branches. We determine the optimized one-shot Holevo information and the classical, quantum, and private capacities for all four one-entry-vanishing branches. If a diago- nal entry vanishes, the channel is an entanglement-breaking measure-and-prepare orbit channel: its classical capacity equals a relative entropy coherence of the environmental state, whereas its quantum and private capacities vanish. If an off-diagonal entry vanishes, the channel is a degradable generalized-dephasing channel: one complete orthonormal basis is transmitted without error, whereas the quantum and private capacities are determined by the entropy deficit of the environ-ental state
  after dephasing in the conjugate basis. Thus, the position of a single vanishing entry selects two qualitatively different communication regimes. When all four entries are nonzero, the channels are irreducibly Weyl covariant, reducing the classical-capacity problem to a regularized minimum-output-entropy problem. For the unique fully nonzero coupling class in the single-qutrit case, we further derive an exact Holevo formula for a depolarized one-parameter family and identify its entanglement-breaking threshold.

</details>


### [105] [Optimal Linear-Rate Conversion of Unknown Mixed Qubit States via SWAP Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17311)
*Sujay Kazi,Iman Marvian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在允许非零误差（要求误差随副本数趋于无穷而消失）条件下，不同纯度量子比特态之间的最大线性转换速率，并揭示该速率由复合右对数导数（RLD）Fisher信息矩阵的特征值决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消耗多份未知量子比特态副本可在保持Bloch矢量方向不变的同时改变其纯度，但不同纯度态之间的最优相互转换速率及其物理刻画此前并不清楚。

Method: 作者分析在允许渐近消失误差（以迹距离量化）下的态转换任务，计算最大线性转换率；并通过SWAP测试、配合初始处于最大混合态的辅助量子比特来实现集中与稀释操作，不借助任何额外双量子比特门。

Result: 最优转换率由与量子比特态SU(2)旋转相关的复合RLD Fisher信息矩阵的两个特征值决定：集中（输出纯度更高）时最优率为输入与输出RLD矩阵最大特征值之比；稀释（输出纯度更低）时则为最小特征值之比。集中与稀释均可仅用SWAP测试作为非平凡双量子比特测量原语实现。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了复合RLD Fisher信息矩阵的一种全新操作解释，并表明其反对称的纯虚部编码了超出密度算符间统计距离的几何信息，在决定最优态转换速率中起关键作用。

Abstract: By consuming multiple copies of an unknown qubit state, one can modify its purity while preserving the direction of its Bloch vector. We determine the maximum linear rate at which qubit states of different purities can be interconverted, allowing a nonzero error, quantified, for instance, by the trace distance, provided that it vanishes in the limit of infinitely many copies. Interestingly, the optimal conversion rate is determined by the two eigenvalues of the complex right-logarithmic-derivative (RLD) Fisher information matrix associated with $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ rotations of the qubit state. When the output qubits have higher purity, corresponding to concentration, the optimal rate is given by the ratio of the maximum eigenvalues of the input and output RLD matrices. In contrast, when the output qubits have lower purity, corresponding to dilution, the optimal rate is given by the ratio of their minimum eigenvalues. Remarkably, both concentration and dilution can be implemented using SWAP tests as the only nontrivial two-qubit measurement primitive, together with ancillary qubits initially prepared in maximally mixed states, without requiring any additional two-qubit gates. Our work thus provides a novel operational interpretation of the full complex RLD Fisher information matrix. Crucially, its antisymmetric, purely imaginary part encodes geometric information beyond the statistical distance between density operators and plays an essential role in determining the optimal state-conversion rates.

</details>


### [106] [Floquet Dressing and Bath Spectral Effects on the Geometric Phase of a Driven Dissipative Qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16609)
*Chirag Arora*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过PT-TEMPO方法研究周期驱动对称自旋-玻色模型的耗散几何相位动力学，揭示驱动抑制或增强环境诱导的几何相位形变，并用Floquet谱调控机制加以解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Wang等关于自旋-玻色模型中耗散几何相位（GP）工作的基础上，进一步探究周期驱动与耗散共同作用下开放量子系统几何相位的演化规律，并建立Floquet工程、量子耗散与几何相位之间的联系。

Method: 采用数值精确的过程张量-时间演化矩阵乘积算符（PT-TEMPO）方法，研究周期驱动对称自旋-玻色模型的GP动力学；对对称二能级系统施加与系统-环境耦合坐标同向的纵向单色驱动，环境为Ohmic玻色浴；引入非加性GP贡献以分离协同驱动-浴效应，并通过Floquet谱分析（准能边带、Fourier分辨的系统-浴矩阵元）解释结果。

Result: 周期驱动对浴诱导的GP形变既可抑制也可增强，取决于驱动与浴时间尺度的相互竞争；Floquet分析表明，主导耗散路径由Floquet矩阵元权重与浴谱重叠共同决定，而非由其中任一单独决定。

Conclusion: 研究将Floquet工程、量子耗散与几何相位动力学相连接，确立周期驱动作为调控开放量子系统几何相位的一种手段。

Abstract: Building on recent work by Wang et al. on the dissipative geometric phase (GP) in the spin-boson model (SBM), the GP dynamics of a periodically driven symmetric SBM are investigated using the numerically exact process-tensor time-evolving matrix product operator (PT-TEMPO) method. A symmetric two-level system subjected to a longitudinal monochromatic drive along the same system coordinate that couples to an Ohmic bosonic environment exhibits rich GP dynamics arising from the interplay between driving and dissipation. To isolate cooperative drive-bath effects, a non-additive GP contribution is introduced that separates the driven dissipative response from its driven-unitary and undriven-dissipative components. Periodic driving is found to either suppress or enhance bath-induced GP deformation depending on the interplay between drive and bath timescales. These trends are interpreted through Floquet spectral steering, whereby the drive redistributes the coherent dynamics among quasienergy sidebands whose transition frequencies and Fourier-resolved system-bath matrix elements determine their coupling to the environmental spectrum. Floquet analysis shows that the dominant dissipative pathways are governed jointly by Floquet matrix-element weights and bath spectral overlap, rather than by either alone. These results connect Floquet engineering, quantum dissipation, and GP dynamics and establish periodic driving as a means of controlling the GP of open quantum systems (OQSs).

</details>


### [107] [Link-middle-cut lower bounds for Clifford circuit synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16208)
*Søren Fuglede Jørgensen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将线性可逆电路的LMC框架推广到由二元辛tableau表示的Clifford算子，引入块支撑连通图与行列式不变量，得到无辅助比特CNOT复杂度的有效下界，证明其对量子比特置换是紧的（k个循环的n比特置换恰需3(n-k)个CNOT），并用作A*合成的可采纳启发式，在基准实例上超越先前SAT方法的最优性证明能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小化合成Clifford算子所需的CNOT门数是量子电路优化的核心问题。已有LMC框架仅适用于线性可逆电路，缺乏针对Clifford算子的有效CNOT复杂度下界，尤其是无辅助比特情形。

Method: 将线性可逆电路的link--middle--cut（LMC）框架推广至二元辛tableau表示的Clifford算子；引入块支撑连通图（block-support connectivity graphs）与行列式不变量，构造可高效计算的CNOT复杂度下界；并推导到输出比特置换等价（自由重标记）下的下界；将该下界作为A*搜索的可采纳启发式用于Clifford合成。

Result: 证明了所得下界对量子比特置换是紧的：n比特置换含k个循环时恰需3(n-k)个CNOT门，即使允许任意单比特Clifford门；得到输出比特置换等价下的可计算下界；在基准实例上，A*搜索能证明超出先前SAT方法所能达到的最优性，并可与其他A*启发式结合以改进启发式搜索得到的CNOT计数。

Conclusion: 该工作为Clifford算子的无辅助比特CNOT复杂度提供了可高效计算的紧下界，特别精确刻画了置换情形，并将下界成功用于A*合成，提升了最优性证明与启发式搜索效果。

Abstract: Minimizing the number of CNOT gates required to synthesize a Clifford operator is a central problem in quantum circuit optimization. We extend the link--middle--cut (LMC) framework for linear reversible circuits to Clifford operators represented by binary symplectic tableaux. By introducing block-support connectivity graphs and determinantal invariants, we obtain efficiently computable lower bounds on ancilla-free CNOT complexity. We prove that these bounds are tight for qubit permutations: a permutation of $n$ qubits with $k$ cycles requires exactly $3(n-k)$ CNOT gates, even when arbitrary one-qubit Clifford gates are available. We also derive efficiently computable bounds for synthesis up to a permutation of the output qubits, corresponding to free qubit relabeling. Finally, we further demonstrate the utility of the bound by using it as an admissible heuristic for $A^*$-based Clifford synthesis and show that, on benchmark instances, the resulting search can prove optimality beyond what was possible with earlier SAT-based methods, and that it may be used in combination with earlier $A^*$ heuristics to improve the CNOT counts obtained through heuristic search.

</details>


### [108] [Initial-Slip Dynamics Enables the Quantum Mpemba Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16781)
*M. Crotti,F. Cavaliere,D. Ferraro,G. Benenti,M. Sassetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了量子谐振子与玻色环境线性耦合时的量子姆潘巴效应，给出超出弱耦合与马尔可夫近似的精确解析判据，并指出系统-浴相互作用突然开启产生的初滑动力学是效应出现的关键，瞬态关联是其核心机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子姆潘巴效应指初态远离平衡的系统反而比初态更接近平衡的系统弛豫更快，其产生机制尚未被充分理解，且已有研究多依赖弱耦合与马尔可夫近似，适用范围受限。

Method: 以不同温度下的因子化热态为初态，考虑量子谐振子与玻色环境线性耦合模型，推导量子姆潘巴效应发生的精确解析判据，该判据同时适用于超越弱耦合与马尔可夫近似的区域。

Result: 获得超出常规近似的精确解析判据，并证明该效应由系统-浴相互作用突然接通所引发的初始滑移动力学所驱动；若忽略这一贡献，反常弛豫现象被完全抑制。

Conclusion: 瞬态系统-浴关联是量子姆潘巴效应的关键成因，初始滑移在非平衡弛豫中起决定性作用。

Abstract: We investigate the quantum Mpemba effect, whereby a system initially farther from equilibrium relaxes faster than one initially closer to equilibrium, emerging in a quantum harmonic oscillator linearly coupled to a bosonic environment. Starting from factorized thermal states at different temperatures, we derive exact analytical criteria for the occurrence of the quantum Mpemba effect valid also beyond the typically considered weak-coupling and Markovian approximations. Most importantly, we show that the effect is enabled by the initial-slip dynamics generated by the sudden switch-on of the system-bath interaction. Neglecting this contribution completely suppresses the anomalous relaxation, identifying transient system-bath correlations as the key ingredient underlying the quantum Mpemba effect.

</details>


### [109] [$d$-pod realization of nonadiabatic holonomic quantum computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16216)
*Oskar Axelsson,Claes Fälth,Elias Henriksson Lindberg,Erik Sjöqvist*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将非绝热Holonomic量子计算推广到d-pod构型，实现qudit通用holonomic单比特/双比特门，并以qutrit为例给出紧凑实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非绝热HQC框架已在编码qubit的三能级Λ系统中充分发展，但向高维qudit的系统性推广仍未被充分探索，需要一种可扩展的通用门构造方案。

Method: 将非绝热HQC推广到d-pod构型：单一激发态耦合d个基态，基态构成计算子空间；利用仅需光学或微波脉冲的Grassmannian几何相位路径（环路）构造单/双qudit门；详细分析qutrit（d=3）情形。

Result: 方案可在囚禁原子或离子上实现通用holonomic单-和双-qudit门，仅需光学/微波脉冲；qutrit情形下每个门最多需两个Grassmannian环路，由最多三个脉冲生成，实现紧凑的单-和双-qutrit门。

Conclusion: 该推广提供了以最少脉冲协调实现离散通用门集的高效途径，为高维holonomic量子计算提供了系统框架。

Abstract: Holonomic quantum computation (HQC) realizes quantum gates through non-Abelian geometric phases, providing an experimentally accessible approach to quantum control. While the nonadiabatic HQC framework has been extensively developed for three-level $Λ$ systems encoding qubits, its systematic extension to higher-dimensional qudits remains largely unexplored. In this work, we generalize nonadiabatic HQC to a $d$-pod configuration, where a single excited state is coupled to $d$ ground states, the latter forming the computational subspace. This scheme enables universal holonomic single- and two-qudit gates using only optical or microwave pulses on trapped atoms or ions, offering an efficient route to implement a discrete universal gate set with minimal pulse coordination. As an explicit example, we analyze in detail the qutrit ($d=3$) case, demonstrating compact realizations of single- and two-qutrit holonomic gates, each gate requiring at most two loops in the Grassmannian generated by at most three pulses.

</details>


### [110] [One Gate at a Time: Complexity Growth in Random Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17457)
*Zhi Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明随机酉量子电路的常数误差电路复杂度随时间几乎线性增长，其下界为Ω(T/log T)，适用于所有2≤T≤4^n且无其他n依赖关系，较此前基于谱隙与酉设计的下界改进了poly(n)因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机酉量子电路预期在指数长时间内不可压缩，但此前从谱隙和酉设计导出的复杂度下界存在poly(n)的差距，需要更紧的随时间增长的下界。

Method: 借鉴随机微积分、几何泛函分析与随机线性代数，利用电路对单个门变化的响应进行分析，且不依赖于向高阶酉设计的收敛控制。

Result: 常数误差电路复杂度下界达到Ω(T/log T)，对全部2≤T≤4^n成立，且除T外无其他n依赖，相比先前结果提升poly(n)因子。

Conclusion: 随机酉电路的电路复杂度随时间的下界可几乎线性增长，且通过不依赖酉设计收敛的方法可获得更强结果，推进了对随机量子电路不可压缩性的理解。

Abstract: A random unitary quantum circuit is expected to be incompressible for exponentially long times. We show that the constant-error circuit complexity of a random unitary circuit grows almost linearly with time as $Ω(T/\log T)$. The bound holds for all $2\leq T\leq 4^n$ where $n$ is the system size, and involves no other $n$-dependence. This improves previous lower bounds derived from spectral gaps and unitary designs by a factor of $\mathrm{poly}(n)$. Drawing on insights from stochastic calculus, geometric functional analysis, and randomized linear algebra, our approach exploits the circuit's response to variations of individual gates and requires no control over convergence to high-order unitary designs.

</details>


### [111] [Preparation-protocol-dependent quantum Mpemba dynamics in a magnetically tunable graphene nanotorus qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17176)
*J. Furtado*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究石墨烯纳米环量子比特中的量子姆潘巴效应，发现其强度强烈依赖初始态制备方式：裸吉布斯制备产生广泛强反常弛豫，而驱动稳态制备几乎完全抑制该效应，机制源于制备依赖的模权重而非衰减率层级改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子姆潘巴效应（反常弛豫）在开放量子系统中的地位日益重要，但其是否以及如何受初始态制备方式调控尚不清楚。本文旨在探究在共享同一末态哈密顿量、热浴与马尔可夫刘维尔量的前提下，不同初始态制备协议对姆潘巴效应强度的影响。

Method: 以石墨烯纳米环量子比特为平台，对比两种制备协议：裸吉布斯制备（相干驱动仅在末态淬火时开启）与驱动稳态制备（初始态为驱动动力学的稳态）。采用到末态稳态的距离与积分姆潘巴参数进行量化，并通过刘维尔量本征模分析揭示机制，比较慢活跃扇区中热态权重比 R_s 的差异。

Result: 裸吉布斯制备在大参数区域内呈现强反常弛豫，姆潘巴参数 M_B 接近 1；而驱动稳态制备几乎完全抑制该效应。模分析显示：裸吉布斯协议通常给出 R_s<1（热态在慢活跃扇区权重小于冷态），驱动稳态协议则以 R_s>1 为主。在对应参数点上两种协议的末态刘维尔谱完全相同。

Conclusion: 石墨烯纳米环量子比特中的量子姆潘巴效应强度由初始态制备方式控制。由于末态刘维尔谱对两种协议一致，效应差异源自制备依赖的模权重而非衰减率层级变化。这表明态制备可作为弯曲石墨烯量子比特中反常量子弛豫的可调控参数。

Abstract: We investigate the quantum Mpemba effect in a graphene nanotorus qubit and show that its strength depends strongly on how the initial state is prepared. We compare two protocols that share the same final Hamiltonian, thermal bath, and Markovian Liouvillian: bare Gibbs preparation, in which the coherent drive is switched on only at the final quench, and driven steady-state preparation, in which the initial states are stationary states of the driven dynamics. Using the distance to the final stationary state and an integrated Mpemba parameter, we find broad regions of strong anomalous relaxation for bare Gibbs preparation, with $M_B$ approaching unity, whereas the driven steady-state protocol almost completely suppresses the effect. A Liouvillian-mode analysis reveals the mechanism: the bare Gibbs protocol typically yields a smaller hot-state weight in the slow active sector than the cold state, $R_s<1$, while the driven steady-state protocol predominantly gives $R_s>1$. Since the final Liouvillian spectrum is identical for both protocols at corresponding parameter points, the contrast originates from preparation-dependent modal weights rather than from changes in the decay-rate hierarchy. These results identify state preparation as a control parameter for anomalous quantum relaxation in a curved graphene qubit.

</details>


### [112] [Error-corrected function estimation advantage in multiparameter Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16226)
*Erfan Abbasgholinejad,Lorcán O. Conlon,Sean R. Muleady,Jacob Bringewatt,Ali Fahimniya,Yu-Xin Wang,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了马尔可夫噪声下估计多个哈密顿参数函数的最终精度极限，通过将多参数函数估计约化为对有效单参数嵌入的优化，得到了量子Fisher信息的紧致界，并给出了Heisenberg极限标度的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数哈密顿估计通常受噪声限制，而多数应用真正关心的是参数的某个函数而非全部参数。因此需要确定在马尔可夫噪声下估计参数函数的最终精度极限，并厘清何时能实现Heisenberg标度、何时只能达到标准量子极限。

Method: 将多参数函数估计约化为对有效单参数嵌入的优化，推导量子Fisher信息的紧致界；提出哈密顿不位于Lindblad张成空间（Hamiltonian-not-in-Lindblad-span）的必要充分条件；在条件成立时构造同时消除噪声与冗余哈密顿参数的编码，在条件不成立时推导最优标准量子极限系数并用近似量子纠错渐近达到该界。

Result: 得到了量子Fisher信息的紧致界；给出了Heisenberg极限标度的必要充分条件；条件成立时可构造同时去噪并保留目标信号的编码；条件不成立时得到最优标准量子极限系数且证明其渐近可达；并证明直接函数估计显著优于逐参数估计后再求函数，该优势可随演化时间和参数数目增长。

Conclusion: 直接估计参数的函数在噪声环境下具有本质优势，其相对逐参数估计的增益可随时间和参数数目标度增长；研究明确了实现Heisenberg极限所需的结构条件及失效时的最优标度行为。

Abstract: We establish the ultimate precision limits for estimating a function of multiple Hamiltonian parameters in the presence of Markovian noise. By reducing multiparameter function estimation to an optimization over effective single-parameter embeddings, we derive tight bounds on the quantum Fisher information. We identify a necessary and sufficient functional Hamiltonian-not-in-Lindblad-span condition for Heisenberg-limited scaling in time. When this condition holds, we construct a code that simultaneously removes the noise and nuisance Hamiltonian parameters while preserving the target signal. When it fails, we derive the optimal standard quantum-limit coefficient and show that it is asymptotically attainable using approximate quantum error correction. Finally, we demonstrate that direct function estimation can substantially outperform estimating all parameters individually and subsequently evaluating the function. This advantage can scale with both the evolution time and the number of parameters.

</details>


### [113] [Regularized barycentric Rényi divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17517)
*Milán Mosonyi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意由可加且单调量子相对熵构造的重心Rényi散度的正则化版本在严格正输入上等于最小重心Rényi散度，从而表明唯一可加的重心Rényi散度就是最小重心Rényi散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重心Rényi散度（barycentric Rényi divergences）是2024年提出的多变量量子Rényi散度构造，依赖一组量子相对熵$D^{q_x}$。其单调性与子可加性可继承自各分量相对熵。然而可加性此前仅在所有$D^{q_x}$取Umegaki相对熵（最小重心Rényi散度）时被建立，且该情形也是唯一有显式表达的情形。本文旨在解决一般的可加性问题。

Method: 利用变分表达式定义的重心Rényi散度，针对任意选取的可加且单调量子相对熵$D^{q_x}$，分析其正则化版本与最小重心Rényi散度的关系，在严格正输入条件下进行证明。

Result: 证明了对于任意可加且单调的量子相对熵选择，正则化重心Rényi散度在严格正输入上与最小重心Rényi散度重合。

Conclusion: 由此推论，唯一满足可加性的重心Rényi散度是最小重心Rényi散度，完成了该可加性问题的完整刻画。

Abstract: Barycentric Rényi divergences were introduced in [Mosonyi, Bunth, Vrana, Linear Algebra and its Applications, 2024] as an alternative to standard Kubo-Ando constructions to define multivariate quantum Rényi divergences. They are defined via a variational expression and depend on a finite collection of quantum relative entropies $D^{q_x}$. When all the relative entropies are monotone under CPTP maps then so are the corresponding barycentric Rényi divergences, and when all the relative entropies are additive then the corresponding barycentric Rényi divergences are subadditive under tensor product. Additivity has only been established before for the case where all $D^{q_x}$ are chosen to be the Umegaki relative entropy, which is also the only case where the barycentric Rényi divergence (called the minimal one) admits an explicit expression.
  Here we settle the problem of additivity by showing that for any choice of additive and monotone quantum relative entropies, the regularized barycentric Rényi divergence coincides with the minimal barycentric Rényi divergence on strictly positive inputs. This in turn implies that the only additive barycentric Rényi divergence is the minimal one.

</details>


### [114] [Towards Surrogate Based Dequantization of Quantum Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16266)
*Pablo Rodriguez-Grasa,Sofiene Jerbi,Mikel Sanz,Ryan Sweke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在带均匀生成模型的强化学习简化设定下，研究经典核化拟合Q迭代对量子Q学习的去量子化能力，给出了有限样本保证及实现有意义去量子化的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化量子电路作为函数逼近器在量子Q学习等变分量子算法中展现出良好经验效果，但能否对实际相关问题提供可证明的量子优势仍不明确。作者以"去量子化"为视角，试图构造能匹配量子变分方法性能的高效经典算法，并将已有的监督学习核方法去量子化结果扩展到强化学习领域。

Method: 在均匀生成模型下（即均匀随机的状态-动作采样，模拟充分探索后从大经验回放缓冲区采样的情形）研究强化学习。提出经典核化拟合Q迭代（Kernelized Fitted Q-Iteration），其核函数被设计为匹配特定参数化量子电路的归纳偏置；针对该算法给出有限样本保证，并推导出关于量子电路数据编码策略、对应经典核及问题结构的充分条件。

Result: 在满足所给充分条件时，核化拟合Q迭代对量子Q学习提供了严格的去量子化保证；当这些条件无法验证时，结果也支持将核化拟合Q迭代用作一种去量子化启发式方法。

Conclusion: 该工作将基于代理（surrogate）的去量子化方案从监督学习推进到强化学习，在简化设定下建立了经典核方法与量子变分方法的性能对应关系，为判断量子强化学习能否带来实际可证明优势提供了理论工具。

Abstract: In recent years, the utility of parameterized quantum circuits as function approximators has been widely studied. In the context of reinforcement learning, this approach has led to variational quantum algorithms such as quantum Q-learning. While these methods show promising empirical results, and can provide provable advantages for artificial problems, it remains unclear whether they can provide a provable quantum advantage over classical approaches for problems of practical relevance. A natural way to investigate this question is through the lens of dequantization: The construction of efficient classical algorithms capable of matching the performance of quantum variational methods. Building on recent kernel-based dequantization results for supervised learning, we take steps towards extending this surrogate-based dequantization program to reinforcement learning. Specifically, we study the simplified setting of reinforcement learning with a uniform generative model in which uniformly random state-action samples are available, which models the regime of sampling from a large experience replay buffer after sufficient exploration. Within this setting, we provide finite sample guarantees for classical kernelized Fitted Q-Iteration, with classical kernels designed to match the inductive bias of particular parameterized quantum circuits. Using these results, we then provide a set of sufficient conditions, on the data-encoding strategy of a parameterized quantum circuit, the corresponding classical kernel, and the problem structure, under which kernelized Fitted Q-Iteration provides a meaningful dequantization of quantum Q-learning, in this simplified setting. Apart from providing rigorous dequantization guarantees when these conditions are met, these results also motivate the use of kernelized fitted Q-iteration as a dequantization heuristic when these sufficient conditions cannot be verified.

</details>


### [115] [Exceptional Topology Survives Strong Hermitian Fields in Radiative Atomic Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17313)
*Tian-Shu Gou,Yi-Cheng Wang,Ya-Tang Yu,Guin-Dar Lin,Jhih-Shih You,H. H. Jen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在二维亚波长原子阵列中，通过方形到三角形的连续形变，体能带经由例外区域在陈数(C1,C2)=(2,-2)的线隙拓扑区间之间转换；在有限形变区间内，增强磁场仅因奇异辐射偶极耦合将例外点推向光锥，且趋肤局域化对磁场呈非单调响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 例外点是复能带的拓扑缺陷，对弱微扰稳定，但在有界双带系统中通常会被足够强的厄米场移除（即存在"强场极限"）。本文旨在探索是否存在对这一常规认知的例外情形，并揭示晶格几何、光锥奇异性与厄米磁场之间的相互作用机制。

Method: 采用二维亚波长原子阵列模型，在固定磁场下沿方形到三角形的连续晶格形变路径扫描；通过计算体能带陈数与线隙拓扑区域、追踪例外点位置（尤其相对光锥的演化），并分析有限开边界下体模的趋肤局域化行为随磁场的变化。

Result: 体态在经历无能隙例外区域时，于两个线隙拓扑区间之间过渡，其陈数为(C1,C2)=(2,-2)。在一有限晶格形变区间内，增强磁场并不消除例外点，而是因奇异辐射偶极耦合将其推向光锥。同时，边界的趋肤局域化对磁场呈非单调响应：中等磁场驱动双极趋肤局域化，体模聚集于相对的两侧边缘；更强磁场则抑制边界局域化。

Conclusion: 结果确立了晶格几何、光锥奇异性与厄米磁场三者的相互作用，为在超越常规强场极限的框架下调控陈拓扑与例外拓扑提供了新途径。

Abstract: Exceptional points are topological defects of complex band structures that are stable against weak perturbations, yet in bounded two-band systems they are ultimately removed by a sufficiently strong Hermitian field. Here we demonstrate a striking exception in a two-dimensional subwavelength atomic array. Along a continuous square-to-triangular deformation at fixed magnetic field, the bulk passes between line-gapped topological regions with band Chern numbers (C_1,C_2)=(2,-2) through a gapless exceptional region. Within a finite interval of lattice deformation, increasing the magnetic field merely drives the exceptional points toward the light cone because of the singular radiative dipolar couplings. We further show that skin localization toward open boundaries responds non-monotonically to the same field. An intermediate field drives a bipolar skin localization, with bulk modes accumulating at opposite edges, whereas stronger fields suppress boundary localization. Our results establish the interplay of lattice geometry, light-cone singularity, and Hermitian magnetic field as a route to engineering Chern and exceptional topology beyond the conventional strong-field limit.

</details>


### [116] [Adaptive Relational Learning on Multi-instance Quantum Data with Photonic Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17352)
*Marcin Jastrzebski,Shang Yu,Raj B. Patel,Oleksandr Kyriienko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出自适应关系型量子机器学习框架，将多个量子态并行加载，通过SWAP/CYCLE测试的全局测量与浅层可训练变换，直接获取成对及高阶关系特征，并在连续变量光子系统中实现，避免影子估计随n快速增长的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多学习任务的关键信息存在于多个量子态之间的关系而非单个态之中，需要在QML中并行加载多量子态并访问关系特征。而基于连续变量经典影子的非自适应"先测量"方法，其估计成本随态数n迅速增长，难以高效恢复关系特征。

Method: 构建自适应关系学习框架：全局测量采用SWAP或CYCLE测试以评估n态Bargmann不变量，并对每个输入态局域施加浅层可训练变换。在连续变量光子系统中实现该方案，用于隐藏关系检测、几何相位分类以及存在未知共享干扰相互作用时的传感任务，并与基于CV经典影子的非自适应"先测量"方法进行基准比较。

Result: 影子估计成本随n快速上升，而所提模型避免了这一依赖性。在n=2时，仅用500次推理采样即达到完美测试精度A=1.0，相较基于影子的方法平均测试精度提升ΔA=0.15，且每个数据点所用采样数减少100倍。

Conclusion: 自适应光子QML能够访问非自适应"先测量"模型难以低成本恢复的关系特征，为传感与量子数据应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Loading multiple quantum states in parallel into a quantum machine learning (QML) model can unlock learning tasks where key information resides in the \emph{relations} between states rather than in individual states. We introduce an adaptive relational learning framework for such multi-instance quantum data that accesses pairwise and higher-order relations. Our model combines global measurements via SWAP or CYCLE tests for evaluating an $n$-state Bargmann invariant with shallow trainable transformations applied locally to each input state. We demonstrate the approach for continuous-variable (CV) photonic systems, which naturally provide access to quantum data and necessary computing operations. We solve tasks involving hidden relationship detection, geometric phase classification, and sensing in the presence of an unknown shared nuisance interaction. We benchmark the adaptive model against a non-adaptive ``measure-first'' approach based on continuous-variable classical shadows, and show that the cost of shadow estimation grows rapidly with $n$, while our model avoids this dependence. Already for $n=2$, we achieve perfect test accuracy $A=1.0$ with $500$ inference shots, improving average test accuracy over the shadow-based method by $ΔA=0.15$ while using $100$ times fewer shots per data point. Our work opens routes to sensing and quantum-data applications where adaptive photonic QML can access relational features that are costly to recover with non-adaptive, measure-first models.

</details>


### [117] [qdmag: A Python package for simulating nonequilibrium magnetization of molecules using quantum master equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16352)
*Shuanglong Liu,Xiao Chen,Andrew Cupo,James N. Fry,Hai-Ping Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: qdmag 是一个计算磁性分子在时变外磁场中磁化强度的工具，通过求解含自旋-声子耦合耗散的广义 Lindblad 量子主方程实现，支持多类交换作用与高阶 ZFS，并可进行毫秒级长时间演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-声子耦合下的自旋动力学在理论与数值计算之间存在鸿沟，缺乏能够处理时变磁场、耗散效应及高维自旋哈密顿量的通用计算工具。

Method: 基于自旋哈密顿量形式（含磁交换作用与零场分裂 ZFS），将自旋-声子耦合作为耗散项，求解广义 Lindblad 量子主方程；ZFS 与交换作用支持至 12 阶并用扩展 Stevens 算符表示；采用磁场的阶梯近似实现长时间演化；对高维哈密顿量构建低维有效哈密顿量以降低计算量。

Result: 实现了磁化强度长达几毫秒的长时间演化；通过单自旋、双自旋和三自旋三个案例研究展示了 qdmag 的使用方式。

Conclusion: qdmag 为含自旋-声子耦合的自旋动力学提供了连接理论发展与数值计算的计算工具。

Abstract: We present qdmag, a computational utility for calculating the magnetization of magnetic molecules in a time-varying external magnetic field by solving a generalized Lindblad quantum master equation with spin-phonon coupling treated as a dissipation term. The package relies on the spin Hamiltonian formalism, which includes both the magnetic exchange interactions and the zero-field splitting (ZFS) interaction. Different types of magnetic exchange interaction and ZFS terms up to 12th order, which are written in terms of the extended Stevens operators, are supported. qdmag enables long-time evolution of magnetization for up to a few milliseconds, which is achieved through a staircase approximation of the magnetic field as a function of time. For high-dimensional spin Hamiltonians, a lower-dimensional effective Hamiltonian is constructed to make the calculations computationally feasible. Three case studies of one-spin, two-spin, and three-spin systems are presented to demonstrate the usage of qdmag. Our work offers a computational tool to bridge the gap between theoretical development and numerical calculations of spin dynamics in the presence of spin-phonon coupling.

</details>


### [118] [Pump-Free Microwave-Optical Bell Pair Generation for Teleportation-Based Quantum Transduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16364)
*Fangxin Li,Jaesung Heo,Zhaoyou Wang,Benjamin Pingault,Xingyu Gao,Tengyang Ruan,Anjun Chu,David D. Awschalom,Andrew N. Cleland,Andrew P. Higginbotham,Alexander A. High,Liang Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种无需光泵浦的微波-光波段贝尔对源，通过自旋/原子系统与光学及微波腔共振耦合产生抗损耗的预报式贝尔对，可实现基于量子隐形传态的转换，预报速率达千赫兹量级且保真度高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子转换需实现微波与光频光子间的相干转换，以连接超导处理器与光网络。现有方案受光泵浦相关复杂性制约，因此需要一种不依赖光泵浦的转换途径。

Method: 提出无泵浦的微波-光贝尔对源，用于基于隐形传态的转换。将自旋或原子系统与光学腔和微波腔共振耦合，生成对损耗鲁棒的预报式（heralded）贝尔对，并在色心、原子系综与声子介导系统等多种平台上实现该构型。

Result: 在色心、原子系综和声子介导系统中，该构型均可实现千赫兹量级的预报速率，并保持高保真度。

Conclusion: 该无泵浦贝尔对源方案规避了光泵浦带来的复杂性，且对多种物理平台具有普适性，为基于隐形传态的微波-光量子转换提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The coherent conversion between microwave and optical photons, known as quantum transduction, is critical for connecting superconducting processors to optical networks. Existing methods are limited by complications associated with optical pumping. We propose a pump-free microwave-optical Bell-pair source for teleportation-based transduction. Using a spin or atomic system resonantly coupled to optical and microwave cavities, the scheme generates loss-robust heralded Bell pairs. Across color centers, atomic ensembles, and phonon-mediated systems, this assembly achieves kilohertz-range heralding rates with high fidelity.

</details>


### [119] [Exact Work Characteristic Functions for Time-Dependent Bosonic Quadratic Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16435)
*F. Nicacio,R. N. P. Maia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文推导了封闭多体玻色子系统在一般含时二次哈密顿量下功的特征函数精确解析表达式，基于非齐次Metaplectic群的统一表述，得到仅含单个辛矩阵、单个相空间位移和单个累积相位的紧凑结果，并给出热力学量及特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子功统计中，特征函数是核心研究对象，但含时二次哈密顿量下存在显式时间排序难题，难以获得一般解析结果。作者希望建立统一表述，绕开时间排序问题，得到适用范围广（任意自由度、任意含时性、任意初始热平衡态）的精确功特征函数。

Method: 采用非齐次Metaplectic群的统一表述，将所有出现在特征函数中的算符视为该群元素；将幺正演化中的显式时间排序问题转化为相空间中辛路径的确定问题，并由该辛路径重构相应驱动哈密顿量；结合两点投影测量方案推广至任意非平衡初始态，同一构造还给出Loschmidt回波及混合态总相位。

Result: 得到功特征函数的精确解析式，仅涉及一个辛矩阵、一个相空间位移和一个累积相位，适用于任意自由度数、任意含时二次哈密顿量及任意初始热平衡态。由此导出Helmholtz自由能、Jarzynski等式、平均功、功方差和非平衡滞后等公式；无位移哈密顿量、突然淬火、常Hessian协议和绝热极限均为通式特例。以一维非常规辛路径系统为例，展示仿射项与纯辛项对功统计和不可逆性的影响。

Conclusion: 该非齐次Metaplectic群框架为含时二次玻色子系统量子功统计提供了统一且精确的解析工具，将时间排序困难转化为相空间辛路径问题，统一涵盖多种已知特例，并清晰区分仿射与纯辛贡献对功统计和不可逆性的作用。

Abstract: We derive an exact analytical expression for the characteristic function of work in closed many-body bosonic systems governed by general time-dependent quadratic Hamiltonians. This is made possible by adopting a unified prescription in which all operators appearing in the characteristic function are treated as elements of the inhomogeneous Metaplectic group. The esulting expression involves a single symplectic matrix, a single phase-space displacement, and a single accumulated phase. It holds for any number of degrees of freedom, arbitrary time dependence of the quadratic Hamiltonian, and any initial thermal equilibrium state of the system. The formalism trades the explicit time-ordering problem associated with the unitary evolution for the determination of a symplectic path in phase space, from which the corresponding driving Hamiltonian can be reconstructed. We also discuss the generalization to arbitrary nonequilibrium initial states within the two-projective-measurement scheme for quantum work. The same construction yields Loschmidt echoes and mixed-state total phases. Using the derived characteristic function, we obtain formulas for the Helmholtz free energy, the Jarzynski equality, the mean work, the work variance, and the nonequilibrium lag. Undisplaced Hamiltonians, sudden quenches, constant-Hessian protocols, and the adiabatic limit follow as special cases of the general result. As an illustration, we analyze a one-dimensional system constructed from an unconventional symplectic path and exhibiting physically relevant limiting cases. We then show how affine and purely symplectic contributions affect work statistics and irreversibility.

</details>


### [120] [Environmental records unlock universal quantum computation from thermal decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16460)
*Chenfeng Cao,Qi Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对理想stabilizer控制、独立局部Markov浴下量子处理器中的"能量计数热空闲仪器"给出精确计算分类，边界由$(1-p_e)T_2/T_1=1$决定：边界以下经典可模拟，边界以上在单一可重复访问位点可实现通用量子计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热暴露下的量子处理器究竟经典可模拟还是通用量子，取决于控制器保留哪些环境记录；作者希望给出统一、精确的计算能力边界，并厘清温度、退相干与记录保留方式对计算能力的影响。

Method: 针对能量计数热空闲仪器（理想stabilizer控制、独立局部Markov浴），以弛豫时间$T_1$、均匀相干时间$T_2$、平衡激发态布居$p_e$为参数，推导精确计算边界$(1-p_e)T_2/T_1=1$；下方构造分支非负stabilizer分解的高效经典采样器（含完整时间分辨交换记录），上方利用合适空闲时长与"无交换"条件化蒸馏ancilla实现通用量子计算；并分析有限温度下擦除记录、终端奇偶校验与保留单比特交换记录的情形。

Result: 边界$(1-p_e)T_2/T_1\leq1$时，分支非负stabilizer分解给出自适应电路及完整时间分辨交换记录的显式高效经典采样器；在单一可重复访问位点上超过边界，配合合适空闲时长与无交换条件化，可提供可蒸馏ancilla并以多项式开销实现通用量子计算。有限温度资源侧，足够长且未分裂的暴露中擦除记录会使平均信道变为stabilizer测量-制备型，即使终端奇偶校验也只产生可模拟分支；但同样暴露下仅保留"是否发生交换"的单比特，仍能预示可蒸馏ancilla，因为热往返在首次交换已移除相干性之后又恢复了奇偶性。

Conclusion: 热空闲仪器的计算能力由环境记录的保留方式与参数边界$(1-p_e)T_2/T_1=1$精确刻画：擦除记录可压低至经典可模拟，而保留关键的交换发生位则足以恢复通用量子计算能力。

Abstract: At a fixed thermal exposure, the same stabilizer processor can be classically simulable or quantum universal, depending on which environmental records its controller retains. We give an exact computational classification of energy-counting thermal-idle instruments in a quantum processor with ideal stabilizer control and independent local Markov baths. The relaxation time $T_1$, homogeneous coherence time $T_2$, and equilibrium excited-state population $p_e$ determine an exact computational boundary at $(1-p_e)T_2/T_1=1$. If every location lies at or below it, branchwise nonnegative stabilizer decompositions give an explicit efficient classical sampler for the adaptive circuit and its full time-resolved exchange record. Above it at a single repeatedly accessible location, a suitable idle duration and no-exchange conditioning supply distillable ancillas and enable universal quantum computation with polynomial overhead. At finite temperature on the resource side, erasing the record at a sufficiently long, unsplit exposure makes the averaged channel stabilizer measure-and-prepare, and even a terminal parity check then yields only simulable branches. Yet at that same exposure, retaining only the bit recording whether any exchange occurred still heralds distillable ancillas, because a thermal round trip restores the parity after its first exchange has already removed the coherence.

</details>


### [121] [Error-Bounded Fixed-Point Design of Super-Sample-Rate IIR Filters for Real-Time Superconducting Qubit Flux Predistortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16488)
*Matt Huszabianlou,Angelos Ioannou,Nirmalendu Bikash Patra,Anastasiia Butko,Gang Huang,Irfan Siddiqi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对超导量子处理器中磁通驱动线失真校正的数字滤波器FPGA实现问题，从阶跃响应误差容限出发推导定点系数量化格式与累加器位宽，并给出量化保持稳定性的条件，在QubiC平台上以500 MHz时钟实现超采样率+分散前瞻流水线的FIR/IIR级联校正，实测校正偏置T型失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁通激活两比特门需要校正线失真，否则会产生残余失谐、条件相位误差和泄漏。已有基于cryoscope的失真校准多依赖商用方案，其只给出滤波器模型而未公开FPGA实现细节；对定制与开源系统而言，定点系数与数据通路格式直接决定校正精度，且标准形式的IIR反馈环在目标时钟频率下无法满足时序，因此需要自主推导量化格式并设计满足时序的实现结构。

Method: 基于阶跃响应误差容限推导校正滤波器的系数格式：利用量化引起的极点与零点在各自失真物理参数范围内的位移上界，确定系数量化位数；由输出端必须可分辨的最小输入变化确定累加器格式；同套分析给出量化保持滤波器稳定性的条件。在QubiC平台上以500 MHz fabric时钟实现FIR/IIR级联，采用超采样率结构与分散前瞻流水线，将反馈递归移出关键路径且不改变目标传递函数。

Result: 针对1 GS/s磁通线的代表性级联（一个积分器、一个二阶节与一个20抽头FIR）消耗88个DSP slice，附加162 ns延迟；通过校正特征性偏置T型失真验证了硬件实现，该步骤是迈向完整cryoscope校准的中间阶段。

Conclusion: 通过阶跃响应误差容限分析可系统确定校正滤波器的定点系数与累加器格式，并给出量化稳定性条件；超采样率+分散前瞻流水线结构使IIR反馈环在500 MHz时钟下满足时序且保持传递函数不变，在开源QubiC平台上以有限DSP资源实现失真校正，为定制/开源超导量子控制系统的cryoscope校准奠定硬件基础。

Abstract: Flux-activated two-qubit gates in superconducting processors require correction of line distortion, which otherwise produces residual detuning, conditional-phase error, and leakage. Prior demonstrations of cryoscope-based distortion calibration have generally used commercial solutions that specify the filter models but not their FPGA implementation. These details matter for custom and open-source systems, where the fixed-point coefficient and datapath formats set how accurately the correction is realized, and the IIR feedback loop in its standard form does not meet timing at the required clock rate. We derive the coefficient formats of the correction filters from a step response error tolerance, using bounds on the quantization-induced displacement of the poles and zeros over the physical parameter range of each distortion, and the accumulator format from the smallest input change that must remain resolvable at the output. The same analysis gives the conditions under which quantization preserves filter stability. We implement the resulting FIR/IIR cascade on the QubiC platform at a fabric clock rate of \SI{500}{MHz}, using a super-sample-rate structure with scattered look-ahead pipelining, which removes the feedback recursion from the critical path without changing the target transfer function. A representative cascade for our \SI{1}{GS/s} flux lines, comprising an integrator, a second-order section, and a 20-tap FIR, consumes 88 DSP slices and adds \SI{162}{ns} of latency. We confirm the hardware implementation by correcting characteristic bias-tee distortion, an intermediate step toward full cryoscope-based calibration.

</details>


### [122] [Truncated hybrid tensor networks for distributed quantum simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16520)
*Yong Liu,Guangyao Huang,Weixu Shi,Yizhi Wang,Jiandong Ouyang,Zeqian Chen,Junjie Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出截断混合张量网络(THTN)框架,通过经典连接张量与接口截断实现跨切分远程门的分布式量子多体模拟,以保留Schmidt模式替代电路编织的准概率采样,显著降低采样开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体模拟是量子计算的核心应用,但现有设备受量子比特数限制,无法容纳目标规模系统。将大演化分布到多个中小型系统上、仅交换经典信息是一种可行思路。然而现有电路编织等协议存在准概率采样代价,该代价随跨切远程门数量指数增长,即便跨切物理关联实际有界时也是如此,亟需更高效的分布式协议。

Method: 提出截断混合张量网络(THTN)框架:将远程两体门表示为各子系统上局域幺正算符之和,并通过经典连接张量相连;引入接口截断,保留跨切Schmidt模式。同一子系统间的多个远程门可合并到单个连接器上,从而将非一维模型(如层内强耦合、层间弱耦合的层状划分)映射为一维切分。保留的非负Schmidt系数同时定义局域可观测量的采样规则。通过经典模拟,在相同键维下与时间演化块消减(TEBD)进行对比验证。

Result: 在相同键维下,截断动力学结果与TEBD参考在链状和梯状模型上保持一致,其中在层状模型上增益最为明显。

Conclusion: THTN框架为分布式量子多体模拟提供了一种有效途径,通过接口截断与Schmidt模式保留避免了电路编织中随远程门数指数增长的准概率采样代价,并能借助连接器合并将高维划分映射为一维切分;经经典模拟验证其动力学在链、梯及层状模型上与TEBD一致,层状模型收益最大。

Abstract: Simulation of quantum many-body systems is a principal application of quantum computing, but available devices remain limited by the number of qubits and cannot accommodate systems of the desired size. One approach is to host a large evolution across several modest distributed systems that exchange classical information alone. Current protocols such as circuit knitting incur a quasi-probability sampling cost that grows exponentially with the number of remote gates across the cut, even when physical correlations across the cut are actually bounded. In this work, we present a truncated hybrid tensor network (THTN) framework in which a remote two-body gate is applied as a sum of local unitaries on the subsystems, linked by a classical connecting tensor, and an interface truncation retains Schmidt modes across the cut. Remote gates between the same subsystems can be merged on one connector, so non-one-dimensional models, such as layered partitions with strong intra-layer and weaker interlayer couplings, may be cast onto a one-dimensional cut. The retained non-negative Schmidt coefficients also define a sampling rule for local observables. We validate this distributed protocol by classical simulation against time-evolving block decimation (TEBD) at the same bond dimension. The truncated dynamics track the TEBD references on chains and ladders, with the clearest gain on a layered model.

</details>


### [123] [Phantom Codes: Hardness, Rate Optimal qLDPC Constructions, and Distance Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16542)
*Rui Mao,Weixiao Sun,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究"phantom"稳定子码（逻辑CNOT可由物理量子比特置换实现）。证明其判定至少与图同构同难；构造出首个速率最优的phantom qLDPC族（k=Θ(log n)，固定距离）；并给出距离no-go定理：当k=ω(√log n)且校验权重O(1)时d≤w。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑纠缠门（如CNOT）通常需要庞大且复杂的物理开销。若代码具有phantom性质，则块内逻辑CNOT仅靠物理量子比特的置换即可实现，从而消除该开销。但phantom码极为受限：数量稀少、逻辑量子比特数被限制为k=O(log n)，且此前不存在逻辑维度可增长的phantom qLDPC族。本文旨在刻画其计算复杂度极限并突破构造与距离的瓶颈。

Method: 1）复杂性：将图同构归约到phantom性判定，构造仅编码k=2个逻辑量子比特的CSS码实例。2）构造：设计CSS码族，使每个固定距离参数D下达到k=Θ(log n)与d≥D。3）下界：结合码参数与校验权重约束，证明当k=ω(√log n)、w=O(1)时距离受限。

Result: （1）判定phantom性至少与图同构同难，即使对k=2的CSS码亦成立。（2）给出首个速率最优phantom qLDPC族：对任意固定D，k=Θ(log n)（最大逻辑缩放）且d≥D。（3）距离no-go：任何k=ω(√log n)、校验权重w=O(1)的phantom码族，对足够大n有d≤w。

Conclusion: phantom码在计算识别上具有图同构级难度，构造上却可达到速率最优；同时其距离扩展存在根本限制。因此本文的固定距离qLDPC族在最大逻辑缩放k=Θ(log n)下达到距离扩展最优，明确了phantom码的能力边界。

Abstract: An $[[n,k,d]]$ stabilizer code is phantom if every in-block logical CNOT gate can be realized by a permutation of its physical qubits. This eliminates the large and complicated physical overhead normally required for logical entangling gates. Yet this symmetry is highly restrictive: phantom codes are rare, the number of logical qubits is limited to $k=O(\log n)$, and no phantom qLDPC family with growing logical dimension was previously known.
  We make three contributions.
  1. We prove that recognizing phantomness of a given stabilizer code is at least as hard as Graph Isomorphism, even for CSS codes encoding only $k=2$ logical qubits.
  2. We propose the first rate optimal phantom qLDPC families: for every fixed $D$, our CSS families achieve the maximal logical scaling $k=Θ(\log n)$ and distance $d\geq D$.
  3. We prove a distance no-go theorem: every phantom code family with $k=ω(\sqrt{\log n})$ and check weight $w=O(1)$ satisfies $d\leq w$ for sufficiently large $n$.
  Thus our fixed-distance qLDPC families are distance-scaling optimal at the maximal logical scaling $k=Θ(\log n)$.

</details>


### [124] [Partial projected ensembles reveal slow tilt-constrained information spreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16544)
*Yi-Rui Zhang,Yu-Jun Zhao,Han-Ze Li,Jian-Xin Zhong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究空间约束如何影响投影系综（projected ensembles）中信息的出现，以空间变化纵向场的踢动Ising链为模型。通过测量远程区域并保留中间缓冲未观测，发现遍历区与倾斜约束区在关联起始时间上差异显著，但条件态涨落随缓冲长度均近似指数衰减；完整投影系综的高阶矩表现出慢弛豫与测量基依赖。作者建立连接二阶矩的信息论刻画，并用可解退相干模型说明相位累积与相干损失的分离，提出部分投影系综可区分"关联形成时间"与"部分观测下仍可获取的信息"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影系综蕴含约化密度矩阵之外的多体动力学信息。作者希望理解空间约束（如未观测缓冲区）如何影响这类信息的涌现，并区分关联建立所需时间与部分观测下仍可获取的信息。

Method: 在带空间变化纵向场的踢动Ising链中引入部分投影系综：测量远程区域、保留中间缓冲区未观测。数值计算关联起始、量子互信息与条件态涨落；建立连接二阶矩的信息论刻画；构造含有效基测量的可解退相干模型进行分析。

Result: 遍历区关联近乎弹道式起始，倾斜约束区起始显著延迟，量子互信息佐证；两种情形下条件态涨落随缓冲长度近似指数衰减。完整投影系综高阶矩呈慢弛豫与持续测量基依赖。可解模型中因子级压低的耦合包络给出亚对数扩散，与有限距离起始趋势一致。

Conclusion: 部分投影系综可作为探针，揭示关联形成时间尺度与部分观测下信息可获取性之间的本质区别；空间约束显著调控该信息的涌现与可及性。

Abstract: Projected ensembles reveal information about quantum many-body dynamics beyond the reduced density matrix. Here we investigate how spatial constraints affect the emergence of this information in a kicked Ising chain with a spatially varying longitudinal field. We consider partial projected ensembles, obtained by measuring a remote region while leaving an intervening buffer unobserved. Our numerical results reveal a pronounced contrast between nearly ballistic correlation onset in the ergodic regime and strongly delayed onset in the tilt-constrained regime, corroborated by quantum mutual information. Despite these different onset scales, the conditional-state fluctuations decrease approximately exponentially with buffer length in both regimes. Full projected ensembles additionally exhibit slow relaxation and persistent measurement-basis dependence in their higher moments. We establish an information-theoretic characterization of the connected second moment and use a solvable dephasing model with effective-basis measurements to illustrate the separation of phase accumulation and coherence loss. Within this model, a factorially suppressed coupling envelope yields sublogarithmic spreading consistent with the finite-distance onset trends. Our results identify partial projected ensembles as probes of the distinction between the time required for correlations to develop and the information that remains accessible under partial observation.

</details>


### [125] [Pretty good quantum state transfer via transcendental edge weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16549)
*Addison Ballif,Mark Kempton,James B. Larsen,Kellon Sandall,Christino Tamon,Trevor Wai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：将具有普适完美态传输的循环图与固定长度路径做根积，且该路径末边权重为超越数时，各路径端点之间存在相当好态传输；由此得到偶数顶点路径在端点相邻两边赋予超越数权重时存在相当好态传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络中的态传输（信息在量子比特间转移）是量子计算与量子通信的核心问题。已有工作关注完美态传输（PST）与相当好态传输（PGST），但将循环图的普适PST性质与带权重路径结合构造PGST的结论尚不充分，作者希望借助超越数权重与根积结构获得更一般的PGST存在性结论。

Method: 采用图论与谱方法，引入“具有普适完美态传输的循环图”，与固定长度路径做根积（rooted product），并将路径的末端边赋予超越数权重；利用谱分析中的数论性质（超越数导致的非共振条件）证明端点间的PGST。

Result: 证明了在该根积图中，任意一对路径端点之间均存在相当好态传输；并作为推论，在具有偶数个顶点的路径上，若与端点相邻的两条边赋予超越数权重，则该路径存在相当好态传输。

Conclusion: 超越数权重与根积构造是生成相当好态传输的有效手段，该结果为在更广泛的图类中构造PGST提供了新途径，也推广了路径上PGST的存在性结果。

Abstract: We prove that if we take a rooted product of a circulant graph with universal perfect state transfer with a path of fixed length whose end edge is weighted with a transcendental number, then there is pretty good state transfer between any pair of endpoints of these paths. As a consequence, in a path with an even number of vertices with transcendental weights on the two edges incident to the endpoints, there is pretty good state transfer.

</details>


### [126] [Evolution of quantum imaginarity in black hole quantum atmosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17314)
*Ruopu Sun,Xiaofen Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了静态Schwarzschild黑洞量子大气中双分狄拉克场约化态的量子虚构性（imaginarity），揭示了三种主流虚构性度量随霍金热辐射的演化规律及曲率背景下的再分配效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子虚构性是量化量子态内在非实相干性的关键资源度量，其在弯曲时空背景下的演化行为尚不清晰，本文旨在探究霍金热辐射对黑洞量子大气中虚构性的调制机制。

Method: 针对静态Schwarzschild黑洞量子大气中的双分狄拉克场约化态，采用相对熵虚构性、几何虚构性与鲁棒性虚构性三种度量，分析其随径向位置的变化及其与Hartle-Hawking常数、事件视界半径的关系。

Result: 完全可及态呈现谷形径向分布，在量子大气内部存在局部极小值；交叉耦合态与完全不可及态则呈相反的峰形趋势。Hartle-Hawking常数显著增强两区域间虚构性的再分配效应，而事件视界半径增大则削弱该再分配并抹平极值特征。

Conclusion: 研究为解读黑洞量子大气的信息结构及霍金辐射的内禀量子本质提供了新视角。

Abstract: Quantum imaginarity, as a critical resource metric for quantifying intrinsic nonreal coherence encoded in quantum states, exhibits nontrivial evolutionary behaviors in curved spacetime backgrounds. This work focuses on bipartite Dirac field reduced states in the quantum atmosphere of a static Schwarzschild black hole, aiming to explore the modulation of three mainstream imaginarity measures by Hawking thermal radiation. We reveal that the relative-entropy imaginarity, the geometric imaginarity and robustness of imaginarity, the fully accessible state presents a valley-shaped radial profile with a local minimum inside the quantum atmosphere, while the cross-coupling and fully inaccessible states follow opposite peak-shaped trends. Further analysis demonstrates that the Hartle-Hawking constant significantly strengthens the redistribution effect of imaginarity across both regions, whereas an increase in the event horizon radius weakens such redistribution and flattens the extremum features. These findings offer a new perspective for decoding the information structure of black hole quantum atmospheres and the intrinsic quantum nature of Hawking radiation.

</details>


### [127] [Strong converse for the quantum capacity of the pure-loss bosonic channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16608)
*Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了纯损耗玻色信道在无约束量子容量下的强逆定理：任何高于容量的固定速率，其纠缠生成保真度都被常数与信道使用次数倒数之积上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯损耗玻色信道的无约束量子容量已知，但此前缺乏相应的强逆定理，即速率超过容量时编码保真度如何随使用次数衰减的严格上界。

Method: 结合量子Chebyshev不等式与hockey-stick检验不等式，以及对平衡纯损耗信道（透射率η=1/2）的一致相对熵方差界；将该方差界通过亮模/暗模分解导出（暗模处于真空态，任何正交于该真空的态至少含一个暗光子）；对一般透射率，通过扩张退化衰减器归约到平衡信道，并以产生已知量子容量阈值的因子约束解码器检验。

Result: 对任意速率高于容量，纠缠生成保真度被常数乘以信道使用次数的倒数所上界；该界无需能量约束，适用于任意编码态（包括跨所有输入模相关的态）与任意联合解码器。

Conclusion: 该论证建立了所有纯损耗玻色信道在无约束量子容量处的强逆定理。

Abstract: This paper reports the proof of a strong converse for the unconstrained quantum capacity of the pure-loss bosonic channel. At every fixed rate above capacity, the entanglement-generation fidelity of every code is bounded by a constant times the reciprocal of the number of channel uses. The bound holds without an energy constraint and for arbitrary encoded states, including states correlated across all input modes, and arbitrary joint decoders. The proof combines quantum Chebyshev and hockey-stick testing inequalities with a uniform relative-entropy-variance bound for the balanced pure-loss channel, corresponding to transmissivity $η=1/2$. The variance bound follows by expressing the balanced beam splitter in bright and dark modes: the dark modes are exactly in vacuum, and any state orthogonal to that vacuum contains at least one dark photon. For general transmissivity, dilating the degrading attenuator reduces the problem to this balanced-channel setting and bounds the decoder test by precisely the factor that produces the known quantum-capacity threshold. The resulting argument establishes the strong converse at the unconstrained quantum capacity for every pure-loss bosonic channel.

</details>


### [128] [Quantum Simulation of Two-Dimensional Free Dirac Hamiltonian in Multimode Circuit QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16628)
*Jiwon Kang,Jiuk Lee,Eliya Blumenthal,Shay Hacohen-Gourgy,Eunseong Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作在多模电路QED平台上实现了可编程二维大质量Dirac哈密顿量，观测到旋转Zitterbewegung及其对有效质量的依赖，为高维相对论量子动力学提供紧凑可编程平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维大质量Dirac系统具有带隙Dirac锥色散，是凝聚态与拓扑物理中众多现象的核心。已有量子模拟仅实现了一维Dirac动力学和二维无质量Weyl动力学，而二维大质量Dirac动力学的可编程模拟在实验上仍属空白。

Method: 利用单个Rabi驱动的qubit与多模腔的两个模式耦合，构建可独立调节的自旋-动量耦合项与质量项，从而在电路QED中实现可编程的二维自由Dirac哈密顿量。

Result: 观测到二维大质量Dirac粒子的旋转Zitterbewegung，并验证其对有效质量的依赖关系；含测量的退相干、超出RWA的修正以及transmon非谐性的含时主方程模拟与实验动力学吻合。

Conclusion: 结果确立了多模电路QED作为高维相对论量子动力学的紧凑可编程平台，并为探索带隙Dirac系统中的动力学与拓扑现象奠定基础。

Abstract: Two-dimensional massive Dirac systems feature a gapped Dirac-cone dispersion central to a broad range of phenomena in condensed-matter and topological physics. While quantum simulations have demonstrated one-dimensional Dirac dynamics and two-dimensional massless Weyl dynamics, programmable simulation of massive two-dimensional Dirac dynamics remains experimentally unexplored. Here, we realize a programmable two-dimensional free Dirac Hamiltonian in circuit QED, with independently tunable spin-momentum couplings and mass, using a single Rabi-driven qubit coupled to two modes of a multimode cavity. Using this Hamiltonian, we observe rotational Zitterbewegung of a two-dimensional massive Dirac particle and its dependence on the effective mass. Time-dependent master-equation simulations incorporating measured decoherence, corrections beyond the rotating-wave approximation (RWA), and anharmonicity of the transmon reproduce the observed dynamics. Our results establish multimode circuit QED as a compact, programmable platform for higher-dimensional relativistic quantum dynamics and provide a foundation for exploring dynamical and topological phenomena in gapped Dirac systems.

</details>


### [129] [Two-qubit gates for the soft 0-π qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16630)
*Zhenxing Liu,Eli Weissler,Joshua Combes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对电容耦合的软 0-π 量子比特的非受保护 CZ 门方案。该方案利用计算态与更高非计算态之间的直接跃迁，在忽略器件无序并采用唯象噪声模型的假设下，模拟 CZ 门在约 160 ns 的门时间内实现了约 99.9% 的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 0-π 量子比特有望比 transmon 具备更长的寿命，是下一代低错误率量子比特的有力候选。此前 Gyenis 等人在软 0-π 区域实验演示了非受保护的单量子比特门，但尚无人提出相应的双量子比特门，本文将填补这一空白。

Method: 针对电容耦合的软 0-π 量子比特设计非受保护 CZ 门，利用计算态与更高非计算态之间的直接跃迁实现门操作；在假定器件无序可忽略并采用唯象噪声模型的条件下进行数值模拟。

Result: 模拟结果表明，该 CZ 门在约 160 ns 的门时间下保真度约为 99.9%。

Conclusion: 该工作首次提出软 0-π 量子比特的 CZ 门方案，展示了高保真度与较短门时间的潜力，为 0-π 量子比特平台实现双量子比特门提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The 0-π qubit promises longer lifetimes than the transmon, making it a strong candidate for a next-generation, lower-error qubit. Gyenis et al. experimentally demonstrated an unprotected single-qubit gate in the soft 0-π regime [1], but no corresponding two-qubit gate has yet been proposed. We propose an unprotected CZ gate for capacitively coupled soft 0-π qubits. The gate uses a direct transition between a computational state and a higher non-computational state. Assuming negligible device disorder and a phenomenological noise model, the simulated CZ gate achieves a fidelity of approximately 99.9% at a gate time of approximately 160 ns.

</details>


### [130] [Approximate synthesis of general single-qubit unitaries over the Clifford+$\sqrt{T}$ gate set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16659)
*Mathias Weiden,Jae Won Kim,Justin Kalloor,John Kubiatowicz,Costin Iancu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将 Morisaki 等人的整数格点枚举法推广到 Clifford+$\sqrt{T}$ 门集，首次给出确定性的、无辅助量子比特的一般单量子比特酉算子合成算法，并在魔法态催化成本模型下证明其成本渐近低于最优 Clifford+$T$ 电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\sqrt{T}$ 门旋转角为 $T$ 门的一半，可生成更细的可实现操作格点，直觉上应能降低合成成本；然而此前不存在针对确定性、无辅助单量子比特酉算子的直接 Clifford+$\sqrt{T}$ 合成算法。

Method: 推广 Morisaki 等人的整数格点枚举方法以适配 Clifford+$\sqrt{T}$ 门集；采用 Gidney 与 Fowler 的魔法态催化资源成本模型进行成本评估。

Result: 在 $\varepsilon=10^{-3}$ 至 $10^{-8}$ 的 Haar 随机目标上，Clifford+$\sqrt{T}$ 电路成本按 $2.4\log_2(1/\varepsilon)$ 缩放，而可证明 $T$-count 最优的 Clifford+$T$ 电路为 $3.0\log_2(1/\varepsilon)$；一次性催化态摊销后，Clifford+$\sqrt{T}$ 电路成本始终不高于其 Clifford+$T$ 对应方案。

Conclusion: $\sqrt{T}$ 魔法态的引入确实能显著降低确定性与无辅助单量子比特酉算子合成的成本，且 Clifford+$\sqrt{T}$ 电路在摊销条件下具有严格不劣于最优 Clifford+$T$ 电路的资源表现。

Abstract: For the standard Clifford+$T$ gate set, deterministic, ancilla-free synthesis now attains the minimal $T$-count for general single-qubit unitaries (Morisaki et al., arXiv:2510.05816). The $\sqrt{T}$ gate rotates by half the angle of $T$, generating a finer lattice of implementable operations. It was assumed that access to this magic state lowers the cost of deterministic and ancilla-free synthesis of general single-qubit unitaries, but no direct Clifford+$\sqrt{T}$ algorithm existed for this case. We provide one by extending the integer lattice-point enumeration method of Morisaki et al. We adopt a resource state cost model based on the magic-state catalysis approach of Gidney and Fowler (arXiv:1812.01238). On Haar-random targets synthesized to precisions ranging from $\varepsilon=10^{-3}$ to $10^{-8}$, the cost of Clifford+$\sqrt{T}$ circuits scales as $2.4\log_2(1/\varepsilon)$ compared to $3.0\log_2(1/\varepsilon)$ for the provably $T$-count-optimal Clifford+$T$ circuits. Once a one-time catalyst state is amortized, the Clifford+$\sqrt{T}$ circuits are never costlier than their Clifford+$T$ counterparts.

</details>


### [131] [Phase Transition in Binary Compressed Sensing via Annealing with Adaptive Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16712)
*Xiaoxin Huang,Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种正则化选择方法，将系统参数搜索与随机森林回归结合，用于退火类二值压缩感知；预测的正则化值可复用于SA与量子-经典混合求解器，并在部分参数区间使混合求解器重建误差更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则化选择会改变退火类二值压缩感知的恢复相图，因此需要一种可迁移、可复用的正则化确定方式，以避免在每个问题设置下重复进行候选网格搜索。

Method: 在无噪声高斯测量且稀疏度已知条件下，通过重复模拟退火（SA）试验，在候选网格中按最小均方重建误差选取参考正则化参数；再用随机森林从信号维度、采样比和稀疏度预测这些参考值；随后将同一预测器直接用于SA和量子-经典混合求解器，无需重新训练。

Result: 使用预测正则化时，SA恢复转变在较大信号维度下大体遵循盒约束ℓ1恢复的渐近参考边界；在匹配问题实例上，混合求解器在部分评估参数空间中的均方重建误差小于SA；所得规则可复用搜索信息而无需逐设置重复候选搜索。

Conclusion: 结果为所述有限候选网格与求解器设置下的经验性能提供了量化结果，但不构成与求解器无关的恢复保证，也不构成求解时间对比。

Abstract: Regularization choice changes the recovery phase diagrams of annealing-based binary compressed sensing. We develop a regularization-selection method that combines systematic parameter search with random forest regression. Under noiseless Gaussian measurements with known sparsity, reference parameters are selected from a candidate grid by minimizing mean squared reconstruction error over repeated simulated annealing (SA) trials. The fitted model predicts these reference values from signal dimension, sampling ratio, and sparsity. With predicted regularization, the SA recovery transition broadly follows the asymptotic reference boundary for box-constrained $\ell_1$ recovery at the larger signal dimensions examined. Without retraining, the same predictor supplies identical regularization values to SA and a quantum--classical hybrid solver. On matched problem instances, the hybrid solver yields smaller mean squared reconstruction errors than SA in parts of the evaluated parameter space. The resulting rule reuses the searched information for subsequent reconstruction without repeating candidate searches at each setting. The results quantify empirical performance under the stated finite candidate grid and solver settings; they do not constitute a solver-independent recovery guarantee or a time-to-solution comparison.

</details>


### [132] [Improved Separations between Quantum and Classical Communication Complexity of Total Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16726)
*François Le Gall*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在Gavinsky框架（arXiv:2608.18784）基础上改进，得到全函数量子与随机通信复杂度之间更大的指数级分离：两量子消息情形下量子通信为多对数级、随机通信为Ω̃(√n)；更多量子消息情形下随机通信可达Ω(n^{1-ε})（任意固定0<ε<1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gavinsky已给出全函数上的量子-随机通信复杂度指数分离框架，但分离幅度有限。本文旨在细化该框架，在相同问题类型（全函数）下获得量子与随机通信复杂度之间更大的分离，逼近甚至达到已知不可能性界附近的极值。

Method: 细化并推广Gavinsky的框架（arXiv:2608.18784），通过改进构造与参数设置来放大分离幅度：在仅允许两条量子消息的受限通信模型下构造新的全函数；再借助增加量子消息数目的扩展版本，进一步将随机通信复杂度的下界提升到多项式级。

Result: 两量子消息模型：量子通信复杂度为polylog(n)，而随机通信复杂度为Ω̃(√n)；用量子消息更多的模型：随机通信复杂度达到Ω(n^{1-ε})（对每个固定的0<ε<1），而量子通信复杂度仍保持多对数级。

Conclusion: 该工作细化了Gavinsky的框架并获得更大的量子-随机通信复杂度分离，表明全函数上量子相对随机的优势可以随量子消息数增加而显著增强，把两消息情形推向Ω̃(√n)、多消息情形推向近线性下界，为全函数通信复杂度的量子优势上限提供了更强的证据。

Abstract: We refine Gavinsky's framework (arXiv:2608.18784) for exponential separations between quantum and randomized communication complexity of total functions and obtain larger separations: polylogarithmic quantum communication versus $\tildeΩ(\sqrt n)$ randomized communication with two quantum messages, and versus $Ω(n^{1-\varepsilon})$ for every fixed $0<\varepsilon<1$ with more quantum messages.

</details>


### [133] [Experimental Private Quantum Networked Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16743)
*Nicolas Laurent-Puig,Laura dos Santos Martins,Luis Bugalho,Santiago Scheiner,Majid Hassani,Sean William Moore,Damian Markham,Eleni Diamanti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种抗噪声的私密量子网络化传感协议，利用高保真GHZ态源实现，并对比GHZ态、两个Bell对和完全可分离态在攻击下的表现，证明GHZ态在精度、准确度和隐私方面具有明显优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠远距离量子传感器可实现局部参数全局函数的高精度估计，但网络中存在恶意方导致信息泄露风险。通常要求局部参数保密、仅共享全局函数，因此需要兼顾估计优势与隐私保护的方案。

Method: 引入一种噪声鲁棒的私密量子网络化传感协议，并使用高保真Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger（GHZ）态源进行实现；同时开展对比分析，模拟针对三种量子态（四量子比特GHZ态、两个Bell对、完全可分离态）的攻击。

Result: 对比与攻击模拟结果表明，GHZ态在分布式估计任务中维持高精度、高准确度和隐私性方面具有明显优势。

Conclusion: GHZ态是实现噪声鲁棒私密量子网络化传感的优越资源，为未来量子网络中兼顾估计增益与隐私保护提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Entangling distant quantum sensors is a key application of future quantum networks, allowing for the estimation of global functions of local parameters, with precision that is not possible with stand-alone, individual, quantum sensors. However, with this advantage comes the risk of information leakage over the network via possible malicious parties. It is often particularly important that local parameters remain unknown and that only the global function is shared across the network. Recently, the notion of privacy has been introduced in this context, which ensures that only the agreed function of parameters is shared over the network, even when malicious parties control the network itself, whilst maintaining the estimation advantage. In this work, we introduce a noise-robust protocol for private quantum networked sensing, which we realise using a high-fidelity Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) state source. We further run a comparative analysis and simulate attacks using three different quantum states, a four-qubit GHZ state, two Bell pairs, and a fully separable state. Our results highlight the clear advantage of GHZ states in maintaining high precision, accuracy, and privacy for the distributed estimation task.

</details>


### [134] [Understanding the spin coherence of molecular photoexcited triplet states from first principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16851)
*Ecaterina Păunică,Sam L. Bayliss*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用第一性原理和广义团簇关联展开方法，系统研究并阐释了并五苯客分子/对三联苯主体体系中光学可读分子自旋的Hahn回波退相干机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学可读分子自旋因纳米尺度模块化、可合成调控及灵敏读出而成为有吸引力的量子传感器，有机分子光激发三重态可在室温下提供高光学-自旋对比，其应用依赖于相干性，因此需要深入理解其退相干机制。

Method: 采用第一性原理方法，结合广义团簇关联展开（generalized cluster-correlation expansion, gCCE）方法，系统计算并分析并五苯客分子与对三联苯主体构成的基准体系在零场至高磁场下的Hahn回波退相干，考察客/主分子、特定原子核、零场分裂相互作用及超精细参数的作用。

Result: 零场退相干主要由客分子上约6个原子核驱动，而高场退相干主要由主体中约600个原子核驱动；纵向零场分裂参数D可延长T2；并揭示了磁场依赖的退相干驱动过程。

Conclusion: 研究深化了对光学可读分子自旋退相干的理解，为其合成增强及作为量子探针的应用提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Optically readable molecular spins are attractive as quantum sensors due to their nanoscale modularity, synthetic tunability, and scope for sensitive readout. In particular, photoexcited spin-triplet states in organic molecules are appealing in supporting high optical-spin contrast at room temperature. Their utility is underpinned by their coherence, which warrants a detailed understanding of it. Here, from first principles, we systematically explore the Hahn-echo decoherence of the benchmark molecular system for room-temperature optically detected spin coherence---pentacene guest molecules coupled to a para-terphenyl host. Using generalized cluster-correlation expansion methods, we investigate the mechanisms of nuclear-spin-induced decoherence from zero to high magnetic field, exploring the role of guest vs host molecules, specific nuclei, zero-field splitting interactions, and hyperfine parameters. We describe how zero-field decoherence is driven by ~6 nuclei on the guest, while high-field decoherence is driven by ~600 nuclei in the host; how the longitudinal zero-field splitting parameter, $D$, can prolong $T_2$; and the magnetic-field-dependent processes which drive decoherence. These results advance our understanding of decoherence in optically readable molecular spins, providing insight for their synthetic enhancement and deployment as quantum probes.

</details>


### [135] [Portable Vector NV-Diamond Magnetometer for Shot-Noise-Limited, Drift-Free Operation in Unshielded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16901)
*Annirudh K P,Vinayak Rane,Shradha Atakar,Sanika Joshi,Maheshwar Mangat,Jay Gharat,Siddharth Tallur,Kasturi Saha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种集成数字架构，通过动态差分读出、二阶导数洛伦兹线形跟踪与磁本征矢量变换，在多时间尺度上抑制NV金刚石磁强计的激光噪声、增益漂移与温度致偏置场漂移，实现长期稳定测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成NV金刚石磁强计兼具高灵敏度与矢量场重构能力，但实际部署受两类误差限制：短时高频激光噪声，以及长时缓变增益波动和零点漂移；此外长期漂移的主导来源尚未明确。

Method: 提出集成数字架构：1）动态差分读出，持续平衡荧光通道与参考通道以抑制相关激光噪声；2）二阶导数洛伦兹线形跟踪，原位校正增益与光激发缓变引起的斜率变化；3）识别温度诱导偏置磁涨落为主要长期漂移源，并引入磁本征矢量变换，利用NV共振的内禀响应对其进行消除。

Result: 在无屏蔽环境条件下，该架构实现非共振噪声因子1.0 ± 0.1，达到基本原理极限，并将长期漂移抑制十倍。

Conclusion: 该集成数字架构可在不同时间尺度上保证NV磁强计稳定性，实现可现场部署的稳定量子磁测量。

Abstract: Ensemble nitrogen-vacancy (NV) diamond magnetometers combine high sensitivity with vector-field reconstruction, but practical deployment is limited by errors arising from high-frequency laser noise during short-duration operations and slow-varying gain fluctuations and offset drift during long-term operation. Here, we present an integrated digital architecture for achieving NV magnetometry stability across distinct timescales. A dynamic differential readout continuously balances fluorescence and reference channels to suppress correlated laser noise. Second-derivative Lorentzian lineshape tracking enables in-situ correction of slope variations arising from slow changes in gain and optical excitation. We further identify temperature-induced bias-magnet fluctuations as a dominant source of long-term drift and introduce a magnetic eigenvector transformation that uses the intrinsic response of NV resonances to eliminate these variations. We show, under unshielded ambient conditions, that this architecture achieves an off-resonance noise factor of 1.0 $\pm$ 0.1, matching the fundamental limit with ten-fold suppression of long-term drift, enabling stable, field-ready quantum magnetometry.

</details>


### [136] [Exact efficient simulation of noisy logical magic states using Clifford stabilizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16929)
*Yugo Takada,Stephen D. Bartlett,Dominic J. Williamson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出Clifford-stabilizer simulation方法，可对含电路级Pauli噪声的逻辑magic state制备协议进行精确高效模拟，实现fault distance 7的magic state cultivation仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真逻辑magic state制备是通用容错量子计算(FTQC)的关键子程序，而预测FTQC性能和改进协议依赖于经典数值模拟。然而，Pauli-stabilizer形式虽可高效模拟含电路级Pauli错误的Clifford逻辑，但制备逻辑magic state所需的非Clifford操作使通用模拟变得低效。

Method: 提出Clifford-stabilizer simulation：一种基于更新Clifford-stabilizer群的精确高效算法。适用于Clifford层级第三层中用于实现非Clifford门的一大类逻辑magic state在电路级Pauli错误下的噪声制备协议，涵盖Pauli-stabilizer测量、逻辑Clifford测量以及横向(transversal)非Clifford门等操作。该算法将带采样电路级Pauli错误的非Clifford电路映射为能精确复现其测量结果分布的Clifford电路，时间与空间复杂度对相关协议参数均为多项式级。

Result: 通过Clifford-stabilizer simulation实现了fault distance最高达7的magic state cultivation的精确模拟。

Conclusion: 该方法为对有用FTQC所需的大规模逻辑magic state制备协议进行精确benchmarking提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The preparation of high-fidelity logical magic states is a crucial subroutine for universal fault-tolerant quantum computation (FTQC). Predicting the performance of FTQC and developing improved protocols rely on numerical methods to classically simulate logical magic state preparation in the presence of noise. Clifford logic on Pauli-stabilizer codes with circuit-level Pauli errors can be efficiently simulated using Pauli-stabilizer formalism, but the non-Clifford operations required to prepare logical magic states render generic simulation inefficient. We introduce Clifford-stabilizer simulation, an exact and efficient algorithm based on updating a Clifford-stabilizer group to simulate noisy preparation protocols for a broad class of logical magic states used to implement non-Clifford gates in the third level of the Clifford hierarchy under circuit-level Pauli errors. Clifford-stabilizer simulation applies to a range of operations that commonly appear in preparation protocols for such logical magic states, including Pauli-stabilizer measurements, logical Clifford measurements, and transversal non-Clifford gates. Our algorithm for Clifford-stabilizer simulation maps a non-Clifford circuit with sampled circuit-level Pauli errors to a Clifford circuit that exactly reproduces its measurement outcome distribution, achieving time and space complexities polynomial in relevant protocol parameters. We perform exact simulation of magic state cultivation up to fault distance 7 by Clifford-stabilizer simulation. Our method provides a route to perform exact benchmarking of large-scale logical magic state preparation protocols required for useful FTQC.

</details>


### [137] [Classical Communication Protocol based on Joint Classical-Quantum Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16938)
*Kristian Skafte Jensen,René Bødker Christensen,Čedomir Stefanović,Petar Popovski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种融合经典纠错编码、纠缠分发与超密编码的鲁棒量子通信协议，并证明其在数据率与能效上可超越传统方案的性能上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信受暗计数与光子损耗影响，传统超密编码在错误频率受限条件下的性能存在瓶颈，需寻求更高效的纠错与编码协同方案。

Method: 将经典差错控制编码、纠缠分发与超密编码集成；用经典纠错码确定量子比特传输位置并保护超密编码数据；推导有界错误频率模型下成功纠错的条件；建立基于光纤衰减、探测器效率、暗计数与时间槽时长的物理错误模型并评估不同码配置。

Result: 推导出传统超密编码加经典纠错的性能上界；在相同错误频率约束下，本协议合适的码配置在数据率和能量效率两方面均能超过这些上界。

Conclusion: 该协议适用于短距离量子链路（如量子局域网QLAN），可在集成纠缠分发的同时提供高通信吞吐量。

Abstract: We introduce a robust quantum communication protocol that integrates classical error-control coding, entanglement distribution, and superdense coding. Classical error-correcting codes are used to mitigate dark counts and photon losses by determining the positions of qubit transmissions and protecting the data embedded through superdense coding. We derive conditions on the employed codes that guarantee successful error correction under a bounded error-frequency model. Moreover, upper bounds are derived on the performance of conventional superdense coding protected by classical error correction.
