It is shown that, under the same constraints on error frequency, suitable code configurations of the proposed protocol can exceed those upper bounds both in terms of data rate and energy efficiency. Finally, we develop a physical error model based on fiber attenuation, detector efficiency, dark counts, and time-slot duration, and use it to evaluate the effective performance of different configurations of error-correcting codes. The proposed approach is primarily suited for short-distance quantum links, as in Quantum Local Area Network (QLAN) where it can provide high communication throughput while integrating entanglement distribution directly into the communication process.

</details>


### [138] [Spin Grid States for Quantum Metrology in Atomic Clocks Limited by Spontaneous Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16975)
*Marius Burgath,Klemens Hammerer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究受自发辐射噪声限制的原子系综量子计量，提出自旋网格态（SGSs）作为中等及大规模系综下的近最优探针，并给出可接近量子Fisher信息的序贯读出方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠可提升相位估计与原子钟频率估计的精度，但在真实噪声过程下识别有用的量子态与测量方式仍是核心挑战。自发辐射会限制可用询问时间，从而制约频率估计精度。

Method: 在自发辐射噪声下研究原子系综量子计量；识别SGSs并给出其制备方式（两次单轴扭转OAT操作之间插入一次集体旋转）；在置换对称态上优化量子Fisher信息以评估最优探针；提出利用OAT回波将自发辐射事件映射为集体旋转的序贯读出策略。

Result: 发现系综规模超过51时出现交叉点，GHZ类态不再最优，SGSs成为近最优探针；SGSs在Bloch球上呈现周期性网格；通过优化量子Fisher信息，SGSs在中大型系综下接近全局最优探针（最优为自旋GKP态）；所提序贯读出策略几乎饱和相应量子Fisher信息，并使测量基可依赖于衰变事件数。

Conclusion: SGSs为受自发辐射限制的原子系综实现量子增强的相位与频率计量提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Entanglement can enhance precision in phase estimation and in frequency estimation with atomic clocks, but it remains a central challenge to identify useful states and measurements under realistic noise processes. Here, we study quantum metrology with an ensemble of atoms subject to spontaneous emission, which limits frequency estimation by constraining the useful interrogation time. We identify spin grid states (SGSs) as the relevant near-optimal probes beyond a crossover at an ensemble size of 51, where GHZ-like states cease to be optimal. SGSs display a periodic grid on the Bloch sphere and can be generated with two one-axis-twisting (OAT) operations separated by a collective rotation. By optimizing the quantum Fisher information over permutationally symmetric states, we find that SGSs perform close to the globally optimal probes for intermediate and large ensembles, which turn out to be spin GKP states. We further present a sequential readout strategy that nearly saturates the corresponding quantum Fisher information. This strategy uses an OAT echo to map spontaneous-emission events onto collective rotations, making the corresponding jump sectors distinguishable and allowing the measurement basis to be conditioned on the number of decay events. Together, these results show that SGSs provide a practical route to quantum-enhanced phase and frequency metrology in atomic ensembles limited by spontaneous emission.

</details>


### [139] [Indistinguishability of single Raman photons from single atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17009)
*Pascal Baumgart,Max Bergerhoff,Jürgen Eschner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论上研究了纳秒级拉曼散射脉冲驱动下单个囚禁40Ca+离子产生单光子的不可分辨性，重点分析自发衰变回初始态对HOM干涉的影响，并比较不同囚禁离子种类在长程双轨纠缠交换中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单光子不可分辨性是量子干涉与纠缠交换等应用的核心指标，而囚禁离子拉曼散射过程中自发衰变可能破坏光子不可分辨性，需要量化其影响并指导激发脉冲优化。

Method: 针对单个囚禁40Ca+离子，在几纳秒激发脉冲驱动的拉曼散射过程中，采用数值模拟研究自发衰变回初始态对Hong-Ou-Mandel光子干涉的作用，并分析激发脉冲在光子产率与不可分辨性之间的权衡优化，同时比较不同囚禁离子种类用于长程双轨纠缠交换的性能。

Result: 数值模拟表明，平均回衰变次数是一个可与可达到的HOM可见度相关联的可测量量。

Conclusion: 平均回衰变次数可作为衡量HOM可见度的有效指标，激发脉冲需要在光子产率与不可分辨性之间优化权衡，且不同囚禁离子种类在长程双轨纠缠交换中性能存在差异。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the indistinguishability of single photons generated from single trapped $^{40}$Ca$^+$ ions in a Raman scattering process driven by few-nanosecond excitation pulses. Of particular interest is how spontaneous decay back to the initial state affects Hong-Ou-Mandel (HOM) photon interference. Numerical simulations identify the mean number of back-decays as a measurable quantity that correlates with achievable HOM visibility. Optimization of the excitation pulse with respect to a trade-off between photon yield and indistinguishability is analyzed. Finally, we compare the performance of different trapped-ion species for long-range dual-rail entanglement swapping via HOM photon interference.

</details>


### [140] [Thermodynamic Uncertainty of Work in Time-Dependently Driven Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17032)
*Chulan Kwon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为受时变协议驱动、并经历重复投影能量测量的开放量子系统推导出功的热力学不确定关系，揭示方差下界由Fisher信息、动力学活度与熵产生共同约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化开放量子系统中功的涨落下界，将经典热力学不确定关系拓展到含投影测量与耗散的量子驱动过程，并用统一框架联系Fisher信息、动力学活度与熵产生。

Method: 将驱动协议建模为一系列协议猝火（quench）之间夹有有限时长的Lindblad演化；功在猝火处累积、耗散发生在两次猝火之间。利用功统计量的Gallavotti-Cohen对称性导出Crooks与Jarzynski涨落关系；再通过扰动耗散动力学，结合Cramér-Rao不等式与量子Fisher信息，得到两个独立扰动产生的热力学界。

Result: 得到 Var W/[τ∂_τ⟨W⟩]^2 ≥ 1/F ≥ max(1/A, 2/E)，其中⟨W⟩为功期望、Var W为功方差、F为Fisher信息、A为动力学活度、E为无量纲熵产生、τ为猝火间切换时间间隔；并给出两个热力学界来自两个独立扰动但产生相同功统计响应。

Conclusion: 该关系在受耗散的两能级系统（方波与正弦驱动）中得以验证，表明更紧的热力学界依赖于驱动协议与切换时间尺度。

Abstract: We derive a thermodynamic uncertainty relation for work in an open quantum system driven by a time-dependent protocol and subject to repeated projective energy measurements. The protocol is represented by a sequence of protocol quenches separated by finite-time Lindblad evolution, so that work is accumulated at the quenches while dissipation occurs between them. The resulting work statistics obey the Gallavotti-Cohen symmetry to give rise to the Crooks and Jarzynski fluctuation relations. By perturbing the dissipative dynamics and combining the Cramér--Rao inequality with the quantum Fisher information, we obtain $\mathrm{Var}\,{\cal W}/[τ\partial_τ\langle{\cal W}\rangle]^2\ge 1/{\cal F}\ge\max(1/{\cal A},2/{\cal E})$, where $\langle{\cal W}\rangle$ is the work expectation value, $\mathrm{Var}\,{\cal W}$ is the variance of work, ${\cal F}$ is the Fisher information, ${\cal A}$ is the dynamical activity, ${\cal E}$ is the dimensionless entropy production, and $τ$ is the switching time interval between quenches. The two thermodynamic bounds follow from two independent perturbations that generate the same response of the work statistics. We illustrate the relation for a dissipative two-level system under square-wave and sinusoidal driving and show that the tighter thermodynamic bound can depend on the driving protocol and switching time scale.

</details>


### [141] [Eigenstate thermalization beyond the envelope: exact two-point overlap statistics in random free fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17037)
*Zhiqiang Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在随机自由费米子系综中精确求解了本征态-基矢重叠涨落场的两点协方差，揭示平滑包络无法决定微观涨落结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本征态热化仅约束可观测量对能量的平滑依赖（单点陈述），但未确定本征态与选定基矢重叠的微观统计；其遗留的涨落场带有结构化的两点协方差，这一区分与精确刻画在以往工作中缺失。

Method: 构造由高斯正交单粒子哈密顿量生成Slater行列式本征态的随机自由费米子系综（Magán已证其本征态热化）。利用每通道重叠为Haar正交矩阵子式的性质，借助经典随机矩阵理论进行精确解析求解，并结合有限尺寸精确数值验证。

Result: 单点律对每通道严格平坦，矩层级非Porter-Thomas：小强度呈代数增强而非指数衰减，平均扇区带有纯归一化来源的负一阶关联修正。两点协方差在高斯不动点精确闭合，由两本征态共享单体模式数与两通道共享模式数组织；其能量分辨形式因子分解为该几何因子与单粒子半圆经典卷积之积，两预解式协方差经积分变换得到。有限尺寸计算证实所有闭式解，字典结构恒等式在机器精度成立。

Conclusion: 结果为多预解式描述本征态热化所需的两点涨落扇区提供了精确可解的微观实现，结论是：平滑包络不决定精细涨落。

Abstract: Eigenstate thermalization constrains the smooth dependence of observables on energy, but it does not by itself fix the microscopic statistics of the overlaps between many-body eigenstates and a chosen basis: the smooth envelope is a one-point statement, and the fluctuation field it leaves undetermined carries a structured two-point covariance. We establish this distinction, and solve that fluctuation field exactly, in random free fermions---an ensemble of Slater-determinant eigenstates built from a Gaussian orthogonal single-particle Hamiltonian, whose eigenstate thermalization was established by Magán.
  In this ensemble every channel overlap is a minor of a Haar-distributed orthogonal matrix, so its statistics follow from classical random-matrix theory. The one-point law is exactly flat for every channel, with an exact moment hierarchy that is not Porter--Thomas: small intensities are enhanced algebraically rather than by the exponential Porter--Thomas form, and the mean sector carries a negative, order-one correlation correction of purely normalization origin. The two-point covariance closes exactly at the Gaussian fixed point: it is organized by the number of one-body modes shared by two eigenstates and by the number of modes shared by two channels, and its energy-resolved form factorizes into this geometry times the classical convolution of the single-particle semicircle, from which the two-resolvent covariance follows by an integral transform. Exact finite-size computations confirm all closed forms, and the structural identities of the dictionary hold at machine precision. The results provide an exactly solvable microscopic realization of the two-point fluctuation sector underlying multi-resolvent descriptions of eigenstate thermalization: a smooth envelope does not determine fine-grained fluctuations.

</details>


### [142] [Constant sized support state distillation with qubit recycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17044)
*Victor Barizien*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一族递归构造的魔法态蒸馏协议，可产生Clifford层级各级的对角态，并借助码加倍与量子比特回收实现固定逻辑量子比特数下的任意高距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于给定量子纠错码上无法实现通用门集合，需要借助魔法态注入，而这依赖高保真度魔法态；高质量魔法态可由低质量态蒸馏获得。因此作者希望构造更高效、更紧凑且可推广到Clifford层级各层的蒸馏协议。

Method: 利用码加倍技术递归构造蒸馏协议，并结合量子比特回收：将协议中处于空闲的量子比特进行测量并重新使用。

Result: 该协议族在Clifford层级某一给定层上，可在固定逻辑量子比特数下以任意高距离执行；它涵盖多种已知高效协议，并发现新的协议，例如距离7的|T⟩态111→1蒸馏协议，其体积最为紧凑。

Conclusion: 作者给出了一族覆盖Clifford层级各级的递归蒸馏协议，说明其在资源与距离上的优势，并讨论了将该框架扩展到产生多个输出态的蒸馏协议的可能方向。

Abstract: In order to circumvent that no universal set of gates can be achieved on a given quantum error-correcting codes, additional gates can be performed through magic state injection. This however requires to have access to high fidelity magic state, which can be obtained by the distillation of low quality states to fewer higher quality ones. In this paper, we provide a family of distillation protocols able to produce diagonal states at every level of the Clifford hierarchy. This family is obtained recursively using code doubling techniques and leveraging qubit recycling, the fact that some idle qubits of the protocols can be measured and re-used. At a given level of the Clifford hierarchy, we show that the protocols we derive can be performed at arbitrary high distance on a fix number of logical qubits. This family recovers many known efficient protocols, and uncovers new ones, such as a $111 \to 1$ protocol for $|T\rangle$ state distillation at distance $7$, which are the most compact in term of volume. Finally, we discuss possible extension of this framework for distillation protocols producing more than one output state.

</details>


### [143] [Probabilistic Error Cancellation for Single-Mode Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17095)
*Victoria Wadewitz,Alessandro Ciani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在GKP编码下结合量子错误缓解（概率错误消除，PEC）与量子纠错，比较Steane型与基于传态的GKP纠错方案，针对方形和六边形GKP码计算采样开销，并分析有限压缩噪声与解码方式对开销的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用量子计算需借助容错纠错克服噪声，GKP码以硬件高效方式在连续变量模式中编码有限维逻辑子空间。但在近期实现中无法完全消除错误，因此在纠错之外还需结合错误缓解技术，而二者在连续变量码中的结合方式尚需研究。

Method: 采用稳定子子系统分解方法，为含噪操作获得有效逻辑信道，并用以表示目标逻辑酉操作的理想信道，从而计算概率错误消除的采样开销。比较Steane型与传态型GKP纠错，覆盖方形与六边形GKP码，噪声来源为数据模式及纠错所需两个辅助模式上的有限压缩，并考察不同解码方法。

Result: 针对单量子比特与双量子比特GKP Clifford门及一轮纠错，计算出各方案的采样开销，并给出采样开销与噪声水平、解码方法之间的关系。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了连续变量码中纠错与错误缓解的非平凡结合特性，为近期GKP实现中纠错与缓解策略的协同设计提供依据。

Abstract: In order to solve practical problems on a quantum computer, it is necessary to use fault-tolerant quantum error correction schemes to overcome noise: the errors arising from imperfections in physical components. The Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) code aims at achieving this in a hardware efficient manner by encoding finite-dimensional logical subspaces in the Hilbert space of one or more continuous variable modes. In near term implementations, however, it is not feasible to eliminate errors entirely, so it is natural to also employ alternative error mitigation techniques together with error correction. In this work, we study a quantum error mitigation method known as probabilistic error cancellation in the context of the GKP code. We compare Steane-type and teleportation-based GKP error correction, and calculate the sampling overheads associated with the technique for square and hexagonal GKP codes. We employ the stabilizer subsystem decomposition for GKP codes to obtain an effective logical channel for noisy operations that is used to express the ideal one of a target logical unitary. We consider noise from finite squeezing on the data and the two ancilla modes, needed for error correction, and examine the relationship between the sampling overhead and the noise for different decoding methods. Our results are calculated for single- and two-qubit GKP Clifford gates and one round of error correction, and show the nontrivial combination of error correction and mitigation in continuous variable codes.

</details>


### [144] [Large-scale NMR simulation on a trapped-ion quantum computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17102)
*Pascal Stadler,Alec Owens,Etienne Granet,David Muñoz Ramo,Michael Marthaler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用Quantinuum的H2离子阱量子计算机，实现了对经典难算分子1,2-二叔丁基二膦烷的大规模端到端数字化NMR谱模拟，重现了以往量子硬件未能捕捉的关键谱学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NMR谱模拟是量子模拟的重要应用方向，但经典方法难以处理复杂分子，且以往量子硬件演示未能捕捉关键谱学特征，因此需要大规模、端到端的数字化NMR量子模拟以推进其实用价值。

Method: 在Quantinuum的System Model H2离子阱量子计算机上，采用硬件高效的方法约化核自旋哈密顿量，对有效21自旋模型进行Trotter化的实时演化，并施加定制的误差抑制以降低器件噪声影响。

Result: 重建的液态质子NMR谱与经典参考计算相符，并重现了以往量子硬件演示未能捕捉的关键谱学特征。

Conclusion: 鉴于NMR在化学分析与工业研究中的广泛应用，该成果推动了数字化NMR谱量子模拟向实际量子实用性的迈进。

Abstract: Simulating nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectra is a promising application of quantum simulation. Using Quantinuum's System Model H2, a trapped-ion quantum computer, we demonstrate an end-to-end, large-scale digital NMR simulation of a classically challenging benchmark molecule, 1,2-di-tert-butyl-diphosphane. We implement a hardware-efficient reduction of the nuclear-spin Hamiltonian, enabling Trotterized real-time evolution of an effective 21-spin model with tailored error suppression to reduce the effects of device noise. The reconstructed liquid-state proton NMR spectrum agrees with classical reference calculations and reproduces key spectroscopic features that previous quantum hardware demonstrations did not capture. Given the widespread use of NMR in chemical analysis and industrial research, these results advance digital quantum simulation of NMR spectra towards practical quantum utility.

</details>


### [145] [Thouless pumping via discrete jumping of the adiabatic Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17116)
*Lang Yu,Yihan Wu,Zhen-Yu Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作证明Thouless泵浦可仅通过对哈密顿量的离散采样实现，从而摆脱了传统理论中连续、缓慢变化控制参数的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准绝热泵浦理论要求哈密顿量在整个泵浦过程中连续且缓慢变化，这限制了其在量子技术中的实际应用。本文旨在去除这一传统约束。

Method: 选取哈密顿量的特定参数点用于常规连续量子绝热演化，随后在这些参数点上施加哈密顿量，使具有给定准动量的赝自旋发生测地线演化。

Result: 基于量子绝热演化的充要条件，实现了Thouless泵浦的绝热演化，仅通过哈密顿量的离散采样即可完成泵浦过程。

Conclusion: Thouless泵浦无需连续慢变控制，离散采样哈密顿量即可实现，拓展了拓扑泵浦的实现方式。

Abstract: Thouless pumping is a celebrated topological effect that enables quantized particle transport and holds promise for applications in quantum technologies. However, the standard adiabatic pumping theory dictate that the Hamiltonian must be varied continuously and slowly throughout the whole pumping process. In this work, we show that Thouless pumping can be realized by using only discrete sampling of the Hamiltonian, removing the conventional constraint of continuous and slow change of control. Specifically, we select particular parameter points of the Hamiltonian for conventional continuous quantum adiabatic evolution, and then apply the Hamiltonian at these parameter points which induces a geodesic evolution for the pseudospin with a given quasimomentum. This realizes an adiabatic evolution for the Thouless pumping according to a necessary and sufficient condition for quantum adiabatic evolution.

</details>


### [146] [Floquet Theory and Average Hamiltonian Theory Revisited: Equivalence, Convergence and Applications to NMR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17121)
*Antonia J. Bock,Matthias Ernst,Götz S. Uhrig*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Floquet-Magnus展开证明Floquet理论与平均哈密顿理论在数学上等价，给出二者的前四阶表达式，并基于数值鲁棒性推荐采用Floquet-Van Vleck方法（含有效哈密顿量与kick算符）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期驱动量子系统的精确理论处理对NMR等领域至关重要，通常使用平均哈密顿理论或Floquet理论来预测时间演化和谱学结果。需要详细分析两者的等价性，以加深对NMR实验的理论理解。

Method: 以Floquet-Magnus展开为关键工具，证明Floquet理论与平均哈密顿理论的数学等价性；提出一种避免显式积分、不易产生代数错误的计算方案，并据此推导两种理论的前四阶结果，应用于Bloch-Siegert位移和魔角旋转下的偶极耦合自旋体系。

Result: 推荐使用同时包含有效哈密顿量和kick算符的Floquet-Van Vleck方法；其长期项与非长期项的分离有利于数值鲁棒性，精度约为平均哈密顿理论的三倍，尽管两者形式上等价。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了Floquet理论与平均哈密顿理论在代数与微扰意义上的等价程度，并为固态NMR实验分析提供了理论依据与方法选择建议。

Abstract: An accurate theoretical treatment of periodically driven quantum systems is crucial for various fields in the exact sciences, for instance Nuclear Magnetic Resonance (NMR) spectroscopy. Conventionally, either average Hamiltonian theory or Floquet theory is used to predict or describe experimental outcomes, such as the time evolution or the spectra yielding the information of the sample under study. A detailed analysis of the equivalence of these two approaches with an emphasis on applications in NMR will help to improve the theoretical understanding of NMR experiments.
  In this work, we identify the Floquet-Magnus expansion as essential to prove the mathematical equivalence of Floquet theory and average Hamiltonian theory. We advocate a calculation scheme which is less prone to algebraic mistakes because explicit integration is avoided. On this basis, we provide the first four orders of both theories. We further examine their applicability to some experiments in NMR. As examples, we investigate the Bloch-Siegert shift and dipolar coupled spin systems under magic-angle spinning.
  Based on our analysis, we recommend the use of the Floquet-Van Vleck approach including both the effective Hamiltonian and the kick operator. The consistent separation of secular and non-secular contributions appears to be especially advantageous for numerical robustness. Its accuracy is about three times better than the one provided by average Hamiltonian theory despite their formal equivalence.
  Our findings provide important insights into the theoretical background of Floquet theory and average Hamiltonian theory. This includes the extent of their algebraic and perturbative equivalence, with an emphasis on how these findings are of relevance in the analysis of solid-state NMR experiments.

</details>


### [147] [Phase-stable voltage bias for Josephson photonics devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17165)
*Amir Hosein Esmaeili,Naveen Nehra,Alexandre Paquette,Mona Arabmohammadi,Baptiste Monge,Alexandre Rogalle,Francois Cyrenne-Bergeron,Yannick Lapointe,Nicolas Lavoie,Nicolas Bourlet,Max Hofheinz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作通过集成约瑟夫森电压标准(JVS)建立耐噪声的相位参考，并将其相干转移到ICTA放大器，从而克服了直流偏置约瑟夫森光子学的相位噪声问题，实现14 dB增益提升、量子极限噪声性能，并首次在直流偏置放大器中观测到相位敏感增益与压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统微波驱动泵浦会引入寄生克尔非线性与高次谐波，限制超导量子器件性能；约瑟夫森光子学(JP)通过直流偏置结避免了这些寄生效应，但长期受限于高相位噪声以及缺乏稳定相位参考。因此，本文旨在为JP架构建立耐噪声的相位参考，并实现相位相干驱动。

Method: 将约瑟夫森电压标准(JVS)集成为耐噪声相位参考，利用超导序参量将该参考相干转移到非弹性库珀对隧穿放大器(ICTA)中，构建低相位噪声、无克尔非线性的参量放大架构。

Result: 该架构在平均增益上获得14 dB提升，并具有更优的量子极限噪声性能；关键的是，相位参考首次实现了在直流偏置放大器中观测到相位敏感增益与压缩现象。

Conclusion: 通过将洁净、无克尔的非线性与相位相干驱动相结合，该架构为超导电路中的高纯度参量过程建立了稳健平台。

Abstract: Parametric interactions are foundational to superconducting quantum technologies, yet conventional microwave-driven pumping introduces parasitic Kerr nonlinearities and higher-order harmonics that limit device performance. Josephson Photonics (JP) avoids these parasitics by utilizing dc-biased junctions but has remained constrained by high phase noise and the absence of a stable phase reference. Here, we overcome this limitation by integrating a Josephson voltage standard (JVS) to establish a noise-tolerant phase reference, and demonstrate that this reference is coherently transferred to an inelastic Cooper-pair tunneling amplifier (ICTA) via the superconducting order parameter.The resulting low-phase-noise architecture yields a 14-dB enhancement in averaged gain and better quantum-limited noise performance. Critically, the phase reference enables the first observation of phase-sensitive gain and sqeezing in a dc-biased amplifier. By reconciling clean, Kerr-free nonlinearities with phase-coherent drive, our architecture establishes a robust platform for high-purity parametric processes in superconducting circuits.

</details>


### [148] [Real-Space Renormalization of Stabilizer Rényi Entropies in Spin Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17188)
*Sonja Gombar,Petar Mali,Slobodan Radošević,Milica Rutonjski,Milan Pantić,Milica Pavkov-Hrvojević*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过计算自旋哈密顿量基态的稳定子Rényi熵，利用实空间重整化群方法研究了量子非稳定子性（量子魔力）在低能长程区域的行为，并给出闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子态虽可由Clifford电路高效经典模拟，无法单独提供量子计算优势，因此需要非稳定子资源（量子魔力）才能实现量子加速。本文旨在探究特定自旋哈密顿量中出现的量子态的非稳定子性，以理解量子魔力的产生与演化。

Method: 通过计算稳定子Rényi熵来量化量子态的非稳定子性；采用实空间重整化群技术，在低能、大距离极限下推导出闭式表达式，并分析量子魔力在粗粒化过程中的演化，以及在不同参数区域和量子相中的行为。

Result: 获得了低能、大距离区域有效的稳定子Rényi熵闭式表达式，揭示了量子魔力在粗粒化下的演化规律，并刻画了其在不同参数区域和量子相中的行为特征。

Conclusion: 自旋哈密顿量基态中的量子魔力可通过稳定子Rényi熵与重整化群方法进行定量刻画，其行为随粗粒化和量子相变化而呈现规律性，为理解量子计算优势的资源来源提供了新视角。

Abstract: Stabilizer states constitute an important class of quantum states that can be generated from computational-basis states using Pauli operators and Clifford gates. Although they may exhibit substantial multipartite entanglement, quantum circuits restricted to stabilizer operations can be efficiently simulated classically and therefore cannot, by themselves, provide a quantum computational advantage. Such an advantage requires non-stabilizer resources, commonly referred to as quantum magic. In this paper, we investigate the non-stabilizerness of quantum states arising in a class of spin Hamiltonians by computing their stabilizer Rényi entropies. Using real-space renormalization-group techniques, we obtain a closed-form expression valid in the low-energy, large-distance regime. We analyze how quantum magic evolves under coarse-graining and explore its behavior across different parameter regimes and quantum phases.

</details>


### [149] [Geometric quantifier of the incompatibility of single-particle property attribution in indistinguishable boson systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17256)
*P. Céspedes,A. Valdés-Hernández,F. H. Holik,A. P. Majtey*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Majorana恒星表示，提出一种几何量来刻画N个全同玻色子纯态中同时属性归属的不相容性，并将其从量子比特推广到量子dit。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全同粒子系统中的纠缠可通过"无法明确将一整套物理属性归属于各个组成粒子"来刻画，本文旨在为这种同时属性归属的不相容性建立几何量化工具，并揭示Majorana表示几何与纠缠判据之间的联系。

Method: 利用Majorana恒星表示，将任意对称多量子比特态写成组成单体态的对称化形式，从而把单体属性与对应Majorana星直接关联；据此在Bloch球上建立Majorana表示几何与基于单体属性归属的纠缠判据之间的直接联系；随后将该几何方法推广到高维玻色子（qudit），以Fubini-Study角来保持属性归属不相容性的几何解释。

Result: 建立了Majorana表示在Bloch球上的几何与全同二能级玻色子系统基于单体属性归属的纠缠判据之间的直接联系，并成功把该几何量从qubit推广到qudit，同时保留以Fubini-Study角表达的属性归属不相容性几何解释。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个基于Majorana恒星表示几何的、可推广至高维全同玻色子系统的纠缠量化框架，将属性归属不相容性与Fubini-Study几何联系起来。

Abstract: Entanglement in indistinguishable particle systems can be characterized by the impossibility of unambiguously attributing a complete set of physical properties to the individual constituent particles. In this work, we introduce a geometric quantifier of the incompatibility of such simultaneous property attribution for pure states of $N$ indistinguishable bosons. Using the Majorana stellar representation, any symmetric multi-qubit state can be expressed as the symmetrization of constituent single-particle states, allowing the associated single-particle properties to be directly linked with the corresponding Majorana stars. This establishes a direct connection between the geometry of the Majorana representation on the Bloch sphere and the entanglement criterion based on the attribution of single-particle properties in systems of identical two-level bosons. We then generalize this geometric approach to higher-dimensional bosons, extending the quantifier from qubits to qudits, while retaining a geometric interpretation of property-attribution incompatibility in terms of Fubini-Study angles.

</details>


### [150] [ConteXtuAlity: an open source Python package for contextuality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17294)
*Vallée Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍一个开源Python软件包ConteXtuAlity，用于计算与contextuality（上下文性）相关的各类量，提供基于层论框架的接口、测量场景与经验模型的实现，以及计算contextual fraction和signalling fraction的线性规划。文中通过示例展示其功能并给出性能基准，面向理论专家与新手，强调灵活性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: contextuality是量子基础与量子信息中的核心概念，涉及多种定量刻画（如contextual fraction、signalling fraction），但缺少统一、易用且可扩展的计算工具。作者希望提供一个面向专家与新手的开源工具，降低计算门槛并支持扩展。

Method: 作者开发开源Python包ConteXtuAlity：其核心是提供sheaf-theoretic（层论）框架的接口，实现measurement scenarios与empirical models，并利用线性规划（linear programs）计算contextual fraction与signalling fraction；通过示例演示用法，并通过benchmark评估随测量场景规模变化的性能表现。

Result: 该包实现了上述功能并可计算contextual fraction和signalling fraction；文中给出使用示例与benchmark结果，展示性能随measurement scenario规模扩展的变化趋势。

Conclusion: ConteXtuAlity是一个开源、灵活且可扩展的Python工具包，为contextuality相关计算提供统一接口，适用于经验丰富的理论研究者与领域新手。

Abstract: We present an open source Python package, ConteXtuAlity, for computing various quantities related to contextuality. This package features an interface to the sheaf-theoretic framework for contextuality, providing an implementation of measurement scenarios and empirical models as well as linear programs to compute the contextual fraction and the signalling fraction. In this work, we demonstrate the package's features and give examples of its use. We also provide benchmarks, demonstrating how performances scale with the size of measurement scenarios. The package is designed for both experienced theorists and newcomers to the field of contextuality alike, with an emphasis on flexibility and extensibility.

</details>


### [151] [Forbidden Subspaces in Quantum State Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17307)
*Chon-Fai Kam,Kai-Wen Wong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以"正性"为判据，研究了"制备—后选择"之间存在争议的量子态定义问题，指出后选择是否允许围绕对称排序的排序区间，取决于被禁止子空间是否由满秩过滤态的本征矢张成；否则即证明存在可在单量子比特上检验的上下文性，并揭示平均过滤态拓扑奇偶性对应记录的拓扑类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自1964年以来，"制备与后选择之间"的量子态缺乏公认定义，这一基础性争议长期未决，本文旨在以正性准则为该问题提供可操作的判别框架。

Method: 以正性（positivity）作为判据，分析后选择所禁止的子空间是否由满秩过滤态的本征矢张成；在单量子比特体系中构造上下文性检验；并考察平均过滤态所携带的记录纠缠谱。

Result: 后选择当且仅当被禁止子空间由满秩过滤态的本征矢张成时，才存在围绕对称排序的一个排序区间；否则即可认证上下文性，并可在单量子比特上加以检验。平均过滤态携带记录的纠缠谱，因而对称性所允许的最小禁止子空间在 Haldane 类中为偶数维、在平凡类中为奇数维。

Conclusion: 最小禁止子空间的奇偶性即为该记录的拓扑类，从而将态定义的正性判据与记录的拓扑性质建立了联系。

Abstract: A system between a preparation and a post-selection has had no agreed state since 1964. With positivity as the criterion, a post-selection admits an interval of orderings around the symmetric one exactly when the subspace it forbids is spanned by eigenvectors of a full-rank filtered state. Otherwise it certifies contextuality, testable on a qubit. The averaged filtered state carries the entanglement spectrum of the record, so the smallest forbidden subspaces a symmetry allows are even-dimensional in the Haldane class and odd in the trivial one. That parity is the record's topological class.

</details>


### [152] [QAC0 Can Prepare Every Logarithmic-Qubit State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17408)
*Lucas Gretta,Meghal Gupta,Malvika Raj Joshi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明任意 O(log n) 量子比特态都可由 poly(n) 辅助比特的 QAC⁰ 电路精确且干净地制备，将此前需要双指数规模的结果改进为指数级提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意量子态的制备通常依赖 FANOUT 或 QRAM（索引）门，而这些门并不已知属于 QAC⁰。此前在 QAC⁰ 中构造任意 n 量子比特态需要双指数规模，规模开销过大。

Method: 在 QAC⁰ 电路模型下，构造使用 poly(n) 个辅助比特的电路来精确、干净地制备任意 O(log n) 量子比特态，不借助 FANOUT 或 QRAM 门。

Result: 证明每个 O(log n) 量子比特态都可由 poly(n) 辅助比特的 QAC⁰ 电路精确且干净地制备；相比此前需双指数规模的构造，获得指数因子改进。

Conclusion: 任意 O(log n) 量子比特态可在 QAC⁰ 中以 poly(n) 辅助比特高效制备，缩小了 QAC⁰ 与含 FANOUT 门模型之间的差距。

Abstract: $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ is the class of constant-depth $\mathrm{poly}(n)$-ancilla circuits obtained by extending $\mathsf{QNC}^0$, the class of local circuits, to include nonlocal interactions via arbitrary width Toffoli gates. It is believed to be weaker than its counterpart, $\mathsf{QNC}^0_f$, obtained by including arbitrary-width FANOUT gates instead ($\mathsf{QAC}^0 \subseteq \mathsf{QNC}^0_f$ [Moo99]).
  In this note, we show that every $O(\log n)$-qubit state can be exactly and cleanly prepared by a $\mathrm{poly}(n)$-ancilla $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuit. Previous known $\mathrm{poly}(n)$-ancilla circuits for arbitrary such states are only known via additional access to either FANOUT or QRAM (indexing) gates [Ros21b, GGJ26b], neither of which are known to be in $\mathsf{QAC}^0$. Equivalently, prior constructions of arbitrary $n$-qubit states in $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ require doubly exponential size and we obtain an exponential factor improvement.

</details>


### [153] [Sharp bounds for perfect quantum state classification beyond antidistinguishability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17411)
*Nathaniel Johnston,Benjamin Lovitz,Vincent Russo,Jamie Sikora*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出k-可学习性概念，统一了量子态区分与反区分问题，并给出两个最优的Gram矩阵判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子态识别框架中，完全区分（distinguishability）与反区分（antidistinguishability）是两个极端，缺乏对部分识别问题的统一刻画。作者引入k-可学习性以填补这一空白，实现从区分到反区间的连续插值。

Method: 定义k-可学习性：存在测量策略总能将未知样本缩小到至多k个候选态。证明两个普适且最优的Gram矩阵判据——Frobenius范数充分条件与逐元素ℓ1范数必要条件，并将其应用于SIC-POVM、互不偏基、稳定子态等集合，以及零误差突变检测（异常检测、变点检测）。

Result: 给出上述量子态集合的显式可学习性与拷贝复杂度保证，并将判据成功应用于异常检测与变点检测等零误差突变检测问题。

Conclusion: k-可学习性提供了部分量子态识别的统一框架，两个最优Gram矩阵判据具有普适性，可推广至多种实际检测任务。

Abstract: A multiset of pure quantum states is said to be k-learnable if there is a measurement strategy that always narrows an unknown sample drawn from the list down to one of at most k candidates. The parameter k interpolates between distinguishability and antidistinguishability, and provides a unified framework for partial state identification. We prove two universal, and optimal, Gram-matrix criteria for k-learnability: a Frobenius-norm sufficient condition and an entrywise-$\ell_1$ necessary condition. We apply them to derive explicit learnability and copy-complexity guarantees for several well-known sets of states including SIC-POVMs, mutually unbiased bases, and stabilizer states. We further apply our results to zero-error mutation detection problems such as anomaly detection and changepoint detection.

</details>


### [154] [Evaluating Verified Autonomy in Quantum Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17439)
*Naixu Guo,Changhao Li,Siyu Cheng,Qicheng Tang,Binzhao Luo,Bikun Li,Yuxuan Du,Shihao Ru,Jiaqi Cai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出量子工程虚拟实验室 Quantum-Harbor 与包含49项专家任务的基准 QIQCBench，系统评估17个前沿智能体系统，揭示其验证性能差异巨大，并指出能力展示与可靠运行之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子平台规模与复杂度不断提升，其表征与运行需要大量人力与协调；科学AI智能体有望实现自主量子工程，但当前智能体在该场景下能否可靠工作尚无系统性的评估依据。

Method: 构建 Quantum-Harbor 虚拟实验室，提供可控执行环境，使智能体与量子系统交互，并可对智能体的操作动作与所得结论进行直接验证；在此框架上提出 QIQCBench 基准，包含49项由专家撰写、覆盖校准与控制、纠错与编译、传感与网络等多个层次的任务。

Result: 在17个前沿智能体系统上运行 QIQCBench，结果显示经验证的性能存在广泛差异。

Conclusion: 结果暴露出从"展示能力"到"实现可靠运行"之间的实质性差距，并将 Quantum-Harbor 确立为衡量量子工程中可验证自主性进展的基础平台。

Abstract: Reliable quantum engineering is essential for turning quantum phenomena into practical technologies. As quantum platforms grow in scale and complexity, their characterization and operation require increasing human effort and coordination. Scientific artificial intelligence agents, which can plan experiments, operate instruments, and analyze observations, offer a promising route towards autonomous quantum engineering. Yet whether current agents can perform reliably in this setting has not been systematically established. To fill this gap, we developed Quantum-Harbor, a virtual laboratory that provides a controlled execution environment for agents to interact with quantum systems. This design enables direct verification of both the actions taken and the conclusions drawn. Building on this framework, we introduce QIQCBench, a benchmark of $49$ expert-authored tasks spanning multiple layers including calibration and control, error correction and compilation, sensing and networking. Across $17$ frontier agentic systems, QIQCBench reveals wide variation in verified performance. These results expose a substantial gap between demonstrating capability and achieving reliable operation, and establish Quantum-Harbor as a foundation for measuring progress towards verified autonomy in quantum engineering.

</details>


### [155] [Quantum Compiler Design for Fault-Tolerant Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17465)
*Chenghong Zhu,Jiahan Chen,Keming He,Hongshun Yao,Zhaohui Yang,Jin-Guo Liu,Anbang Wu,Xiaotong Ni,Xingsheng Luan,Zhuo Fu,Shenggen Zheng,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述面向量子纠错（QEC）保护的容错量子计算（FTQC）编译器设计，提出逻辑层QEC编译、物理层QEC实现与解码器运行时集成三层全栈框架，并梳理开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规模化量子计算依赖容错量子计算，QEC需抑制物理错误以支撑可靠逻辑操作。这要求量子编译超越通用电路优化，转向编码感知、协议结构化的全栈编译。现有工作分散，缺乏面向FTQC编译器的系统性全栈视角。

Method: 采用综述方法，将已有工作组织为三个相互作用的层次：1）逻辑层QEC编译：表面码晶格手术编译器、超越表面码的码手术框架（含新兴qLDPC方法）、非Clifford操作编译支持（魔态蒸馏、码切换）；2）物理层QEC实现：超导、囚禁离子、中性原子平台的硬件感知QEC实现；3）解码器运行时集成：解码模型、实时解码系统与帧管理机制以闭合容错执行反馈回路。并维护在线论文列表。

Result: 系统梳理了三层的代表性工作：逻辑层的表面码/码手术/qLDPC编译与非Clifford操作支持；物理层的多平台硬件感知实现；解码器层的模型、实时系统与帧管理。并明确了跨层优化、qLDPC编译、编译器-解码器协同设计、运行时自适应、集成可基准测试的FTQC编译栈等开放挑战。

Conclusion: FTQC编译需全栈协同，从算法级操作降低到逻辑门集、协调编码数据与辅助资源、结合重复症候提取、并向实时解码输送测量流。未来方向聚焦跨层优化、qLDPC编译、编译器-解码器协同设计、运行时自适应及可基准化集成编译栈的建设。

Abstract: Scalable quantum computation is expected to rely on fault-tolerant quantum computation (FTQC), in which quantum error correction (QEC) suppresses physical errors sufficiently to support reliable logical operations. This requires quantum compilation to move beyond general-purpose circuit optimization toward encoding-aware and protocol-structured compilation across the full stack of fault-tolerant quantum computers. Beyond circuit synthesis and hardware mapping, an FTQC compiler must lower algorithm-level operations into the logical gate set supported by the chosen code, coordinate encoded data and ancilla resources, realize logical operations together with repeated syndrome extraction under hardware constraints, and provide the resulting measurement stream to real-time decoding.
  This survey presents a full-stack view of compiler design for QEC-protected quantum computation. We organize existing work into three interacting layers: logical-level QEC compilation, physical-level QEC realization, and decoder runtime integration. At the logical level, we review surface-code lattice-surgery compilers, beyond-surface-code code-surgery frameworks including emerging qLDPC approaches, and compilation support for non-Clifford operations such as magic-state distillation and code switching. At the physical level, we survey hardware-aware QEC realization on superconducting, trapped-ion, and neutral-atom platforms. We further examine decoder models, real-time decoding systems, and frame-management mechanisms that close the feedback loop during fault-tolerant execution. Finally, we identify open challenges in cross-layer optimization, qLDPC compilation, compiler-decoder co-design, runtime adaptivity, and the development of integrated and benchmarkable FTQC compilation stacks. An actively maintained paper list is available at: github.com/chenghongz/QEC-compiler-design.

</details>


### [156] [Predictive Structure Behind Rare Outcomes in Random Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17482)
*Myeongsu Kim,Travis Humble,Sabre Kais*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 稀有输出不仅提供终端统计，还揭示了可跨新动力学、可指导电路构造的中间物理组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上把随机量子电路的稀有输出视为终端统计，作者质疑其是否包含仍然有用的中间组织信息，并希望在"未来选择"之外检验这种信息能否迁移到新动力学中。

Method: 比较同一局部生成器下的无条件下随机构型（RN）与峰值选择后构型（RP，n=8-16）；使用精确全后缀分解分析峰值指向干涉；在 n=8-14 的四个标称深度上检验结构分离与"新鲜未来"优势，并在固定独立校准阈值下检验深度扩展；构建不依赖被评估电路终端结果的结构前缀评分；采用保持布居数的相位扰乱、RP 轨迹引导初始化，以及中间多变量引导。

Result: RP 表现出更强成对相关与概率重分布，但双分和输出扩散减弱；全后缀分解显示其峰值指向干涉更强；结构分离与新鲜未来优势在四个深度上持续存在，深度扩展中 RP 富集达 2.01–7.15 倍；结构前缀评分可预测新鲜事件概率；保持布居数的相位扰乱表明相对相位组织有功能性贡献（含主深度留出确认）；RP 轨迹引导初化在局部细化下优于 Haar 初化的高蜂值产率；中间多变量引导提升构造态的新鲜延续敏感性。

Conclusion: 稀有输出蕴含中间物理组织，该组织在新动力学下仍然有用，并能反向指导电路构造与初始化，超越仅依赖峰值统计的视角。

Abstract: Rare outputs of random quantum circuits are usually treated as terminal statistics. We ask whether they reveal intermediate organization that remains useful beyond the selecting future. Comparing unconditioned random-normal (RN) and peak-selected random-peaked (RP) circuits from the same local generator, we find stronger pairwise correlations and probability redistribution but reduced bipartite and output spreading in RP across n=8-16. Exact full-suffix decomposition reveals stronger peak-directed interference. The structural separation and fresh-future advantage persist across four nominal depths at n=8-14, with 2.01-7.15 fold RP enrichment in the depth extensions at fixed, independently calibrated thresholds. Within the unconditioned ensemble, a structural prefix score predicts fresh-event probability without using the evaluated circuits' terminal outcomes. Population-preserving phase scrambling identifies a functional contribution from relative-phase organization across the tested settings, including primary-depth held-out confirmation. RP trajectory-guided initialization improves high-peak yield over Haar initialization under common local refinement. Intermediate multivariate guidance also improves the fresh-continuation susceptibility of constructed states beyond peak-only guidance across the same size-depth grid. Rare outputs thus reveal intermediate physical organization that remains useful under new dynamics and can guide circuit construction.

</details>


### [157] [Query-optimal quantum simulation of Lindblad evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17490)
*Chunhao Wang,Christopher Ye*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在 block-encoding 模型下，给出 Lindblad 演化模拟的查询复杂度最优算法，实现了对演化时间 t 和精度 ε 的加性依赖 Ω(t + polylog(1/ε))，闭合了此前算法为乘性依赖 O(t·polylog(1/ε)) 的查询复杂度差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lindblad 演化模拟中，哈密顿量模拟给出加性查询下界 Ω(t + polylog(1/ε))，而此前一般 Lindblad 模拟算法只能达到乘性上界 O(t·polylog(1/ε))。乘性依赖是否必要长期悬而未决，本文旨在闭合该查询复杂度差距。

Method: 采用 transducer 框架，降低组合演化信道一阶近似时的查询开销；并结合不同长度复用电路的线性组合，抑制 catalyst-removal 误差。

Result: 在 block-encoding 模型下，得到查询复杂度对演化时间和精度均为最优加性依赖的 Lindblad 模拟算法；其额外门复杂度高于现有算法。

Conclusion: 该算法解决了 oracle 访问本质必要量的问题，确立了最优查询复杂度；同时指出现有算法在达到最优门复杂度方面仍存在待解决的挑战。

Abstract: For the problem of simulating Lindblad evolution for time $t$ to precision $ε$, Hamiltonian simulation provides an additive query lower bound, informally, $Ω(t + \mathrm{polylog}(1/ε))$. However, the best previously known algorithms for general Lindblad simulation achieve a multiplicative upper bound, informally, $\mathcal{O}(t\,\mathrm{polylog}(1/ε))$, in gate complexity. It has remained open whether this multiplicative dependence is necessary. In this paper, we close the gap in query complexity by giving an algorithm with optimal additive dependence on evolution time and precision in the block-encoding model. Our approach uses the transducer framework to reduce the query cost of composing first-order approximations to the evolution channel, together with linear combinations of reuse circuits of different lengths to suppress catalyst-removal error.
  Although our additional gate complexity is higher than that of existing algorithms, our optimal query complexity resolves the question of how much oracle access is fundamentally necessary and identifies the remaining challenge to achieve the optimal gate complexity.

</details>


### [158] [Beyond Hardware: Adaptive Algorithmic Control by State-Proxy Equalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17497)
*Jianlong Lu,Hongrui Zhang,Vishal Sharathchandra Bajpe,Thorsten Koch,Ying Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出自适应算法控制（A2C）软件范式，基于状态代理均衡定理，通过均衡累积计算硬度而非物理时间来分配量子计算资源，在最多156量子比特的优化问题上显著提升低能采样概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子计算的进展主要依赖硬件改进。本文旨在证明，即使硬件条件不变，通过优化有限计算资源在量子计算过程中的分配方式，也能带来显著性能提升。

Method: 提出A2C（Adaptive Algorithmic Control）软件范式，其理论基础为"状态代理均衡定理"（State-Proxy Equalization theorem），该定理证明对于状态导出的代理误差泛函，最优分配应均衡累积计算硬度而非物理时间。所需计算硬度直接从演化的量子态中推断，无需显式重建指数级大的多体谱。实验结合精确模拟、大规模超级计算机计算与IBM量子硬件，覆盖最多156量子比特的量子优化问题。

Result: 在匹配的电路深度和测量预算下，A2C将低能采样概率提升22%至超过100,000%。

Conclusion: 量子计算性能不仅取决于硬件能力，还取决于有限计算资源的组织方式；自适应算法控制为推进量子计算提供了一条互补的软件路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in quantum computing have been driven primarily by improvements in hardware. Here we show that substantial gains can instead arise from how finite computational resources are allocated throughout a quantum computation. We introduce Adaptive Algorithmic Control (A2C), a software paradigm founded on a State-Proxy Equalization theorem, which proves that the optimal allocation for a state-derived proxy-error functional equalizes cumulative computational hardness rather than physical time. The required computational hardness is inferred directly from the evolving quantum state, avoiding explicit reconstruction of the exponentially large many-body spectrum. Across quantum optimization problems containing up to 156 qubits, combining exact simulations, large-scale supercomputer computations and IBM quantum hardware experiments, A2C improves the low-energy sampling probabilities by $22\%$ to over $100,000\%$ under matched circuit depths and measurement budgets. These results demonstrate that quantum computational performance depends not only on hardware capabilities, but also on how finite computational resources are organized, establishing adaptive algorithmic control as a complementary software pathway for advancing quantum computation.

</details>


### [159] [Antidistinguishability of states in General Probabilistic Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17498)
*Satyaki Manna,Anandamay Das Bhowmik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在广义概率理论（GPTs）框架下研究态的反可区分性，提出反可区分性、强反可区分性与等反可区分性三种精化概念，给出等反可区分集基数与态空间仿射维数之间的上界，并在多边形理论中刻画反可区分态集，借助随机排除码探测多边形理论的非经典性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反可区分性是量子信息中刻画"无法可靠区分"这一任务的重要性质，但其在GPTs这一更一般框架下的结构尚不清晰；作者希望厘清不同强度的反可区分概念之间的关系，并利用其作为探针比较多边形理论与量子理论的能力边界。

Method: 在GPTs框架中给出反可区分性、强反可区分性与等反可区分性的形式化定义，强调对测量效应施加的附加约束；建立三者之间的一般性关系并推导等反可区分集基数的上界；针对多边形理论分析态集反可区分的条件；构造随机排除码，以其成功概率由编码态集的反可区分性决定，用于量化非经典性。

Result: 得到等反可区分集基数受态空间仿射维数约束的上界；给出多边形理论中态集反可区分的判据，证明多边形模型中全体纯态构成反可区分集，并识别出若干强反可区分与等反可区分的广泛态族；随机排除码显示部分多边形模型可超越最优量子值，而在大多边形极限下其最优性能收敛于量子极限。

Conclusion: 研究表明反可区分性可作为GPTs中区分经典与量子行为的有效工具，多边形理论在排除码任务中展现出超越量子的能力且在大尺寸极限回归量子结果，为理解超越量子关联提供了新的操作性视角。

Abstract: We investigate antidistinguishability of states within the framework of general probabilistic theories (GPTs). We formulate antidistinguishability, strong and equal antidistinguishability as refined notions that imposed additional constraint on the measurement effects. We establish general results relating these notions of antidistinguishability and derive an upper bound on the cardinality of equally antidistinguishable sets in terms of the affine dimension of the state space. We then study antidistinguishability in polygonal theories, obtaining conditions for antidistinguishability of a set of states. In consequence, we show that the set of all pure states in a polygon model is antidistinguishable. Additionally, we identify broad families of strongly and equally antidistinguishable states. Finally, using Random Exclusion Codes, whose success probability is governed by the antidistinguishability of different sets of encoding states, we probe the nonclassicality of polygon theories. We find that certain polygon models can outperform the optimal quantum value, while their optimal performance converges to the quantum limit in the large-polygon limit.

</details>


### [160] [A Case Study on Noise Resilient Operator Selection in Adaptive Variational Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17501)
*Soorya Haravu,Mafalda Ramôa,Bharath Sambasivam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作研究了硬件噪声对ADAPT-VQE中算子选择步骤的影响，并以线性H₃分子为测试案例，证明通过组合量子误差缓解技术可恢复收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注噪声对ADAPT-VQE参数优化步骤的影响，而对其关键的算子选择步骤受噪声影响的研究相对缺乏。

Method: 考察多种噪声信道对算子选择步骤的影响，并采用动力学解耦、零噪声外推和Pauli twirling等量子误差缓解技术及其组合。

Result: 选择准则对部分噪声具有天然鲁棒性，但相干与不相干噪声在足够高的噪声率下均可导致收敛失败；合理组合误差缓解技术后可恢复成功的收敛曲线。

Conclusion: 误差缓解能提升ADAPT-VQE性能并在硬件噪声下实现收敛，为近中期量子硬件上的算法实现提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: Hardware noise has been shown to significantly impact the accuracy of ADAPT-VQE, a ground state preparation algorithm. While previous work has studied the impact of noise on its parameter optimization step, its impact on the critical operator selection step remains comparatively unexplored. In this work, we examine the impact of a variety of noise channels on this step, using a linear H$_3$ molecule as a test case. We show that, despite the selection criterion's natural resilience to some noise, both coherent and incoherent noise can prevent convergence for sufficiently high noise rates. We employ quantum error mitigation techniques--dynamical decoupling, zero noise extrapolation, and Pauli twirling--and show that when combined appropriately, these techniques are capable of restoring a successful convergence profile. Our results highlight how error mitigation can improve the performance of ADAPT-VQE and enable convergence in the presence of hardware noise, offering valuable insights into the implementation of the algorithm on near-term quantum hardware.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Inverse Reconstruction of Causal Nonlinear Electrodynamics: Exact Functional Families and Single-Horizon Black-Hole Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16016)
*Ariel Guzmán,Mohsen Fathi,J. R. Villanueva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将非线性电动力学表述为构成性逆问题，在仿射可分约化下构造了由有界对数指标编码的无限维因果类，并分析其谱表示、磁因果性与引力耦合性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法先选取拉格朗日量再检验因果性与可积性，缺乏系统性；作者希望反转这一逻辑，将可积性、麦克斯韦极限与因果传播作为先验约束，再反推出拉格朗日量的可能类别。

Method: 在双不变量问题的仿射可分约化框架下，将非线性电动力学作为构成性逆问题处理；利用磁轴上的完全单调性引入正谱表示，分析自对偶性、双折射、单极子自能与磁因果性条件，并将重构物质耦合至爱因斯坦引力。

Result: 得到由有界对数指标编码的无限维因果类，一般非自对偶，Born-Infeld为其唯一自对偶成员，并含已知幂律线之外的闭式双折射理论。有限单极子自能等价于$-1/4$阶谱矩；全局磁因果性迫使谱支撑在原点上累积，而有限自能排除该处原子，从而排除有限原子测度。对广义伽马谱，条件选出$1/4<γ\leq 1/2$窗口。引力耦合给出精确几何与至多一个正视界的单调性判据。两个光学度规以闭式给出且因果锥相交，确立对称双曲性。

Conclusion: 该工作以逆问题视角系统刻画了满足因果性与可积性的非线性电动力学大类别，揭示了谱条件与物理约束的等价关系，并给出可自然嵌入爱因斯坦引力的精确解，为构造性理论提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We formulate nonlinear electrodynamics as a constitutive inverse problem: integrability, the Maxwell limit, and causal propagation are imposed before a Lagrangian is chosen. Within an affine-separable reduction of the two-invariant problem, we obtain an infinite-dimensional causal class whose functional freedom is encoded by a bounded logarithmic index. The class is generically non-self-dual; Born-Infeld is its unique self-dual member, and it contains closed-form birefringent theories beyond the known power-law line. On the magnetic axis, complete monotonicity yields a positive spectral representation. Finite monopole self-energy is equivalent to a spectral moment of order $-1/4$. Global magnetic causality forces the spectral support to accumulate at the origin, while finite self-energy excludes an atom there; together these conditions rule out finite atomic measures. For generalized-gamma spectra, global magnetic causality and finite self-energy select the exact window $1/4<γ\leq 1/2$ for every shape parameter. We further determine when these magnetic laws admit a causal two-invariant completion and give an explicit spectral realization of a non-power member of the full class. Coupling the reconstructed matter to Einstein gravity produces exact geometries and a monotonicity criterion that allows at most one positive horizon. Finally, the two optical metrics of the full class are obtained in closed form and shown to possess intersecting causal cones, establishing symmetric hyperbolicity under the stated regularity assumptions.

</details>


### [162] [Joint Constraints on Quantum Oppenheimer--Snyder Black Holes from High-Frequency Quasi-Periodic Oscillations in X-ray Binary Systems and the S2 Star Orbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16018)
*A. Errehymy,Y. Khedif,M. Daoud,B. Turimov,S. Usanov,F. Turaev,Z. Yasakov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究量子修正的Oppenheimer-Snyder时空，引入单一有效参数α描述小量子引力效应；给出黑洞形成最小质量、ISCO与QPO频率修正，并用四个X射线双星拟合得到α=0.22±0.10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典Oppenheimer-Snyder坍缩基础上引入现象学量子引力修正，考察强场区可观测效应，并借助黑洞形成质量下限及轨道/QPO观测对有效参数α进行限制。

Method: 采用含有效参数α的量子修正OS时空，修正项正比于αM²/r⁴；计算视界形成的最小质量、ISCO半径与径向/垂直epicyclic频率；结合GRS 1915+105、XTE J1550-564、XTE J1859+226、GRO J1655-40四个X射线双星的QPO观测进行拟合，并检验弱场约束（S2、水星）。

Result: 出现视界形成最小质量M_min~(0.5-1.2)M_⊙（α≈0.22），对应裸LQG参数下M_min~M_Pl~10⁻⁵ g；ISCO移至r_ISCO=6M-α/(12M)，对恒星级黑洞修正~10⁻⁴、对Sgr A*~10⁻¹⁰；径向epicyclic频率在r~5M附近改变约5%-10%，垂直模影响较小；双星拟合得α=0.22±0.10，质量5.4-12.4M_⊙，发射半径r/M~5.5-8.6，QPO频率100-450 Hz被较好重现；弱场给出f_sp=1.10±0.19。

Conclusion: 该量子修正OS模型在保持弱场物理近乎不变的同时，对强场区域产生可观测的结构性修正，可自洽解释X射线双星QPO观测，并与理论预期及弱场检验相容。

Abstract: We study a quantum-corrected version of the Oppenheimer--Snyder (OS) spacetime in which classical collapse is modified by a single effective-parameter \(α\), representing small-quantum-gravitational effects in a phenomenologically rescaled-form. Although the correction enters through a term proportional to \(αM^2/r^4\), it produces noticeable changes in the strong-field region while leaving weak-field physics almost unchanged. One of the main results is the appearance of a minimum-mass for horizon-formation, \(M_{\min} \sim(0.5-1.2)\,M_\odot\) for the effective-parameter \(α\approx0.22\). We emphasize that this \(M_{\min}\) is expressed in terms of the effective-parameter \(α\); in terms of the bare loop-quantum-gravity (LQG) parameter, \(M_{\min}\sim M_{\text{Pl}}\sim10^{-5}\)~g, consistent with the theoretical-expectation. Orbital properties are also slightly modified, with the innermost-stable-circular-orbit (ISCO) shifting to \(r_{\rm ISCO}=6M-α/(12M)\), giving corrections-of-order \( \sim 10^{-4} \) for stellar-black-holes and \(\sim 10^{-10}\) for Sgr A$^\ast$. In the same region, radial epicyclic frequencies change by about \(5\%-10\%\) near \(r\sim5M\), while vertical modes are less affected, leading to small but structured shifts in quasi-periodic oscillation (QPO) behavior. Using four X-ray binaries (GRS 1915+105, XTE J1550-564, XTE J1859+226, GRO J1655-40), we find a consistent range \(α=0.22\pm0.10\), with masses between \(5.4-12.4\,M_\odot\) and emission radii \(r/M \sim 5.5 - 8.6\). The observed QPO frequencies, lying in the range \(100-450\) Hz, are well reproduced within this framework. Weak-field tests such as S2 and Mercury still allow \(f_{\rm sp}=1.10 \pm 0.19\), leaving room for these small strong-gravity corrections.

</details>


### [163] [The third law of black hole thermodynamics: Quantum versus classical considerations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16127)
*Shahar Hod,Tsvi Piran*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出，在带电标量场塌缩形成极端黑洞的问题中，量子真空极化效应可保证 $|eQ|\geq\hbar/2$ 时第三定律成立，并猜想经典禁戒区域可强化至 $|eQ|<\hbar/2$，从而弥补量子效应不足的区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kehle和Unger证明无质量带电经典标量场可在有限时间内形成极端（零温）黑洞，这似乎违反热力学第三定律。但第三定律本质上是量子原理，因此必须考虑量子效应才能自洽地判断其是否真正被违反。

Method: 通过分析系统电场对真空的自发极化，指出其会产生无质量带电粒子对并使黑洞放电；同时结合Schneider关于小电荷区间的经典界 $|eQ|\leq\hbar/3$，对经典形成条件进行论证与推广。

Result: 真空极化产生的带电粒子对会通过放电机制在 $|eQ|\geq\hbar/2$ 区间保证第三定律的量子有效性；而在 $|eQ|<\hbar/2$ 区间，量子极化效应过弱，不足以维持第三定律。

Conclusion: 作者猜想Schneider的经典界可以加强为：在 $|eQ|<\hbar/2$ 区间，经典考虑本身即可阻止极端带电Reissner-Nordström黑洞动态形成，从而完整地维护热力学第三定律。

Abstract: Kehle and Unger have recently shown that, under suitable conditions, a massless charged classical scalar field can produce an extremal black hole in finite time. This result appears to violate the third law of thermodynamics (TLT), since extremal black holes have zero temperature. However, because the TLT is intrinsically a quantum principle, a self-consistent analysis of this problem must account for quantum effects. In particular, we point out that the electric field of the system may spontaneously polarize the vacuum, thereby creating massless charged pairs that will discharge the black hole. This mechanism guarantees the {\it quantum} validity of the TLT in the regime $|eQ|\geq\hbar/2$ (here ${Q,e}$ denote, respectively, the electric charges of the black hole and the scalar field). This leaves open the question of what happens for lower values of $|eQ|/\hbar$. Recently, Schneider derived a classical bound showing that extremal black holes cannot be formed à la Kehle-Unger in the small-charge regime $|eQ|\leq\hbar/3$. His analysis leaves open the possibility that a stronger bound may exist. We conjecture here that this bound can indeed be strengthened such that classical considerations would prevent extremal (zero-temperature) charged Reissner-Nordström black holes from forming dynamically in the regime $|eQ|<\hbar/2$, where quantum polarization effects are too weak to preserve the validity of the TLT.

</details>


### [164] [Analog Gravity in Magneto-Viscous Fluids: Enhanced Analog Hawking Temperature in Accretion Disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16135)
*Aliv Sahoo,Mayank Pathak,Banibrata Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了磁流体力学(MHD)流动中的三维黏性磁声学类比引力框架，证明在程函极限与Shakura-Sunyaev α黏性下快磁声模保留有效时空几何，并利用吸积盘生成黏性磁声学视界，揭示类比Hawking温度对磁场拓扑高度敏感且随黏性参数单调增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流体耗散通常会破坏声学度规的洛伦兹签名，从而阻碍在真实耗散流体中建立类比引力描述；同时自相似ADIOS模型因Mach数恒定而无法形成视界，需寻找可行途径在含耗散的MHD流动中构造并研究类比视界及其热力学。

Method: 建立三维黏性磁声学类比引力框架；在程函极限下结合Shakura-Sunyaev α黏性处方评估波扰动；以天体物理吸积盘为背景，数值模拟旋转黑洞周围的磁化平流吸积流，并采用解析的ADIOS解；引入破坏自相似性的磁场扰动以产生动态Mach数，进而形成黏性磁声学视界；通过计算视界处的自发声子发射得到类比Hawking温度。

Result: 快磁声模可获得非退化有效度规，而慢磁声模与Alfvén模不具非退化度规；引入磁场扰动后突破自相似ADIOS的恒定Mach数限制，成功形成动态Mach数及黏性磁声学视界；类比Hawking温度对磁场拓扑高度敏感，背景磁场梯度的空间取向决定Hawking辐射被放大或抑制；增大黏性参数导致类比Hawking温度单调升高。

Conclusion: 含耗散的MHD流动中仍可构建良定义的类比引力模型，吸积盘可作为研究类比视界热力学的有效背景；磁场拓扑与黏性强度是调控类比Hawking辐射的关键参数，为耗散体系中的类比引力研究提供了新途径。

Abstract: We present a three-dimensional visco-magnetoacoustic framework for analog gravity in magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) flows. While standard fluid dissipation typically breaks the Lorentzian signature of acoustic metrics, we demonstrate that evaluating wave perturbations in the eikonal limit alongside the Shakura-Sunyaev $α$-viscosity prescription preserves a well-defined effective spacetime geometry for the fast magnetoacoustic mode. The slow-magnetoacoustic mode and Alfvén mode do not admit a non-degenerate metric. To investigate analog horizon thermodynamics, we utilize astrophysical accretion disks as background media, specifically modeling numerical magnetized advective accretion flows around rotating black holes and analytical advection-dominated inflow-outflow solutions (ADIOS). Standard self-similar ADIOS models strictly enforce a constant Mach number, precluding horizon formation. We therefore introduce a magnetic field perturbation that breaks self-similarity, generates a dynamic Mach number, and enables the formation of a visco-magnetoacoustic horizon. By evaluating the spontaneous phonon emission at these horizons, we reveal that the analog Hawking temperature is highly sensitive to the magnetic field topology; the spatial orientation of the background magnetic gradients dictates whether the Hawking radiation is amplified or suppressed. Furthermore, we find that increasing the viscosity parameter leads to a monotonic increase in the analog Hawking temperature.

</details>


### [165] [Bridging the chirp-mass gap: searching for gravitational waves from inspiraling ultra-compact binaries using LIGO O4ab data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16238)
*Andrew L. Miller*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究利用BinaryGFH-v2时频搜索方法，在LIGO O4ab数据中搜索啁啾质量为10^-2–10^-1 M_⊙的超致密双星旋进，填补了行星质量区与常规双星搜索之间的空白，未发现显著候选体，并对双星并合率密度及原初黑洞暗物质占比给出首批引力波约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚太阳质量致密双星搜索存在参数空间空白：长时搜索覆盖行星质量区，而常规致密双星分析仅探测较高质量区，啁啾质量10^-2–10^-1 M_⊙区间此前缺乏引力波约束。

Method: 采用统计稳健的BinaryGFH-v2时频技术，搜索持续数分钟至数小时的快速演化旋进信号，对LIGO O4ab数据进行超致密双星搜索，计算成本约350 CPU小时。

Result: 未发现显著候选体，给出90%置信度的双星并合率密度上限，并在此啁啾质量区间首次获得引力波约束；在无双星抑制（f_sup=1）假设下，对等质量双星（组分质量10^-2–10^-1 M_⊙）及非对称双星（主质量2.5 M_⊙、次质量3×10^-4–10^-2 M_⊙）给出f_PBH<1的原初黑洞暗物质占比上限。

Conclusion: BinaryGFH-v2以较低计算成本实现了该挑战性参数空间的搜索，首次在此啁啾质量范围给出引力波并合率上限与原初黑洞暗物质占比约束，为亚太阳质量致密双星与原初黑洞研究提供了新的观测限制。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave searches for subsolar-mass compact binaries have left a gap between the planetary-mass regime accessible to long-duration searches and the higher masses probed by conventional compact-binary analyses. We close this gap by searching for the inspiral of ultra-compact binaries with chirp masses $10^{-2}$--$10^{-1}\,M_\odot$ in LIGO O4ab data. Using the statistically robust BinaryGFH-v2 method, a time-frequency technique to search for rapidly evolving inspirals lasting minutes to hours, we cover this previously challenging parameter space with a computational cost of only $\sim 350$ CPU-hours, and find no significant candidates. We thus set 90\% confidence upper limits on the binary merger-rate density, providing the first gravitational-wave constraints across this chirp-mass range. Assuming that primordial-black-hole binaries formed in the early Universe with no binary suppression ($f_{\rm sup}=1$), these results provide the first gravitational-wave constraints on the primordial-black-hole \dmh fraction in this mass range, yielding upper limits with $f_{\rm PBH}<1$ for equal-mass binaries with component masses $10^{-2}$--$10^{-1}\,M_\odot$ and asymmetric binaries with secondary masses $m_2=3\times10^{-4}$--$10^{-2}\,M_\odot$ for a primary mass $m_1=2.5\,M_\odot$.

</details>


### [166] [Schwarzschild black holes as low-pass filters of fuzzy dark matter granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16249)
*Flavio Rosales-Infante,Iván Álvarez-Rios,Francisco S. Guzmán*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了FDM在超大质量黑洞附近的弛豫：史瓦西时空充当低通滤波器，晚期所有谱宽初值都趋向仅由低多极（ℓ≤2）与小径向波数主导的普适态，σ仅决定电荷耗尽的时标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究具有空间颗粒性的模糊暗物质（FDM）在黑洞视界附近的动力学弛豫，理解不同初始谱结构如何被背景时空过滤。

Method: 将复质量标量场初始化为动量空间中宽带、各向异性的多模随机场（谱宽σ），用球谐分解至ℓmax=100，在适配背景空间几何的专用径向基上于有限域数值演化；追踪各多极的演化并计算其跨事件视界与外边界的Noether流。

Result: 角动量势垒抑制高ℓ模的视界吸收，高频径向成分与细角结构通过向外辐射和视界吸积耗散；无论初始谱宽如何，所有构型均收敛到几乎只由低多极（ℓ≤2）和小径波数主导的普适晚期弛豫态，初始带宽σ决定全局电荷耗尽的时标。取m_b=10^{-22}eV/c²、M_BH=6.5×10⁹M_⊙给出具体物理估计。

Conclusion: 史瓦西时空对近视界FDM场起有效低通滤波作用，晚期谱普遍低多极、低径波数化；谱宽只改变弛豫时标而非终态。

Abstract: We study the relaxation of a complex massive scalar field around a supermassive black hole, modeling the near-horizon dynamics of Fuzzy Dark Matter (FDM) with spatial granularity. The field is initialized as a broadband, anisotropic multi-mode random field in momentum space with various spectral widths $σ$, decomposed into spherical harmonics up to $\ell_{\max}=100$, and numerically evolved on a finite domain using a specialized radial basis adapted to the background spatial geometry. We track the evolution of each multipole and calculate its Noether flux across both the event horizon and the outer boundary. Since angular momentum barriers suppress the horizon absorption of high-$\ell$ modes, while high-frequency radial components and fine-scale angular structures dissipate via outward radiation and horizon accretion, the Schwarzschild spacetime acts as an effective low-pass filter. Regardless of the initial spectral width, all configurations converge toward a universal late-time relaxation state dominated almost exclusively by low multipoles ($\ell \le 2$) and small radial wavenumbers, with the initial bandwidth $σ$ determining the timescale of global charge depletion. For concrete physical estimates, we set the boson mass to $m_b=10^{-22}\,\mathrm{eV}/c^2$ and the black hole mass to $M_{\mathrm{BH}}=6.5\times10^9 M_\odot$.

</details>


### [167] [General first-order constitutive equations for a relativistic dissipative multi-component fluid in the presence of a weak background electromagnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16325)
*J. Félix Salazar,Ana Laura García-Perciante,Olivier Sarbach*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 建立了相对论性多组分等离子体在背景电磁场中的普遍本构方程，基于梯度展开推导熵产生并与Onsager倒易关系及热力学第二定律相容，重点讨论双组分混合物的交叉效应，并给出任意时空背景上的完整演化方程组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论性等离子体广泛存在于天体物理与宇宙学场景（如致密星体、早期宇宙），其组分数目任意且处于背景电磁场中，而电磁力强度与状态变量梯度同阶，可充当耗散的一阶驱动力。现有研究缺乏对多组分、带电磁场情形下系统性的相对论本构关系与输运系数约束的刻画，故需建立普遍框架。

Method: 采用梯度展开（gradient expansion），将作用在任一组分上的电磁力视为与状态变量梯度同阶的一阶耗散驱动力；假设各组分在平衡态下共享同一温度和流速，计算熵产生率，并用一阶标架不变量（frame invariant）表示本构方程；通过施加Onsager倒易关系与第二定律约束确定输运系数条件；进而导出双组分混合物在任意时空背景上的演化方程组。

Result: 给出了以熵产生为基础的一般性本构方程，明确了满足Onsager倒易关系与第二定律的输运系数条件；具体讨论了双组分混合物中可能对天体物理和宇宙学有意义的交叉效应（cross effects），并得到一套完整的、描述二元混合物在任意时空背景下动力学演化的方程组。

Conclusion: 该工作为含背景电磁场的相对论性多组分等离子体提供了自洽的耗散流体力学本构框架与演化方程，可为天体物理与宇宙学中的多元混合物建模提供理论基础。

Abstract: In this work we establish general constitutive equations for a relativistic plasma composed of an arbitrary number of classical, charged, and chemically non-reacting species in the presence of a background electromagnetic field. The intensity of the electromagnetic force acting on either species is assumed to be comparable with the gradients of the state variables and is thus considered as a first-order driving force for dissipation within the gradient expansion. Assuming that the species share the same temperature and velocity flow when in equilibrium, we determine the entropy production and establish constitutive equations in terms of first-order frame invariant quantities. Conditions on the corresponding transport coefficients are given in order to comply with Onsager's reciprocal relations and the second law of thermodynamics. In particular, we discuss cross effects for the binary mixture which may be relevant for astrophysical and cosmological applications. Finally, we derive a complete set of evolution equations governing the dynamics of a binary mixture propagating on an arbitrary spacetime background.

</details>


### [168] [Charging of rotating black holes: kinetic simulations of black hole magnetospheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16447)
*Martin Kološ,Farukh Abdulkhamidov,Arman Tursunov,Jorge A. Rueda,Benoît Cerutti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文利用含自洽对产生的轴对称GRPIC模拟，研究旋转黑洞在外磁场中的电荷饱和问题，发现电荷平衡值为动力学吸引子（ξ_eq≈0.3，高自旋趋于0），远低于Wald电荷上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转黑洞浸没于外部磁场是否充至Wald值Q_W=2aMB，是黑洞电动力学的长期开放问题，涉及电荷分离、粒子加速及黑洞磁层结构，需明确其平衡电荷的真实取值。

Method: 使用GRZeltron进行轴对称广义相对论粒子模拟（PIC），包含自洽对产生过程；对代表性自旋值，从两个相反的初始视界电荷Q_0=0与Q_0=Q_W演化相同的渐近均匀Wald磁场，并通过视界追踪累积电荷；另推导解析表达式，要求正负电荷通过视界的磁通量相等。

Result: 两种初始分支在几十个引力时标内均弛豫至相同的饱和电荷值，说明平衡是动力学吸引子而非初值记忆；吸引子远低于Q_W，当a≲0.7时ξ_eq≡Q_eq/Q_W≈0.3，大自旋a≈1时降至ξ_eq≈0；解析表达式给出的自旋依赖与模拟吻合。

Conclusion: 天体物理黑洞的电荷状态由动力学等离子体过程决定，Wald电荷是上限而非一般平衡值；由于ξ_eq<1，充电不会淬灭驱动Blandford-Znajek等能量提取机制的视界-无穷远电势差。

Abstract: Whether a rotating black hole (BH) immersed in an external magnetic field charges up to the Wald value $Q_{\rm W}=2aMB$, where $M$ and $a$ are the BH mass and spin parameter, and $B$ is the strength of the external field, is a long-standing open question in BH electrodynamics, with consequences for charge separation, particle acceleration and the structure of BH magnetospheres. We address it with axisymmetric general-relativistic particle-in-cell simulations performed with GRZeltron, including self-consistent pair creation. For representative values of the BH spin, we evolve the same asymptotically uniform Wald field from two opposite initial horizon charges, $Q_{0}=0$ and $Q_{0}=Q_{\rm W}$, and track the accumulated charge through the BH horizon. We find that both branches relax within a few tens of gravitational times to the same saturated charge value. Thus, the equilibrium is a dynamical attractor of the kinetic magnetosphere rather than a memory of the initial data. The attractor lies well below $Q_{\rm W}$, at $ξ_{\rm eq}\equiv Q_{\rm eq}/Q_{\rm W}\approx 0.3$ for $a\lesssim0.7$, then falling almost to $ξ_{\rm eq}\approx0$ for large spins $a\approx1$. We derive an analytic expression for $ξ_{\rm eq}$ by requiring equal magnetic fluxes through the horizon associated with positive and negative charges, and find its spin dependence to be in agreement with the simulations. The charge state of an astrophysical BH is therefore driven by kinetic plasma processes, and the Wald charge is an upper bound, not a general equilibrium value. Since $ξ_{\rm eq}<1$, charging does not quench the horizon-infinity potential drop that powers energy-extraction processes such as the Blandford-Znajek mechanism.

</details>


### [169] [Weak in the Presence of Beauty: Gravitational waves from the mergers of black holes and neutron stars as a messenger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16463)
*Fiona H. Panther,Jordan W. N. Moncrieff,Mac L. Button*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文综述引力波作为新型"信使"，探讨如何通过地面激光干涉仪探测的致密双星并合事件获取黑洞与中子星的信息，并展望未来数十年的关键机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2015年以来引力波探测已积累超过200个事件（第四版引力波瞬变星表，2025年），引力波作为传递致密天体物理性质与宇宙学信息的新型信使，亟需系统梳理其科学价值与信息提取路径。

Method: 综述性研究：梳理引力波观测流程，阐述如何将LIGO、Virgo、KAGRA等激光干涉仪的输出转化为关于黑洞与中子星的知识，并以引力波源族群作为信使进行分析。

Result: 引力波源族群可作为信使，在恒星演化理解、宇宙随宇宙时的变化规律以及基础物理三个方向带来突破性认识。

Conclusion: 论文最后讨论了未来数十年利用引力波作为信使深化宇宙认知的三个关键机遇。

Abstract: In 2025, the 4th Gravitational Wave Transient Catalog reported the discovery of over 200 gravitational wave events since 2015. These gravitational waves (GWs) are emitted by compact binary coalescenses (CBCs) between neutron stars and black holes, and detected with a global network of ground-based laser interferometers. GWs represent a new `messenger' -- ripples in spacetime that propagate across cosmological distances, and carry information about the physical properties of compact objects and their location in the Universe. In this review, we consider what information we can learn about black holes and neutron stars using GWs as a messenger. We explore how the GW observation process allows us to transform the outputs of laser interferometers like LIGO, Virgo and KAGRA into knowledge about black holes and neutron stars. We show that the GW source population acts as a messenger that can lead us to breakthroughs in our understanding of stellar evolution, how the Universe has changed across cosmic time, and fundamental physics. Finally, we discuss three key opportunities to learn more about our Universe using GWs as a messenger in the coming decades.

</details>


### [170] [Study of Null Geodesics and their Stability in Kalb-Ramond Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16471)
*P. A. González,Marco Olivares,Eleftherios Papantonopoulos,Yerko Vásquez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究四维带宇宙学常数的带电Kalb-Ramond黑洞中零测地线及其稳定性，量化洛伦兹对称性破缺参数l对光子球、偏折轨迹与不稳定性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 背景反对称张量场的非零真空期望值诱导自发洛伦兹对称性破缺并变形Reissner-Nordström-(A)dS几何，需要判断零测地线能否作为探测洛伦兹破缺效应的敏感探针。

Method: 推导无质量粒子的有效势，解析求解光子球半径、临界碰撞参数、捕获截面与偏折轨迹；讨论l→0极限以恢复RN、RN-dS、RN-AdS结构，并分析AdS分支的limaçon型零测地线；计算不稳定圆轨道的Lyapunov指数。

Result: 有限l值移动转向点、改变光线弯曲并变形第一类与第二类光子轨迹；AdS分支存在角结构受Kalb-Ramond参数修改的limaçon型零测地线；宇宙学常数改变不稳定时间尺度，洛伦兹破缺参数对光子球不稳定性产生非平凡变形。

Conclusion: 零测地线可作为带电黑洞时空中洛伦兹破缺效应的敏感探针。

Abstract: We investigate null geodesics and their stability in four-dimensional charged Kalb-Ramond black holes with a cosmological constant. The non-vanishing vacuum expectation value of the background antisymmetric tensor field induces spontaneous Lorentz symmetry breaking, controlled by a dimensionless parameter $l$, and deforms the Reissner-Nordström-(A)dS geometry. We derive the effective potential for massless particles and obtain analytic expressions for the photon-sphere radius, critical impact parameter, capture cross section, and deflection trajectories. The limiting cases $l\rightarrow0$ recover the RN, RN-dS and RN-AdS geodesic structures, while finite values of $l$ shift the turning points, modify the bending of light and deform both first-kind and second-kind photon trajectories. In the AdS branch, we find a special limiting limaçon-type null geodesic whose angular structure is modified by the Kalb-Ramond parameter. We also compute the Lyapunov exponent of the unstable circular null orbit and show that the cosmological constant changes the instability timescale, whereas the Lorentz-violating parameter produces a non-trivial deformation of the photon-sphere instability. Our results identify null geodesics as sensitive probes of Lorentz-violating effects in charged black hole spacetimes.

</details>


### [171] [Correlated Galactic Confusion Foreground for the LISA-Taiji-TianQin Network and Its Implications for Resolvable Source Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16477)
*Minghui Du,Peng Xu,Ziren Luo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文构建了LISA-Taiji-TianQin空间引力波探测器网络的全前景噪声协方差矩阵，并据此评估跨探测器前景相关性对信号探测与参数估计的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫赫兹频段的主要噪声来源是约10^7个未分辨银河系双星（GBs）产生的混淆前景。由于同一星族投影到不同探测器的TDI通道上，该前景在网络中必然相关。以往常采用块对角噪声模型（忽略跨探测器相关性），其适用性与影响尚需系统检验。

Method: 基于约3×10^7个GB的星表进行数值模拟，构建LISA-Taiji-TianQin三探测器网络的完整前景噪声协方差矩阵；推导跨探测器前景相干性的解析模型，用于交叉验证并给出物理解释；据此推导网络整体灵敏度，并刻画前景相关性随频率与时间的变化；以MBHB（瞬变源）和GB（连续源）为代表，对比块对角模型与完整协方差模型在SNR、参数不确定度及贝叶斯后验上的差异，并用P-P检验检测参数偏差；参数估计采用Triangle-BBH和Triangle-GB网络分析流程。

Result: 完整协方差与块对角模型的影响局限于特定信号区间：主要影响高质MBHB（M_c约为10^7太阳质量）和低频GB（f_0小于约2 mHz），SNR相对差异分别可达约30%和约10%；两种噪声模型下均未出现统计显著的参数偏差（P-P检验验证）。

Conclusion: 跨探测器前景相关性在特定源区间内对SNR有不可忽略的影响，但对参数估计无显著偏差；两个分析代码库连同模拟前景数据与网络灵敏度均已公开，便于开展多样化科学研究。

Abstract: The millihertz gravitational-wave sky is expected to be observed by a network of space-based detectors (LISA, Taiji, and TianQin) in the 2030s. A dominant noise in this band is the confusion foreground produced by O(10^7) unresolved Galactic binaries (GBs). Because the same population projects onto the time-delay interferometry (TDI) channels of the different detectors, the foreground is necessarily correlated across the network. We construct the full foreground noise covariance matrix for the LISA-Taiji-TianQin network from a catalogue of ~3 x 10^7 GBs through numerical simulation, and we derive an analytic model of the cross-detector foreground coherence that provides a cross-check and physical interpretation of the numerical results. We derive the overall sensitivity of the detector network based on this covariance matrix, and further characterize the frequency- and time-dependent cross-detector foreground correlations. Taking massive black hole binaries (MBHBs) and GBs as representative transient and continuous sources, we further compare the block-diagonal (i.e., neglecting the cross-detector foreground correlation) and full-covariance noise models in terms of their impacts on the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), parameter uncertainties, and Bayesian posteriors. The impact is confined to specific signal regimes, affecting primarily high-mass (M_c ~ 10^7 M_sun) MBHBs and low-frequency (f_0 <~ 2 mHz) GBs, with SNR relative differences of up to ~30% and ~10%, respectively. No statistically significant parameter bias arises under either noise model, as verified by probability-probability tests. Parameter estimations for MBHBs and GBs are performed using the network analysis pipelines implemented in the Triangle-BBH and Triangle-GB codes. Both repositories, together with the simulated foreground data and network sensitivities, are publicly released for diverse scientific investigations.

</details>


### [172] [The Null Energy Condition through the Lens of Optimal Transport: Smooth and Non-Smooth Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16942)
*Fabio Cavalletti,Davide Manini,Andrea Mondino*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文综述了从最优传输视角研究零能量条件（NEC）的进展，涵盖光滑与合成（非光滑）洛伦兹几何，并提出合成曲率条件 NC^e(N) 及合成 Penrose 奇点定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典能量条件依赖光滑可微结构，而其几何与因果后果是否在非光滑洛伦兹几何中依然成立尚不明确；希望借助最优传输建立曲率、因果性与奇点形成之间的联系。

Method: 在光滑情形，利用建立在零超曲面上的零测地传输方案与 rigged measure，将 NEC 等价刻画为 Shannon 熵幂的位移凹性；进而通过非时序边界、gauge 函数与参考测度在拓扑因果空间中定义合成零超曲面，并将位移凹性推广为合成曲率条件 NC^e(N)。

Result: 给出 NEC 的最优传输刻画及其应用，包括加权 Hawking 面积定理；证明 NC^e(N) 与光滑理论相容、在 gauge 与参考测度自然变换下不变、在收敛意义下稳定；并得到连续洛伦兹度规下的合成 Penrose 奇点定理。

Conclusion: NEC 的几何与因果后果可超越光滑设定而持续存在，最优传输为洛伦兹几何中曲率、因果性与奇点形成提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We survey recent developments on the null energy condition (NEC) from the perspective of optimal transport, with emphasis on non-smooth Lorentzian geometry. After recalling the role of classical energy conditions in general relativity, we discuss the relation between Ricci curvature and entropy convexity in optimal transport.
  The main focus is the authors' optimal-transport characterization of the NEC. In the smooth setting, the NEC is equivalent to displacement concavity of the Shannon entropy power along suitable null-geodesic transport plans supported on null hypersurfaces and measured with respect to rigged measures. We review the geometric ingredients of this characterization and applications, including a weighted Hawking area theorem.
  We then turn to the synthetic theory introduced in a second paper of the authors. Synthetic null hypersurfaces are defined using achronal boundaries, gauge functions, and reference measures in topological causal spaces. In this framework, the displacement-concavity characterization becomes a synthetic curvature condition, $\mathsf{NC}^{e}(N)$, meaningful without differentiable structure. We discuss compatibility with the smooth theory, invariance under natural changes of gauge and reference measure, and stability under convergence. Finally, we present a synthetic version of Penrose's singularity theorem for continuous Lorentzian metrics.
  These results indicate that geometric and causal consequences of the NEC persist beyond the smooth setting and highlight optimal transport as a framework connecting curvature, causality, and singularity formation in Lorentzian geometry.

</details>


### [173] [Bridging Between Statistical Mechanics and Black Hole Evolution: Theoretical Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16534)
*Suvodip Mukherjee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次提出用统计力学描述黑洞演化：尽管单个黑洞演化由随机过程支配，但其分布函数的宏观演化可用Kramers-Moyal展开表达，从而揭示驱动黑洞演化的物理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙中黑洞质量跨越近十个数量级，且存在于极早宇宙时期。由于缺乏能覆盖全质量范围与全部宇宙时间的直接探测手段，黑洞的形成与演化机制仍是未解之谜。作者希望找到一种不依赖微观随机过程细节、又能刻画整体演化的宏观描述框架。

Method: 将黑洞演化视为随机过程，转而研究黑洞在宇宙中的分布函数；把该分布函数在整个质量范围和宇宙时间上的演化用Kramers-Moyal展开表示，从而在统计力学与黑洞演化之间建立桥梁。

Result: 成功实现了黑洞演化的统计力学描述，证明分布函数的演化可纳入Kramers-Moyal展开框架；该桥梁使得能够识别参与黑洞演化的潜在物理过程，即使对底层微观随机过程并不了解。

Conclusion: 提出了一种数据驱动的统计力学技术，未来可应用于引力波、电磁波等不同信使的观测数据，从而在未知微观随机过程的情况下理解黑洞演化的物理机制。

Abstract: Black holes in the Universe span nearly ten orders of magnitude in mass over a large cosmic timescale, dating back to at least the Universe's early history, when it was only a few hundred million years old. The formation and evolution of these objects remain a mystery because no direct probe can trace each black hole across the full mass range and cosmic time. This work shows, for the first time, a statistical mechanical description of black hole evolution. I show that though each black hole and its evolution are governed by stochastic processes, one can make a macroscopic description of the distribution function of black holes in the Universe, and its evolution over the entire mass range and cosmic time can be expressed in terms of the Kramers-Moyal expansion. This bridging between statistical mechanics and black hole evolution in terms of the Kramers-Moyal expansion enables the discovery of the underlying physical processes that play a role in the evolution of black holes. In the future, the application of this technique to data accessible from different messengers, such as gravitational waves and electromagnetic waves, which probe black holes in the Universe, can lead to a data-driven understanding of the underlying physical processes by this statistical mechanics technique despite the ignorance of the underlying microscopic stochastic processes.

</details>


### [174] [Thermodynamic topological classification of magnetically charged slowly rotating Kerr black holes in nonlinear electrodynamics with a cosmological constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16568)
*Peng Zhao,Yu-Die Wan,Zheng-Wen Long*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文用约化的巨正则off-shell方案对含磁荷的非线性电动力学（NLED）慢转Kerr黑洞进行热力学拓扑分类，发现在dS与AdS时空中均属于预测的 \(\overline{W}^{1-}\) 子类，整体拓扑数 \(W=-1\)，仅存单一不稳定支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望把热力学拓扑分类框架推广到更一般的旋转黑洞解，特别是NLED与磁荷共存的Kerr黑洞，并检验所预测的 \(\overline{W}^{1-}\) 子类是否有真实物理实现。

Method: 构造广义off-shell自由能及其对应的拓扑向量场，在约化巨正则系综下计算局部绕数（winding number）与整体拓扑荷，并扫描自旋、NLED参数与磁荷；同时分析逆温度曲线在视界半径端点的行为。

Result: 逆温度曲线在较低径向端点取有限非零值、在大视界半径处发散，且仅有一个绕数 \(w=-1\) 的零点；由此识别出整体拓扑数 \(W=-1\)、单不稳定支的 \(\overline{W}^{1-}\) 子类，且在所取参数范围内分类不变。与双支 \(W^{0-}\) 的Kerr-AdS结果对比，NLED有效质量函数导致端点行为改变并缺失稳定大黑洞支。

Conclusion: NLED-Kerr黑洞是预测的 \(\overline{W}^{1-}\) 子类的一个显式旋转物理实现，且在宇宙学常数两种符号下均成立；拓扑分类对自旋、NLED与磁荷参数稳健。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the thermodynamic topological classification of slowly rotating Kerr black holes with magnetic charge in nonlinear electrodynamics (NLED) using a reduced grand-canonical off-shell prescription. By constructing the generalized off-shell free energy and the associated topological vector field, we determine the local winding numbers and the global topological charge of the NLED-Kerr black hole in both de Sitter (dS) and anti-de Sitter (AdS) spacetimes. The inverse-temperature curve starts from a finite nonzero value at the lower radial endpoint and diverges at large horizon radius. Together with the single zero of winding number $w=-1$, this behavior identifies the black hole as a realization of the predicted \(\overline{W}^{1-}\) subclass, with global topological number $W=-1$ and a single unstable branch. The classification remains unchanged over the sampled rotation, NLED, and magnetic-charge parameters. Within the same prescription, comparison with the two-branch $W^{0-}$ Kerr-AdS result associates the NLED effective mass function with the changed endpoint behavior and the absence of a stable large-black-hole branch. Thus, the physical novelty is an explicit rotating NLED realization of the predicted \(\overline{W}^{1-}\) subclass for both signs of the cosmological constant.

</details>


### [175] [Black-Hole First Laws and Horizon Constraints: A Differential Rank Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16571)
*Gunn Kim*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出微分秩判据，用显式约束的微分张成条件刻画视界热力学第一定律的逐点可表示性与邻域恒等式，并应用于Kerr–Newman、旋转BTZ及新型NMG黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视界方程的热力学改写依赖于哪些关系被变分、哪些已被施加，这种选择往往隐含不清；需明确区分第一定律的代数（逐点）条件与邻域光滑恒等式，避免把第一定律的推论与关于所选约束及其延拓的额外主张混为一谈。

Method: 引入微分秩判据：构造含独立标记径向曲面的稳态度规族、由度规参数确定的物理荷及视界热力学量的指定延拓；将第一定律余向量视为可选约束的线性组合，判定其是否落在这些约束微分张成的空间中，并区分逐点可表示性与邻域恒等式，同时分析固定系综数据在自由能变分中的作用。

Result: 若正则约束已定义完整物理族，则逐点可表示性由第一定律本身推出，而邻域恒等式还取决于所选延拓；在显式给定延拓下，Kerr–Newman、新质量引力中旋转BTZ黑洞及新型NMG黑洞均与各自视界约束精确吻合；反之，把精确度规族代入场方程分量可能在检验任何视界条件前就消去该残差；并给出显式熵–体积比较。

Conclusion: 该框架将既定第一定律的推论与关于所选约束及其延拓的附加主张清晰分离，为视界热力学改写提供了可操作的代数判据与一致性检验标准。

Abstract: Thermodynamic rewritings of horizon equations depend on which relations are varied and which have already been imposed. We formulate a differential rank criterion that makes these choices explicit. The construction uses a stationary metric family together with an independently marked radial surface, physical charges determined by the metric parameters, and specified extensions of the horizon thermodynamic quantities. A first-law covector is pointwise representable by selected constraints precisely when it lies in the span of their differentials. We distinguish this algebraic condition from a smooth identity in a neighborhood. If regular constraints already define the complete physical family, pointwise representability follows from the first law itself; a neighborhood identity additionally depends on the chosen extensions.
