With explicit prescriptions, Kerr--Newman, rotating BTZ black holes in new massive gravity, and the new-type NMG black hole all exhibit exact alignment with their horizon constraints. By contrast, substituting an exact metric family into a field-equation component can annihilate that residual before any horizon condition is tested. We also give an explicit entropy--volume comparison and specify the role of fixed ensemble data in free-energy variations. The framework separates consequences of the established first law from additional claims about selected constraints and their extensions.

</details>


### [176] [Radial Stress and the Innermost Stable Circular Orbit of a Bardeen Black Hole in a Dark Matter Halo: A First-Order Response Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16685)
*Yang Deng,Jia-Zhou Liu,Wen-Di Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 暗物质密度分布本身无法决定黑洞周围时空，径向应力需独立设定并主导强场轨道响应。文章在Hernquist晕中研究Bardeen黑洞，通过三个密度、质量函数与截断相同但径向应力不同的球对称模型，推导出ISCO移动的一阶判据 $C_\lambda=C_\rho+\lambda C_p$，并用多种密度分布、Hayward背景及已发表Dehnen晕结果验证其普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上常以暗物质密度分布刻画黑洞周围时空，但同一密度可对应不同径向应力，导致强场轨道响应（如ISCO位置）出现差异。作者希望揭示径向应力作为独立自由度的作用，并给出可判别的定量判据。

Method: 将暗物质晕视为小扰动，围绕Bardeen黑洞在Hernquist晕中构造三个球对称模型：$p_r^{\mathrm{DM}}=-\rho_{\mathrm{DM}}$、其截断形式，以及截断的Einstein团簇（$p_r^{\mathrm{DM}}=0$）。在此基础上推导ISCO主导移动的一阶判据 $C_\lambda=C_\rho+\lambda C_p$，并对五种密度分布进行未展开计算，另用Hayward背景和已发表Dehnen晕结果作交叉检验，同时引入连续应力插值以定位主导移动消失的临界闭合。

Result: 两种截断闭合对Hernquist剖面给出相反方向的ISCO移动；五种密度分布的未展开计算与判据预测符号一致。Hayward背景与已发表Dehnen晕结果验证判据不限于Bardeen-Hernquist系统。连续应力插值给出使主导移动为零的临界闭合。对代表性的四年极端质量比旋进，在固定密度与截断下改变径向应力，可在一阶绝热处理中产生数弧度的相位差。

Conclusion: 暗物质晕的径向应力是独立于密度分布的强场轨道响应控制量；仅凭密度分布不足以确定黑洞周围时空及ISCO位置。所给一阶判据具有跨背景、跨剖面的适用性，并可导致EMRI波形中可观测的数弧度相位差，为区分暗物质晕模型提供潜在探针。

Abstract: A dark matter density profile alone does not determine the spacetime around a black hole; the radial stress must be prescribed separately and governs the strong-field orbital response. We study a Bardeen black hole in a Hernquist halo through three spherical models sharing the same density, mass function, and cutoff but differing in radial stress: a halo with $p_r^{\mathrm{DM}}=-ρ_{\mathrm{DM}}$, its truncated form, and a truncated Einstein cluster with $p_r^{\mathrm{DM}}=0$. Treating the halo as a small perturbation, we derive a first-order criterion for the leading innermost stable circular orbit shift, $C_λ=C_ρ+λC_p$. The two truncated closures shift this orbit in opposite directions for the Hernquist profile, and unexpanded calculations for five density profiles confirm the predicted signs. Checks using a Hayward background and a published Dehnen-halo result show that the criterion is not restricted to the Bardeen-Hernquist system. A continuous stress interpolation identifies a critical closure at which the leading shift vanishes. For a representative four-year extreme-mass-ratio inspiral, changing the radial stress at fixed density and cutoff produces a phase difference of several radians in a leading-order adiabatic treatment.

</details>


### [177] [At Most One Inner Killing Horizon in Stationary Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16698)
*Jing-Peng Ye,Yu-Xuan Li,Run-Qiu Yang,Li Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明稳态轴对称黑洞在每个连通内部分支中至多有一个非退化内Killing视界，与物质内容无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳态轴对称黑洞是标准理论框架，但其内部难以探测：缺乏超曲面正交的类时Killing矢量使度规非对角，参考系拖拽阻碍了常规内部探测手段，因此内视界的数目缺乏一般性结论。

Method: 对紧致视界截面，对常时超曲面的法向束应用Raychaudhuri方程，强能量条件使lapse平方为次调和函数，由极大值原理排除第二个内视界；对全息中重要的非紧致平面与双曲截面，改用零能量条件，通过新引入的"逆lapse流"得到单调性障碍，并用粘性解构造其弱延拓。

Result: 任意满足经典能量条件的稳态轴对称黑洞，其每个连通内部分支至多含一个非退化内Killing视界，且与物质内容无关。

Conclusion: 引力由能量条件所编码的吸引性，既驱动视界形成又限制内视界数目，揭示同一聚焦机制的双重角色。

Abstract: Stationary-axisymmetric black holes are the standard theoretical framework for astrophysical black holes, yet their interiors remain largely inaccessible: the absence of a hypersurface-orthogonal timelike Killing vector renders the metric non-diagonal, and frame dragging obstructs the usual techniques for probing the interior. We prove that any stationary-axisymmetric black hole satisfying the classical energy conditions contains at most one nondegenerate inner Killing horizon per connected interior branch, irrespective of its matter content. For compact horizon sections, we apply the Raychaudhuri equation to the congruence normal to constant-time hypersurfaces: the strong energy condition makes the squared lapse subharmonic, and the maximum principle excludes a second inner horizon. For noncompact planar and hyperbolic sections central to holography, the null energy condition instead yields a monotonicity obstruction through a geometric flow we introduce here, the ``inverse lapse flow,'' whose weak continuation via viscosity solutions we also construct. The attractive nature of gravity, encoded in these energy conditions, thus both drives horizon formation and caps the number of inner horizons, revealing a striking dual role of the same focusing mechanism.

</details>


### [178] [An Abel-Inversion Formalism for Spacetime Metric Reconstruction from Light Deflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16844)
*Aniruddha Ghosh,Ujjal Debnath*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种逆引力透镜形式化方法，通过Abel变换建立偏折角与度规函数之间的积分-微分关系，可从可观测量直接重构静态球对称时空的度规函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通常先假定具体度规（如Schwarzschild），再计算偏折角。作者希望反转这一逻辑，不预设度规形式，直接从引力偏折角的观测值出发重构时空几何，从而建立光传播观测量与时空几何之间的联系。

Method: 通过Abel变换将逆问题形式化，推导出联系偏折角剖面α(b)与度规函数A(r)的积分-微分关系。验证方式包括：取α(b)=0时应恢复A(r)=1（平坦Minkowski）；应用太阳观测动机下的领头阶表达式α(b)=2(1+γ)GM⊙/(c²b)，在弱场极限r≫M下重构出Schwarzschild形式；进一步考虑太阳偏折角的高阶修正，重构超出领头阶近似的度规。

Result: 零偏折情形正确恢复平坦时空度规A(r)=1；太阳领头阶偏折角重构出的度规在弱场下回到Schwarzschild形式；引入高阶修正后，结果中(M/r)²项消失，所得弱场展开近似比仅用领头阶偏折角重构的度规更精确。

Conclusion: 引力透镜观测可无需先验假定具体度规，直接重构底层时空几何；该逆透镜形式化为连接观测光传播与时空几何提供了新框架。

Abstract: We develop an inverse lensing formalism for reconstructing the metric function of a static, spherically symmetric spacetime directly from the gravitational deflection angle of light. By formulating the inverse problem through an Abel transformation, we derive an integro-differential relation connecting the observable deflection profile to the underlying spacetime geometry. As a consistency check, we consider the case of vanishing deflection, \(α(b)=0\), and recover \(A(r)=1\), corresponding to flat Minkowski spacetime. We then apply the formalism to the gravitational deflection of light by the Sun using the observationally motivated leading-order expression $α(b)=\frac{2(1+γ)GM_{\odot}}{c^{2}b}.$The resulting metric function is shown to recover the Schwarzschild form in the weak-field limit \(r\gg M\). We further consider the higher-order correction to the solar deflection angle and reconstruct the corresponding metric beyond the leading-order approximation. Interestingly, the \((M/r)^2\) term vanishes in the resulting weak-field expansion, yielding an improved approximation compared with the metric reconstructed from the leading-order deflection angle. Our results demonstrate that gravitational lensing observations can provide a direct route to reconstructing the underlying spacetime geometry without assuming a specific metric a priori. This formalism therefore, offers a novel framework for connecting observational light propagation with spacetime geometry.

</details>


### [179] [Limits on the Inferred Hubble Constant Bias from a Local McVittie Gravitational Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16961)
*Daniele Gregoris*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过求解McVittie时空中径向零测地线的Sachs光学方程，量化一个局域中心质量对哈勃常数H0推断的观测偏差，并证明其效应可忽略，无法解释哈勃张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验局域时空不均匀性（如银河系中心质量）能否偏置低红移距离阶梯测量、进而解释约8%的哈勃张力，是本文动机。

Method: 在渐近de Sitter膨胀背景下的McVittie时空中，积分径向零测地线的Sachs光学方程，以无量纲Hawking-Hayward耦合参数μ=H_dS m_H的一阶展开，推导面积距离与光度距离的线性化解析表达式，并对从Sgr A*到星系团晕等天体物理尺度做数值评估。

Result: McVittie中心质量对入射光子产生坐标不变的路径聚焦效应；对典型星系参数（μ~10^-16），H0修正仅约10^-15%，远不足以解释~8%的哈勃张力；同时给出局域时空不均匀性偏置低红移距离阶梯测量的严格上限。

Conclusion: 局域星系质量本身无法解决哈勃张力；其对低红移距离测量的偏差在数量级上可忽略，并受严格上限约束。

Abstract: We examine the exact propagation of light in a McVittie spacetime to quantify the observational bias introduced on the inferred Hubble constant $H_0$ by a localized central mass embedded in an expanding asymptotically de Sitter background. By integrating the Sachs optical equations for radial null geodesics, we derive linearized analytical expressions for the areal and luminosity distances to first order in the dimensionless Hawking-Hayward coupling parameter $μ= H_{\text{dS}} m_H$. We evaluate the magnitude of this local gravitational field effect across realistic astrophysical scales, ranging from supermassive black holes ($\text{Sgr A}^*$) to galaxy cluster halos. We show that while a McVittie central mass induces a coordinate-invariant path-focusing effect on incoming photons, the quantitative correction for local galactic parameters ($μ\sim 10^{-16}$) is negligible ($\sim 10^{-15}\%$), proving that a localized galactic mass alone cannot resolve the $\sim 8\%$ Hubble tension. We establish strict upper bounds on the extent to which local spacetime inhomogeneity can bias low-redshift distance ladder measurements.

</details>


### [180] [Impact of the Host Galaxy on the Search for Gravitational-Wave Microlensing by Compact Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16990)
*Uddeepta Deka,Apratim Ganguly,Soham Bhattacharyya,Parameswaran Ajith*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 引力波微透镜搜寻通常假设孤立点质量透镜(PML)，但天体物理致密天体多嵌于星系中，其收敛与剪切会扰动信号。本文用NFW分布放置致密天体、以SIS模型计算收敛与剪切，构建PML叠加星系势的透镜模型，发现忽略星系效应会系统性降低搜寻灵敏度，尤其对高质量微透镜，未来约束致密暗物质丰度需纳入星系效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对已探测引力波事件中微透镜信号的搜寻、以及据此对致密天体暗物质占比的限制，均采用孤立点质量透镜模型。但真实致密天体通常位于星系等更大结构中，其引力势提供的额外收敛与潮汐剪切会改变透镜信号，因此需评估这一假设偏差对搜寻的影响。

Method: 将致密天体按Navarro-Frenk-White密度剖面分布在星系内；用嵌入恒定收敛和剪切场中的点质量透镜势描述微透镜效应，其中收敛与剪切由星系透镜的奇异等温球模型计算。

Result: 忽略星系效应会系统性降低微透镜搜寻的灵敏度，且这一效应在高微透镜质量情形下尤为显著。

Conclusion: 未来微透镜搜寻（尤其是旨在对致密暗物质丰度给出稳健限制的工作）需纳入透镜星系带来的附加效应。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave (GW) microlensing occurs when an intervening compact object causes diffraction of the GW, imprinting characteristic frequency-dependent modulations on the observed signal. Current searches for microlensing signatures in the detected GW events, including those used to constrain the fraction of dark matter in the form of compact objects, assume an isolated point mass lens (PML) model. However, astrophysical compact objects are typically embedded within larger structures, such as galaxies, whose potentials introduce additional convergence and tidal shear, thereby perturbing the lensed signal. In this work, we study the impact of the background galaxy in the search for microlensing by compact objects using a PML model. We distribute the compact objects inside a galaxy following the Navarro-Frenk-White density profile. We model the microlensing effects using the potential of a PML embedded in a constant convergence and shear, which are, in turn, calculated from a singular isothermal sphere model of the galaxy lens. We demonstrate that neglecting the galaxy's effects can systematically reduce sensitivity in microlensing searches, particularly for high microlens masses. Thus, future searches may need to incorporate the additional effect of the lens galaxy, particularly those aimed at placing robust constraints on the abundance of compact dark matter.

</details>


### [181] [Pseudospectrum of Braneworld Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17077)
*Hai-Long Jia,Wen-Di Guo,Yun-Tao Gu,Yu-Xiao Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将伪谱分析方法引入膜世界场景，首次给出膜世界扰动问题的双曲超曲面形式及适配翘曲几何的高度函数规范，并计算了引力子零模与准正规模谱的条件数与伪谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪谱分析可有效刻画非自伴算子的谱稳定性，已广泛用于黑洞物理，但尚未应用于膜世界场景；本文旨在填补这一空白，考察标量场生成厚膜背景下张量引力扰动的谱敏感性。

Method: 在标量场生成的厚膜背景中处理张量引力扰动：首次提出膜世界扰动的双曲超曲面表述，并设计适配翘曲几何的高度函数规范；该构造将准正规模的出射边界条件转化为紧致化有限边界上的正则性条件，并将扰动方程重写为由非自伴双曲演化算子生成的一阶系统。基于相应能量范数，计算定域引力子零模与准正规模谱的条件数和伪谱。

Result: 条件数沿泛音序列迅速增长，高泛音区的伪谱等值线形成宽而连通的整体结构。谱敏感性显著依赖于模式：在分析的衰减模式中，高泛音比基模更不稳健；零模条件数大于基模，表明其局域一阶敏感性更强。

Conclusion: 伪谱诊断刻画的是对一般范数有界算子扰动的敏感性；若要将该敏感性关联到具体的膜世界形变，仍需相应的自洽扰动约束。

Abstract: Pseudospectral analysis provides a powerful way to probe the spectral stability of non-self-adjoint operators and has been widely used in black hole physics, but its application to braneworld scenarios has not yet been explored. In this work, we apply this method to tensor gravitational perturbations in a representative scalar-field-generated thick brane background. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first hyperboloidal formulation of braneworld perturbations and propose a height-function gauge adapted to the warped geometry. This construction converts the outgoing boundary conditions of quasinormal modes into regularity conditions at finite compactified boundaries and recasts the perturbation equation as a first-order system generated by a non-self-adjoint hyperboloidal evolution operator. With the corresponding energy norm, we compute the condition numbers and pseudospectra of the localized graviton zero mode and the quasinormal-mode spectrum. We find that the condition numbers grow rapidly along the overtone sequence and that the corresponding pseudospectral contours develop broad, connected structures in the high-overtone region. These results show a strongly mode-dependent spectral sensitivity: among the damped modes analyzed, the higher overtones are less robust than the fundamental mode. The zero mode also has a larger condition number than the fundamental mode, indicating stronger local first-order sensitivity. These diagnostics characterize sensitivity to generic norm-bounded operator perturbations. Relating that sensitivity to a specific braneworld deformation requires the corresponding self-consistent perturbation constraints.

</details>


### [182] [Critical and extremal gravitational collapse of a spherical charged scalar field in 4+1 spacetime dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17207)
*Satwik Mittal,Carsten Gundlach,Laetitia Martel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文数值研究了4+1维球对称时空中带电标量场与Einstein-Maxwell方程耦合的引力坍缩，发现与3+1维类似的II型临界现象，并首次从正则初始数据给出形成严格极端带电黑洞的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望检验高维（4+1维）带电标量场引力坍缩是否仍表现出II型临界现象，并探索坍缩区域内部是否存在形成严格极端黑洞的初始数据。

Method: 在4+1维球对称假设下，对耦合Einstein-Maxwell方程的带电标量场进行数值演化；通过调节初始数据参数（变化幅度达约0.5%）考察坍缩阈值的临界行为，并分析初始数据空间中形成极端黑洞的区域结构。

Result: 在坍缩阈值处发现了与3+1维非常相似的II型临界现象，包括临界解的自相似性和黑洞质量与电荷的幂律标度关系；在坍缩区域内部，识别出能坍缩为极端黑洞的初始数据，并发现初始数据空间存在一个小邻域，其中codimension-1的初始数据集在演化中形成严格极端带电黑洞。

Conclusion: 作者认为这是首次从正则初始数据出发、针对标量场坍缩给出形成严格极端黑洞的证据，表明高维带电标量场坍缩中存在丰富且与四维类似的临界与极端结构。

Abstract: We numerically investigate the gravitational collapse of a charged scalar field coupled to the Einstein and Maxwell equations in spherical symmetry, in 4+1 spacetime dimensions. At the threshold of collapse we find type-II critical phenomena very similar to what is seen in 3+1 dimensions, including self-similarity of the critical solution and power-law scaling of the black hole mass and charge. Well inside the collapse region, we also identify initial data that collapse to an extremal black hole. By varying all parameters of the initial data by up to $\sim0.5\%$, we give evidence that there is a small neighbourhood in the space of initial data where an exactly extremal charged black hole is formed in the evolution of a codimension-1 set of initial data. We believe this is the first such evidence for scalar field collapse from regular initial data.

</details>


### [183] [Symmetry of the Klein-Gordon Equation under Disformal Transformations with Higher-Order Derivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17462)
*Yurev Ivan Ross B. Carag,Allan L. Alinea*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用Takahashi-Motohashi-Minamitsuji提出的高阶导数共形变换，研究标量场Klein-Gordon方程在共形变换下的形式不变性条件，并在二阶情形下找到一族共享同一传播解的共形相关度规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量-张量理论通过引入标量场扩展广义相对论，其中标量场与物质的耦合可由共形变换调节。然而，对于含任意高阶标量场导数的广义共形变换，Klein-Gordon方程的形式不变性条件尚不明确，本文旨在确定这些条件并探讨共形相关度规的物理含义。

Method: 采用Takahashi、Motohashi与Minamitsuji提出的含标量场任意高阶导数的共形变换（用于映射两个无鬼高阶标量-张量理论），推导标量场Klein-Gordon方程保持共形形式不变性的最一般条件；并讨论高阶情形下与理论和现象学约束的相容性，以及将共形变换视为时空流形"形变"的概念。

Result: 在二阶情形下，识别出一族共形相关的度规，它们给出相同的传播解；高阶情形可在满足理论与现象学约束的同时保持Klein-Gordon不变性。

Conclusion: 共形变换可用于刻画保持标量场传播解不变的度规族，且高阶情形在适当约束下仍可实现Klein-Gordon形式不变性，为将共形变换理解为时空流形形变提供了依据。

Abstract: Scalar-Tensor theories extend General Relativity (GR) by including both a scalar field and a metric tensor in describing gravity. Although the matter sector is minimally coupled to the metric in GR, the scalar field coupling to matter may be adjusted by means of Disformal Transformations. One of the most general of these transformations, formulated by Takahashi, Motohashi, and Minamitsuji, include arbitrarily high-order derivatives of the scalar field and are designed to map between two ghost-free Higher-order Scalar-Tensor theories. We used these transformations to determine the most general conditions for the disformal form-invariance of the Klein-Gordon equation for the scalar field. In the second-order case, we identified a family of disformally-related metrics that result in the same propagation solutions. Additionally, higher-order cases could comply with theoretical and phenomenological constraints alongside KG-invariance. Finally, we investigated the notion of disformal transformations as ``deformations'' of the spacetime manifold.

</details>


### [184] [Quasinormal modes of Kerr-Newman-de Sitter black holes from Heun connection formulae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17471)
*Hideo Furugori,Naotaka Kubo,Hayato Motohashi,Daisuke Yoshida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用精确Heun连接公式（以经典共形块表示），解析研究了Kerr-Newman-de Sitter时空中Teukolsky方程支配的准正规模，给出小宇宙学常数、近极端和近Nariai极限下的准正规模谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准正规模是刻画黑洞扰动与稳定性的核心物理量，而Kerr-Newman-de Sitter这类同时含电荷与宇宙学常数的时空其谱结构复杂，需要在不同极限下获得解析结果。作者希望借助Heun连接公式这一解析工具，系统地推导各极限下的准正规模频率。

Method: 将自旋权重和场电荷视为任意参数，把Teukolsky方程的准正规模问题转化为Heun方程，并使用通过经典共形块表达的精确Heun连接公式；在求解连接条件时，不局限于领头极点近似，而在小宇宙学常数与近极端情形下求解完整连接条件；同时对角度本征值问题用同一连接公式方法进行分析。

Result: 在小宇宙学常数极限下得到领头阶带电de Sitter谱及其次领头修正；在小宇宙学常数和近极端两种极限下，超越领头极点近似求解完整连接条件会普遍产生非整数幂修正；而近Nariai极限中不出现类似的非整数幂修正。

Conclusion: 精确Heun连接公式为Kerr-Newman-de Sitter时空准正规模提供了统一的解析框架，揭示了不同极限下谱修正结构的差异（非整数幂修正的出现与否），并同样适用于角度本征值问题。

Abstract: We study quasinormal modes governed by the Teukolsky equation in Kerr-Newman-de Sitter spacetime, formally treating the spin weight and field charge as arbitrary parameters. We use exact Heun connection formulae expressed through classical conformal blocks. We analytically derive the quasinormal mode spectra in the small cosmological constant, near-extremal, and near-Nariai limits. In the small cosmological constant limit, we obtain the leading charged de Sitter spectrum together with its subleading corrections. In both the small cosmological constant and near-extremal limits, solving the full connection condition beyond the leading pole approximation gives rise to generically noninteger-power corrections to the quasinormal mode frequencies, whereas no analogous correction appears in the near-Nariai limit. We also analyze the angular eigenvalue problem as an illustration of the same connection-formula method.

</details>


### [185] [A Fully Analytic Expression for the 2PN N-Body Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17510)
*Felix M. Heinze,Gerhard Schäfer,Bernd Brügmann*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文给出了ADM规范下无自旋点粒子2PN阶N体哈密顿量的完整解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作将一般N体哈密顿量约化到仅含一个未解决积分项的形式，无法得到完全显式的闭式表示。

Method: 解析计算该积分项，推导一般2PN N体哈密顿量的完全显式闭式表达式，并概述推导步骤。

Result: 获得一般2PN N体哈密顿量的完全显式闭式表达式，并通过与原积分表示的数值比较验证了解析表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了2PN阶N体哈密顿量的显式解析表达，为后续后牛顿动力学研究提供了完整基础。

Abstract: We present a complete analytic expression for the $N$-body Hamiltonian of nonspinning point particles at second post-Newtonian (2PN) order in the Arnowitt-Deser-Misner (ADM) gauge. Recent work reduced the general $N$-body Hamiltonian to a form containing a single unresolved integral term, which prevented a fully explicit closed-form representation. Here, we evaluate this integral analytically and thereby obtain a fully explicit closed-form expression for the general 2PN $N$-body Hamiltonian. We outline the main steps of the derivation and validate the analytic expression through direct numerical comparisons with the original integral representation.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [Loop spaces, twistor $\mathbb P^1$ and tempiric parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16246)
*Tsao-Hsien Chen,Lingfei Yi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文建立了循环对称空间上的球面轨道、twistor P¹上主丛与tempered Langlands参数之间的双射，并借助Vogan极小K型理论给出不可约等变局部系统、不可约局部系统与对偶对称子群所有强内形式不可约表示之间的双射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望为实约化群的几何Langlands对偶提供一个组合层面的"影子"，通过将循环对称空间上的轨道与表示论、主丛几何等多种对象联系起来，揭示其间的深层对应关系。

Method: 主要方法包括：构造循环对称空间上球面轨道、twistor P¹上主丛与tempered Langlands参数之间的双射；并运用Vogan的极小K型理论，将双射推广到不可约等变局部系统、主丛上不可约局部系统以及对偶对称子群强内形式的不可约表示之间。

Result: 建立了三组对象之间的双射：（1）球面轨道↔主丛↔tempered Langlands参数；（2）不可约等变局部系统↔不可约局部系统↔对偶对称子群强内形式的不可约表示。

Conclusion: 这些双射可被视为实约化群几何Langlands对偶的组合投影，暗示了在更一般的几何框架下存在相应的对偶关系。

Abstract: We establish bijections among spherical orbits on loop symmetric spaces, principal bundles on the twistor $\mathbb P^1$, and tempiric Langlands parameters. Using Vogan's theory of minimal $K$-types, we further establish bijections among irreducible equivariant local systems on loop symmetric spaces, irreducible local systems on principal bundles on the twistor $\mathbb P^1$, and irreducible representations of all strong inner forms of the dual symmetric subgroup. The latter bijections may be viewed as a combinatorial shadow of geometric Langlands duality for real reductive groups.

</details>


### [187] [Functions on Nilpotent Orbit Covers and Birational Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17272)
*William Graham,Scott Joseph Larson,Alberto San Miguel Malaney*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 利用扩展 Springer 消解构造有限覆盖余切丛的簇，证明其有理奇点，从而描述 SL_n 幂零轨道万有覆叠上正则函数环的 G-模结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画 $G=SL_n$ 幂零轨道万有覆叠 $\widetilde{\co}$ 上正则函数环 $R(\widetilde{\co})$ 的 $G$-模结构，推广扩展 Springer 消解方法。

Method: 构造簇 $\widetilde{\mcM}$，使其有限于 $G/P$ 的余切丛，并在 $\widetilde{\co}$ 的仿射化 $\mcM$ 上proper且birational；用双有理几何技术证明 $\widetilde{\mcM}$ 具有有理奇点，得到上同调消隐。

Result: 证明 $\widetilde{\mcM}$ 有有理奇点；将 $R(\widetilde{\co})$ 描述为来自 $G$ Levi 子群的诱导表示；给出其分次 $G$-模结构；描述 $\mcM$ 的最小嵌入及成分群特征标到抛物、Levi 子群的提升。

Conclusion: 为幂零轨道万有覆叠函数环提供诱导表示刻画，并推广提出更一般的消隐猜想。

Abstract: We use an analogue of the Springer resolution to describe the $G$-module structure on the ring of regular functions on the universal cover $\widetilde{\co}$ of any nilpotent orbit for $G = SL_n$. Building on previous work on the extended Springer resolution, we construct a variety $\widetilde{\mcM}$ that is finite over the cotangent bundle of a partial flag variety $G/P$, and proper and birational over the affinization $\mcM$ of $\widetilde{\co}$. We use techniques in birational geometry to show that $\widetilde{\mcM}$ has rational singularities, which provides the cohomology vanishing needed to describe the ring of functions on $\widetilde{\co}$ as an induced representation from a Levi subgroup of $G$. Our results also yield a description of the structure of $R(\widetilde{\co})$ as a graded $G$-module. We describe the minimal embedding of $\mcM$, study the lifting of characters of the component group of $\widetilde{\co}$ to parabolics and Levi subgroups, and make a more general vanishing conjecture.

</details>


### [188] [A tensor square theorem for characters of $\operatorname{GL}_{n}(q)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17319)
*Nariel Monteiro,Alexander Stasinski*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 构造了 $\operatorname{GL}_n(q)$ 的一个不可约特征标，其平方包含所有在中心上平凡（trivial）的不可约特征标；该特征标一般不同于 Steinberg 特征标。关键工具是证明：任何在中心上平凡的非线性不可约特征标，限制到对角子群 $T$ 时都包含平凡特征标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 $\operatorname{GL}_n(q)$ 的特征标理论，特别是与 Steinberg 特征标相关的、具有"全覆盖"性质（平方包含某类全部不可约特征标）的不可约特征标构造。

Method: 构造 $\operatorname{GL}_n(q)$ 的一个特定不可约特征标，使其平方包含所有在中心上平凡的不可约特征标；并证明辅助引理：每个在中心上平凡的非线性不可约特征标，限制到对角子群 $T$（极大环面）时含平凡特征标。

Result: 得到该不可约特征标的构造及其覆盖性质（平方包含所有中心上平凡不可约特征标），并证明了关于限制到对角子群 $T$ 的关键引理；同时说明该特征标一般与 Steinberg 特征标不相等。

Conclusion: 给出了一类新的具有普适平方分解性质的不可约特征标，推广了 Steinberg 特征标的某些角色，并揭示了中心平凡的不可约特征标在对角子群上的限制结构。

Abstract: We construct an irreducible character of $\operatorname{GL}_{n}(q)$ whose square contains every irreducible character that is trivial on the centre of $\operatorname{GL}_{n}(q)$. This character is related to, but in general not equal to, the Steinberg character. A key ingredient, which we also prove, is that every nonlinear irreducible character of $\operatorname{GL}_{n}(q)$ that is trivial on the centre contains the trivial character when restricted to the diagonal subgroup $T$.

</details>
