### [189] [A LooKK at the Higuchi Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16103)
*Dieter Lüst,Joaquin Masias*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出，任何自洽紧致化产生的KK引力子塔都不应违反de Sitter空间的Higuchi bound（m²≥2H²），并在圆周、warped区间和群流形等例子中进行了检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维引力紧致化到dS₄会产生质量由内部几何决定的KK引力子塔，Higuchi bound因此成为对紧致化的约束。作者希望探究自洽紧致化是否必然满足该约束，以理解dS紧致化的一致性条件。

Method: 通过分析几个具体紧致化模型来检验该猜想：由Casimir能量稳定的圆周、含负张力brane的warped区间，以及群流形上的紧致化，并考察将形变提升为moduli后对10维运动方程求解的影响。

Result: 圆周情形下，除非圆收缩到低于species scale，否则bound成立；warped区间（负张力brane）情形下，无论区间长度如何，引力子塔都满足m²≥(9/4)H²；群流形上，若内部曲率不显著大于Hubble尺度，bound表面上可被光滑形变违反，但将形变提升为moduli后违反消失。

Conclusion: 结果支持所提猜想：自洽紧致化的KK引力子塔不会违反Higuchi bound；表面上的违反在正确处理moduli并求解完整10维运动方程后消失。

Abstract: Massive spin 2 fields on de Sitter must satisfy the Higuchi bound, $m^2\geq 2H^2$. Compactifications of higher dimensional gravity to $\mathrm{dS}_4$ come with a tower of Kaluza-Klein gravitons whose masses are fixed by the internal geometry, so the bound becomes a constraint on the compactification. We propose that the KK gravitons of a consistent compactification never violate it, and test this in a few examples. On a circle stabilized by Casimir energy the bound holds unless the circle shrinks below the species scale, and on a warped interval with negative tension branes the tower is gapped at $m^2\geq \tfrac{9}{4}H^2$ for any length of the interval. On group manifolds, we argue that if the internal curvature is not parametrically larger than the Hubble scale, the bound can apparently be violated by smooth deformations. The violation disappears once one promotes these deformations to moduli, which are immediately extremized when solving the 10d equations of motion.

</details>


### [190] [Canonical differential equations for Feynman integrals from $\mathcal{A}$-hypergeometric systems in the Schwinger representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16107)
*Mateo Jimenez-Santacruz,Cristhiam Lopez-Arcos,Alexander Quintero Velez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出基于D-模方法，直接从GKZ超几何系统构造Feynman积分的正则微分方程，通过广义Euler积分与Frobenius基构造Pfaffian系统，并在有理化变量变换后化为正则ε-形式，其正则基维度由GKZ系统的全纯秩决定，可比IBP主积分数目更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Feynman积分微分方程多依赖Lee-Pomeransky表示与IBP约化，主积分数目可能偏大；作者希望直接从GKZ系统出发，利用D-模理论获得更简洁、维度更低的微分方程描述，并直接获取奇点信息。

Method: 从Schwinger表示出发，识别广义Euler积分及其GKZ系统（替代Lee-Pomeransky表示）；利用Newton多面体面(facet)进行变量约化；用Frobenius基构造Pfaffian系统以获取奇点轨迹；再经由有理化变量变换将系统化为正则ε-形式。

Result: 所构造的正则基维度由GKZ系统的全纯秩决定，在所考察的例子中不大于由IBP得到的主积分数目，从而给出约化后的微分系统描述。

Conclusion: 该D-模/GKZ方法为Feynman积分正则微分方程提供了一种直接从超几何系统构造的途径，可减少变量与方程维度，并直接给出奇异轨迹，具有良好的约化优势。

Abstract: We follow a $D$-module approach to the construction of canonical differential equations for Feynman integrals directly from their Gel'fand-Kapranov-Zelevinsky (GKZ) hypergeometric systems. Starting from the Schwinger representation, we identify the associated generalized Euler integral and its GKZ system, as an alternative to the Lee-Pomeransky representation. The generalized Euler formulation allows reductions associated with facets of the Newton polytope to be identified directly, reducing the number of variables in the resulting differential systems. We construct the associated Pfaffian systems using Frobenius bases, which also provide direct access to the singular loci. After a suitable rationalizing change of variables, the systems are brought into canonical $ε$-form. A key feature of the construction is that the dimension of the canonical basis is determined by the holonomic rank of the GKZ system and, for the examples considered, it is smaller than or equal to the number of master integrals obtained from integration-by-parts, thus providing a reduced description of the differential system.

</details>


### [191] [Spectral Chaos from a Fluctuating Quantum Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15286)
*Chong-Sun Chu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在视界矩阵模型中，视界几何的量子涨落打破parton态简并，生成一个由微观模型决定的单parton哈密顿量系综；其谱按反幺正对称类呈现GOE/GUE/GSE统计，并实现Pandey–Mehta正交–幺正交叉，为黑洞谱混沌提供微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞被认为具有最大混沌性，其量子谱应表现出普适的随机矩阵关联。但此前这一预期主要依赖于全息描述，或依赖于人为引入无序而非从视界自身微观自由度导出的多体模型，缺乏直接从量子视界几何到黑洞谱混沌的微观路径。

Method: 在视界矩阵模型中，模糊球支持高度简并的parton态，其多重数对应黑洞熵。作者引入视界几何的量子涨落以解除该简并，从而生成一个单parton哈密顿量系综。该系综的算符结构与秩依赖协方差均由微观模型确定，而非由淬火耦合固定。随后通过反幺正对称类分析、相邻能隙统计、Pandey–Mehta正交–幺正交叉以及秩分辨方差分析来刻画谱统计。

Result: 尽管系综具有结构化协方差，其谱仍按反幺正对称类呈现GOE、GUE和GSE统计，且相邻能隙统计随矩阵尺寸增大而趋于自平均。模型实现了普适的Pandey–Mehta正交–幺正交叉，交叉尺度由微观几何协方差决定。秩分辨方差分析显示，涨落强度在张量秩上广泛分布直至微观截断，为Wigner–Dyson普适性和有效非局域动力学提供了微观基础。条件哈密顿量的Dyson类由底层量子几何的反幺正对称性决定。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了从量子视界几何到黑洞谱混沌的微观路径，并提供了JT引力中几何与随机矩阵普适性之间Stanford–Witten关系的微观对应物。

Abstract: Black holes are conjectured to be maximally chaotic, with their quantum spectra expected to exhibit universal random-matrix correlations. This expectation, however, has so far rested largely on holographic descriptions or on many-body models in which randomness is introduced rather than derived from the microscopic degrees of freedom of the horizon itself. In the horizon matrix model, the fuzzy sphere supports highly degenerate parton states whose multiplicity accounts for the black-hole entropy. We show that quantum fluctuations of the horizon geometry lift this degeneracy and generate an ensemble of one-parton Hamiltonians. Both the operator structure and the rank-dependent covariance of this ensemble are determined by the microscopic model, rather than fixed by quenched couplings.
  Despite this structured covariance, the resulting spectra exhibit GOE, GUE, and GSE statistics dictated by their antiunitary symmetry classes, with the adjacent-gap statistic becoming increasingly self-averaging with matrix size. The model also realizes the universal Pandey--Mehta orthogonal-to-unitary crossover, with the crossover scale determined by the microscopic geometric covariance. A rank-resolved variance analysis shows that the fluctuation strength remains broadly distributed across tensor ranks up to the microscopic cutoff, providing a microscopic basis for the observed Wigner--Dyson universality and effectively nonlocal dynamics. The antiunitary symmetry acts directly on the fuzzy-sphere geometry, so the Dyson class of a conditional Hamiltonian is determined by the antiunitary symmetry of the underlying quantum geometry. This provides a microscopic counterpart of the Stanford--Witten relation between geometry and random-matrix universality in JT gravity. These results establish a microscopic route from quantum horizon geometry to black-hole spectral chaos.

</details>


### [192] [Boundary duals of bulk detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16109)
*Jacqueline Caminiti,Robert C. Myers,Kelly Wurtz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出利用HKLL重构将体（bulk）中的Unruh-DeWitt探测器与边界上涂抹（smeared）探测器相对应，并借此在AdS₃ Poincaré片中分析纠缠采集（entanglement harvesting），同时讨论体探测器的微因果性与边界对偶探测器可能的直接重叠之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题在于：反德西特（AdS）空间中体内部的Unruh-DeWitt探测器的边界对偶究竟是什么。作者希望为体探测器寻找自然的边界描述，并探究体与边界探测器之间以及两个体探测器之间的纠缠采集行为，同时澄清微因果性与边界对偶重叠之间的表面矛盾。

Method: 作者认为该问题可由HKLL重构纲领自然回答：体中的局域探测器对应于边界上的涂抹探测器，涂抹方式取决于所选的HKLL核。据此在AdS₃的Poincaré片中，将体的Unruh-DeWitt探测器分别与边界探测器或第二个体探测器配对，通过把双探测器密度矩阵在耦合常数λ的二次阶展开来计算纠缠采集量。

Result: 文中给出了在标准方法下（密度矩阵展开至λ的二阶）所计算的纠缠采集量，并据此分析了体探测器与边界探测器及两个体探测器两种配对情形下的纠缠采集结果。

Conclusion: HKLL重构为体的Unruh-DeWitt探测器提供了自然的边界对偶描述；基于该形式可以一致地计算纠缠采集，并可用于讨论类空分离体探测器的微因果性与对偶HKLL边界探测器可能直接重叠之间的表面张力。

Abstract: What is the boundary dual of an Unruh-DeWitt detector in anti-de Sitter space? We argue that this question is naturally answered by the HKLL reconstruction program. A local detector in the bulk can be associated with a smeared detector in the boundary, where the choice of smearing depends on the choice of HKLL kernel. We use this formalism to analyze entanglement harvesting in the Poincaré patch of AdS$_{3}$ for a bulk Unruh-DeWitt detector paired either with a boundary detector or with a second bulk detector. We compute the amount of harvesting in the standard way, namely by evaluating the two-detector density matrix to quadratic order in the coupling $λ$. Additionally, we address an apparent tension between microcausality for spacelike-separated bulk detectors and the fact that the dual HKLL detectors may directly overlap in the boundary theory.

</details>


### [193] [Classification of c=1 CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16111)
*Lorenz Eberhardt,Nat Levine*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文完全分类了中心荷 $c=1$ 且具有离散谱的幺正二维共形场论，证明仅有紧致自由玻色子、其 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 轨道折叠以及由 SU(2)$_1$ WZW 模型轨道折叠得到的三种例外理论，并证明给定态密度后 CFT 可唯一重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $c=1$ 幺正二维 CFT 的分类是共形场论中的经典问题，长期存在一个"民间共识"式的猜想，但缺乏完整严格证明。作者希望给出该猜想的严格证明，并厘清态密度与完整 CFT 之间的对应关系。

Method: 将 $c=1$ 的模形式 bootstrap 问题重新表述为态密度的"晶体测度问题"（crystalline measure problem），并对其进行解析求解，从而完成分类并证明态密度确定后 CFT 的唯一重构。

Result: 完整分类了中心荷 $c=1$、离散谱的幺正二维 CFT：仅包括紧致自由玻色子、其 $\mathbb{Z}_2$ 轨道折叠，以及 SU(2)$_1$ WZW 模型的三种轨道折叠例外理论；并证明态密度一旦给定，完整 CFT 可被唯一重构。

Conclusion: 该工作严格证实了关于 $c=1$ 幺正 CFT 分类的长期猜想，并建立了态密度与完整 CFT 之间的一一对应关系，为模形式 bootstrap 方法提供了新的解析工具与视角。

Abstract: We completely classify unitary 2d CFTs with central charge $c=1$ and discrete spectrum. We confirm the folklore that the only such theories are the compact free boson, its $\mathbb{Z}_2$-orbifold, and three exceptional theories obtained as orbifolds of the SU(2)$_1$ WZW model. The proof is by recasting the $c=1$ modular bootstrap as a crystalline measure problem for the density of states, and analytically solving it. We also show that, once the density of states is specified, the full CFT can be uniquely reconstructed.

</details>


### [194] [When the gravity path integral describes a statistical average](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16113)
*Gabriele Di Ubaldo,Luca V. Iliesiu,Henry W. Lin,Cynthia Yan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出了引力路径积分(GPI)能够被解释为希尔伯特空间内积或边界观测量统计平均的充要条件，发现条件(i)对(ii)必要但不充分，并用玩具模型和bootstrap分析展示了更强条件(ii)的约束能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力路径积分常被用于计算边界观测量，但其是否真正对应一个统计平均（而非仅仅"伪平均"）或内积解释尚不清楚，需要明确的数学判据。作者希望建立区分这两种解释的充要条件，并利用这些正定性条件约束虫洞振幅，从而筛选可行的引力有效场论。

Method: 作者推导GPI可解释为开/闭宇宙希尔伯特空间内积（条件i）以及边界观测量统计平均（条件ii）的必要充分条件；随后构造一类满足(i)但违反(ii)的引力玩具模型以对比两条件差异；并基于条件(ii)开展统计系综的bootstrap研究。

Result: 条件(i)对(ii)必要但不充分；条件(ii)对所有虫洞振幅施加额外约束，提供判断GPI是计算平均还是伪平均的具体诊断；玩具模型证明两者可分离；bootstrap分析表明条件(ii)给出的系综矩约束显著严于仅用(i)所得约束。

Conclusion: 这两类正定性条件对虫洞振幅施加非平凡约束，进而限制了哪些引力有效场论能被嵌入一致的量子引力理论，为GPI的统计解释提供了可操作的判据。

Abstract: We find the necessary and sufficient conditions for the gravitational path integral (GPI) to admit an interpretation in terms of (i) an inner product in the Hilbert space of open/closed universes or (ii) a statistical average of boundary observables. The conditions needed for (i) are necessary but not sufficient for (ii). Requiring the stronger condition (ii) places additional constraints on all wormhole amplitudes, giving a concrete diagnostic of whether the GPI computes an average or merely a pseudo-average. To exemplify the difference between the two constraints, we present a class of gravitational toy models that satisfy (i) but fail the stronger condition (ii); to emphasize the power of the stronger condition, we initiate a bootstrap study of statistical ensembles based on condition (ii), finding that the resulting constraints substantially sharpen the bounds on the moments of the ensemble obtained by imposing (i). More broadly, both of these positivity conditions impose nontrivial constraints on wormhole amplitudes and thereby restrict which gravitational effective field theories can be embedded in consistent theories of quantum gravity.

</details>


### [195] [Single-Instance Observables in the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16130)
*Brian Swingle*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了低权重Majorana弦在固定SYK实现下热期望值的图解展开，按N^{-1/2}组织，各阶可用多项式经典资源高效计算，并在N=24的精确对角化上验证，同时应用于MQ模型以寻找更优热场双态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SYK模型及其相关模型（如Maldacena-Qi模型）的低权重可观测量的热期望值通常依赖精确对角化，规模受限；作者希望发展一种在固定耦合实现下、按大N展开且计算资源多项式可控的图解方法，以刻画有限N效应并用于热场双态等物理问题的研究。

Method: 针对固定SYK实现中的低权重Majorana弦热期望值，构造图解展开：每阶表示为耦合的多项式有限和乘以由大N melonic传播子构成的温度依赖核；在固定βJ下按N^{-1/2}组织，各阶用关于N多项式的经典资源求值；展开推广到任意虚时关联和足够短的实时关联；对四体SYK与N=24精确对角化对比，并将方法用于由两个相同固定SYK拷贝构成的Maldacena-Qi模型。

Result: 对四体SYK模型，图解预测在N到24范围内与精确对角化结果吻合极好；对Maldacena-Qi模型，该方法识别出一个邻近的SYK-like哈密顿量，其热场双态与MQ基态的保真度高于原SYK哈密顿量的热场双态。

Conclusion: 该图解展开为固定SYK实现下低权重Majorana弦的热关联提供了系统、可高效计算的微扰框架，能准确捕捉有限N效应，并可推广至虚时与短实时关联；在MQ模型中可用于优化热场双态，为研究近似量子引力和热场双态构造提供工具。

Abstract: We present a diagrammatic expansion for thermal expectation values of low-weight Majorana strings in a fixed realization of the $N$-fermion Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) model. At each order in the expansion, the observable is expressed as a finite sum of polynomials in the couplings multiplied by temperature-dependent kernels built from the large-$N$ melonic propagator. At fixed $βJ$, the expansion is organized in powers of $N^{-1/2}$, and each order can be efficiently evaluated with classical resources polynomial in $N$. The expansion also extends to correlations at arbitrary imaginary times and sufficiently short real times. For the four-body SYK model, we show that the diagrammatic predictions give an excellent account of exact diagonalization results at sizes up to $N=24$. We also apply the method to the Maldacena--Qi (MQ) model formed from two identical copies of a fixed SYK realization. This enables the identification of a nearby SYK-like Hamiltonian whose thermofield double state has higher fidelity with the Maldacena--Qi ground state than the thermofield double of the original SYK Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [196] [Separation of variables for non-diagonalisable models: XXX with twisted boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16146)
*Juan Miguel Nieto García*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文利用量子分离变量方法研究带缺陷扭曲矩阵的扭曲周期边界Heisenberg XXX自旋链，提出"Jordanian TQ方程"刻画其广义本征矢，并揭示高秩广义本征矢在分离基下不可分离、需用弱组合求和表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带缺陷（非可对角化）扭曲矩阵的周期边界条件会导致转移矩阵不可对角化，从而出现广义本征矢（Jordan链）而非仅真本征矢。经典Bethe ansatz与标准TQ方程难以刻画此类"Jordanian"结构，亟需在分离变量框架下建立相应的代数描述。

Method: 采用量子分离变量（quantum separation of variables）方法处理Heisenberg XXX自旋链，将扭曲周期边界条件中的扭曲矩阵取为缺陷（非对角化）形式，并推导其广义本征矢所满足的修正TQ方程（命名为Jordanian TQ方程），同时在分离基下分析本征矢与广义本征矢的波函数结构。

Result: 1) 证明该系统的广义本征矢可由所提出的Jordanian TQ方程完全刻画；2) 证明在分离基下，只有真本征矢的波函数是可分离的；3) 高秩广义本征矢的波函数不再可分离，而是由对弱组合（weak compositions）的求和给出。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子分离变量方法推广至具有缺陷扭曲的边界条件，给出Jordanian TQ方程作为刻画广义本征谱的核心工具，并明确了可分离性仅在真本征态成立，为理解不可对角化边界条件下的可积自旋链提供了新框架。

Abstract: We study the Heisenberg XXX spin chain with twisted periodic boundary conditions with a defective twist matrix. Using quantum separation of variables, we show that the generalised eigenvectors of this system can be characterised in terms of a modified TQ equation that we have named "Jordanian TQ equation". We also show that the wavefunction in the separated basis is only separable for true eigenvectors, while the wavefunction for generalised eigenvectors of higher rank is given instead by a sum over weak compositions.

</details>


### [197] [Group-averaged baby universe field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16116)
*Hong Zhe Chen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文从群平均振幅出发，构建引力对时空拓扑求和的"婴儿宇宙场论"，其量子为整个宇宙；通过一维世界线等情形证明该场满足线性Wheeler-DeWitt方程且系数对易，并探讨更一般拓扑与Marolf-Maxfield模型的Fock空间表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力对时空拓扑的求和在单宇宙理论中如何从群平均振幅定义物理内积，并如何用"量子是整个宇宙"的场论刻画，需要与普通量子场论形成鲜明对比，同时厘清非平凡拓扑下该场论的适用性。

Method: 以施加引力约束、定义物理内积的群平均振幅为出发点；先从一维世界线（可由高维圆柱约化得到）入手，将配对边界的多世界线情形表述为物理单宇宙Hilbert空间上的对称Fock空间，用壳上模展开宇宙场；再推广到盘状拓扑、非平凡拓扑（相互作用费曼图）及Marolf-Maxfield拓扑模型，用截断与精确Fock空间表示及相互作用插入分析内积。

Result: 多世界线情形下婴儿宇宙Hilbert空间是物理单宇宙Hilbert空间上的对称Fock空间，宇宙场按壳上模展开，每个模乘以产生与湮灭算符之和，故模系数对易，显式体现场的可对易性与Wheeler-DeWitt线性场方程；对角化系数得到以壳上场构型的高斯路径积分描述的统计描述，标记超选择（α-）扇区。盘状拓扑相干移动无边界态，开启Hartle-Hawking波函数作为单点函数；非平凡拓扑下群平均仍强制同一线性场方程，相互作用通过α-扇区上的非高斯测度进入。在Marolf-Maxfield模型中，将盘-柱截断提升为含所有拓扑的精确Fock空间表示，并用相互作用插入联系截断与精确内积。

Conclusion: 婴儿宇宙场论可由群平均振幅系统构建，其线性场方程由边界附近的群平均普遍保证，而相互作用仅体现在α-扇区上的非高斯测度；截断与精确内积之间通过相互作用插入相联系，其微扰展开给出的时间演化求和与群平均形成类比。

Abstract: Gravitational sums over spacetime topology suggest a baby-universe field theory whose quanta are entire universes. We develop such a description from group-averaged amplitudes that impose gravitational constraints and define physical inner products in the single-universe theory. To sharpen the contrast with ordinary QFT, we start with gravity on one-dimensional worldlines, which can arise from reducing higher-dimensional cylinders. For multiple noninteracting worldlines that pair boundary components, the baby-universe Hilbert space is the symmetric Fock space over the physical one-universe Hilbert space, and the universe field expands in the on-shell modes spanning the latter. Each mode multiplies the sum of creation and annihilation operators, so the mode coefficients commute. The mode expansion thus makes explicit the field's commutativity and its Wheeler-DeWitt linear field equation. Diagonalizing the coefficients recovers a statistical description by a Gaussian path integral over on-shell field configurations labelling superselection ($α$-)sectors.
  We take exploratory steps toward more general topologies. Disk-like topologies coherently shift the no-boundary state, turning on the Hartle-Hawking wavefunction as a one-point function. Even with nontrivial topologies represented by interacting Feynman diagrams, we argue that group averaging near each boundary still enforces the same linear field equation, while interactions instead enter through a non-Gaussian measure over $α$-sectors. In the topological Marolf-Maxfield model, we promote a disk-and-cylinder truncation to an exact Fock-space representation of the full theory incorporating all topologies. We also relate the truncated and exact inner products by an insertion of interactions, whose perturbative expansion produces a sum over time evolutions generated by pairs of pants, evoking an analogy to group averaging.

</details>


### [198] [Exact group invariant scar towers in two dimensional gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16132)
*João Barata,Kiryl Pakrouski,Andrey V. Sadofyev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在二维规范理论（两个无质量基本费米子味）中构造了精确的多体scar塔：对反号电荷费米子，scar子空间由规范中性的η-pairing算符生成并可代数构造；电荷共轭将其映射为等电荷模型的矢量味极化扇区，可解释为具有类Josephson相位动力学的相干味模。有限质量会混合受保护配对而破坏精确塔结构。该构造可推广到一大类格点场论，揭示此类态的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体scar态（弱破缺遍历性、低纠缠本征态）此前多在一维或特定自旋/费米子模型中研究，缺乏在具有非平凡连续极限解释的规范理论中的解析构造。作者希望建立规范理论中可解析处理的精确scar子空间，从而赋予scar态更物理、更普适的场论意义。

Method: 在二维规范理论中引入两味无质量基本费米子；针对反号电荷费米子，用规范中性的η-pairing（η配对）算符生成scar子空间，并进行纯代数构造。通过精确对角化数值验证scar态的纠缠熵与长程配对关联；利用电荷共轭将η-塔映射到等电荷模型的矢量味极化扇区，得到类Josephson相位动力学的相干味模解释；并分析有限费米子质量对受保护配对与正交通道混合的效应。

Result: 成功构造出精确的scar塔，数值显示scar态具有反常低的纠缠与长程配对关联。电荷共轭给出矢量味极化扇区的等价描述，并被解释为具有Josephson-like相位动力学的相干味模。引入有限质量后混合正交通道，精确塔被破坏。该scar塔可在一大类格点场论中代数实现，表明其普适性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了规范理论中不变scar子空间的解析构造，并赋予其非平凡连续极限解释，将多体scar研究从一维/特制模型推广到规范场论与味物理背景，揭示了此类受保护态的普适特征。

Abstract: We construct exact many-body scar towers in two dimensional gauge theories with two flavors of massless fundamental fermions. For oppositely charged fermions, the scar subspace is generated by a gauge-neutral $η$-pairing operator, and admits a purely algebraic construction. Exact diagonalization methods reveal anomalously low entanglement and long-range pair correlations in the scar states. Charge conjugation maps the $η$-tower to a vector-flavor polarization sector of the equal charge model, yielding an interpretation as a coherent flavor mode with Josephson-like phase dynamics. Finite fermion masses mix the protected pair with an orthogonal channel and destroy the exact tower. The identified scar tower can be algebraically realized in a large family of lattice field theories, revealing a universal character of these states. Our results provide an analytical construction of invariant scar subspaces in a gauge theory with a nontrivial continuum interpretation.

</details>


### [199] [An attempt to derive the LVS from a 10d EFT perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16117)
*Arthur Hebecker,Andreas Schachner,Gaetano Maria Sifo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文尝试从10维有效场论直接推导LVS AdS标量势，但发现体积压低因子与4维超引力预期不匹配，未找到满意解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在通量紧致化中稳定模量需要非微扰标量势，而直接10维推导LVS AdS势（即uplift之前的大体积标量势）是关键目标。熟悉的4维结果包含三项特定形式，但直接10维EFT推导时出现体积压低因子不匹配的谜题，需要弄清楚其根源。

Method: 将4维超引力与大体积展开得到的三项势重新解读为10维EFT语言：第一项为瞬子-反瞬子效应，第二项为单瞬子与3-形式通量耦合。然后尝试直接从10维EFT推导体积抑制因子，考虑通量稀释因子1/√V、Weyl重标度至4维Einstein frame的1/V^2，并与4维预期比较；同时在gaugino凝聚情形下讨论其逻辑。

Result: 直接10维EFT推导得到第一、二项的总体积压低强于4维EFT预期：第二项预期通量稀释1/√V叠加Weyl重标度1/V^2，导致不匹配。作者未能找到满意解释；Kähler势的对数修正或可负责，但需强至O(1)水平影响LVS方案结果，且这些修正的起源仍不清楚。

Conclusion: 10维直接推导LVS势时存在体积压低因子与4维超引力预期的不一致，尚无满意解释。可能的出路是Kähler势的对数修正，但其必须足够强以O(1)水平影响LVS，且起源不明，因此该问题仍待解决。

Abstract: Non-perturbative scalar potentials are a central ingredient for stabilising moduli in flux compactifications. A key target in this context is the direct 10d derivation of the LVS AdS potential, i.e., the scalar potential of the Large Volume Scenario before the uplift. The familiar result, relying on 4d supergravity machinery and large volume expansion, consists of three terms of parametric form $\exp(-2aτ_s)/{\mathcal V}$, $W_0\exp(-aτ_s)/{\mathcal V}^2$, and $W_0^2/{\mathcal V}^3$. In plain 10d EFT language, the first term is an instanton-anti-instanton effect while the second term comes from a single instanton coupled to 3-form flux. When attempting to derive the suppression by the volume ${\mathcal V}$ directly from the 10d EFT, one encounters a puzzle: One expects a flux-dilution factor $1/\sqrt{\mathcal V}$ for the second term and a factor $1/{\mathcal V}^2$ from Weyl rescaling to the 4d Einstein frame for both the first and second term. Thus, the total volume suppression for these two terms is stronger than in the aforementioned 4d EFT expectations. We discuss the underlying logic in detail, also in the gaugino condensation case, but fail to find a satisfactory explanation for the mismatch. While log-corrections to the Kähler potential may be responsible, these would have to be strong enough to affect the outcome of the LVS scheme at the ${\mathcal O}(1)$ level. What is worse, the origin of the required correction remains unclear.

</details>


### [200] [The Role of Completeness in Probing Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16153)
*Yuya Kusuki,Hiroyasu Tajima,Shion Yamashika*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出完备性在多体系统对称破缺度量中具有物理重要性，并引入基于保真度的对数特征函数（LCF）混合态扩展，展示其在描述对称恢复动力学上优于纠缠不对称性（EA）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息中完备性被视为单调量族的重要性质，可确保态转换相关信息不丢失。作者进一步论证：不完备的对称破缺度量会遗漏对称恢复动力学的重要特征，因此需要研究完备的度量在多体动力学中的物理作用。

Method: 研究对数特征函数（即弦序参量），其完整族对有限群在保对称操作下的精确i.i.d.纯态转换是完备的；提出该LCF向混合态的保真度扩展，并在量子场论中发展其replica构造，对共形场论给出解析结果。

Result: 与纠缠不对称性（EA）相比，LCF有两个具体优势：一是EA受log|G|上界限制，热力学极限下不同初态可趋于同一饱和值，使弛豫曲线无法区分并妨碍识别离散对称Mpemba效应，而LCF可保持广延性，其系数依赖初态与时间，能持续区分弛豫动力学；二是不同LCF分量可呈现不同的弛豫与交叉行为，甚至出现EA无交叉的情形。作者在自旋链淬火及去极化噪声下的单量子比特系统中验证了这些优势。

Conclusion: 完备的对称破缺度量（LCF）在物理上优于不完备度量（如EA），能更细致地刻画对称恢复动力学；作者还建立了混合态保真度LCF的replica框架并得到共形场论解析结果，为后续研究提供了工具。

Abstract: Completeness is widely recognized in quantum information as an important property of a family of monotones, because it ensures that no information relevant to state conversion is lost. We show that completeness is also physically important in many-body systems: an incomplete measure of symmetry breaking can miss essential features of symmetry-restoration dynamics. We study the logarithmic characteristic function (LCF), also known as the string order parameter, whose full family is complete for exact i.i.d. pure-state conversion under symmetry-preserving operations for finite groups. We introduce a fidelity-based extension of the LCF to mixed states and identify two concrete advantages over entanglement asymmetry (EA). First, for a finite symmetry group $G$, EA is bounded by $\log |G|$ and can approach the same saturated value for different initial states in the thermodynamic limit, making their relaxation curves indistinguishable and preventing the identification of a discrete-symmetry Mpemba effect. By contrast, the LCF can remain extensive, with a coefficient that depends on the initial state and time, and therefore continues to distinguish their relaxation dynamics. Second, different LCF components can exhibit distinct relaxation and crossing behavior, including cases in which EA shows no crossing. We demonstrate these advantages in spin-chain quenches and a single-qubit system under depolarizing noise. We also develop a replica construction for the fidelity-based LCF in quantum field theory and derive analytical results for conformal field theory.

</details>


### [201] [$D$-term and $F$-term potential of non-Abelian D7-branes in IIB Calabi-Yau orientifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16136)
*Daniel Junghans*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对Calabi-Yau orientifold紧化下的IIB型弦论中，由D7膜堆栈产生的4维D项和F项势进行了统一的第一性原理推导，并讨论了KK模式修正及其对模稳定性的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该势是IIB模稳定（如利用T-brane uplift实现de Sitter方案）的重要组成，但此前D项和F项部分是通过各种论证间接推断的，缺乏统一的推导。

Method: 通过维度约化D7膜的非阿贝尔DBI作用量，并考虑世界体积流、ISD体流以及膜场的非阿贝尔性质，统一推导完整的势。

Result: 结果推广了此前的表达式，并在适用范围内与其一致，从而确认了各种方法的相互自洽性；同时论证了要求控制KK模式修正会对Calabi-Yau体积和膜场产生界限。

Conclusion: 该计算不仅给出了4维有效作用量的多项结果，还揭示了体流如何修正8维世界体积理论中支配非阿贝尔膜微观动力学的势与BPS方程。

Abstract: We derive the 4d $D$-term and $F$-term potential generated by a D7-brane stack in Calabi-Yau orientifold compactifications of type IIB string theory, taking into account the effect of worldvolume and ISD bulk fluxes and the non-Abelian nature of the brane fields. This potential is an important ingredient for moduli stabilization in IIB, e.g., in de Sitter scenarios using a T-brane uplift. While aspects of the $D$-term and $F$-term parts of the potential have been inferred before with various arguments, we provide here a unified first-principles derivation of the full potential by dimensionally reducing the non-Abelian DBI action of the D7-branes. Our result generalizes previous expressions and agrees with them where applicable, thus confirming the mutual consistency of the various approaches. Moreover, we provide a thorough discussion of corrections from integrating out KK modes and argue that demanding control over such corrections implies bounds on the Calabi-Yau volume and the brane fields. Aside from these various results for the 4d effective action, our calculation also shows how bulk fluxes correct the potential and the BPS equations in the 8d worldvolume theory that governs the microscopic dynamics of non-Abelian branes.

</details>


### [202] [Symmetry Descent in M-theory, Part I: A Twelve-Dimensional Parent Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16141)
*Pinak Banerjee,Subham Roy,Xingyang Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出M理论工程量子场论的对称性下降（symmetry descent）程序：从高阶BF理论加立方体拓扑相互作用出发构建规范不变体-边系统，边界模式经度规依赖边界作用获得广义Maxwell–Chern–Simons动力学，且非线性边界方程完全由立方体相互作用诱导。以12维体为母体时，边界条件重现11维超引力C场的局域通量方程（含引力I₈修正），并借助Hypothesis H将体方程识别为S⁴的Sullivan模型、把非线性规范变换解释为同伦，进而提出扭4-余同伦量子化与体-边场空间的同伦拉回描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M理论及其低能超引力中的规范场、高阶形式场与对称性结构缺乏一个统一的动力学起源，特别是电磁配对、非线性Maxwell–Chern–Simons动力学以及引力修正（如I₈）通常被视为独立输入。作者希望用一个更高维拓扑模型统一导出这些边界动力学，并给出M理论场内容的局域机制与整体（拓扑/同伦）刻画。

Method: 1) 起始于高阶形式BF理论，并加入立方体（cubic）体拓扑相互作用；2) 构造规范不变的体-边（bulk-boundary）系统，引入度规依赖的边界作用，使边界模式获得广义Maxwell–Chern–Simons动力学；3) 证明边界方程的非线性项完全由立方体体相互作用诱导；4) 取12维体为母体，推导边界条件以重现11维超引力C场的局域通量方程（含引力I₈修正）；5) 使用Hypothesis H将体方程识别为S⁴的Sullivan模型，把非线性规范变换解释为同伦；6) 提出体场的扭4-余同伦量子化与耦合体-边场空间的同伦拉回描述。

Result: 构造出一个规范不变的体-边系统，其中电磁配对与非线性Maxwell–Chern–Simons动力学源自单一12维拓扑模型；边界条件精确重现11维超引力C场的局域通量方程并包含引力I₈修正；通过Hypothesis H给出体方程的Sullivan模型（S⁴）解释，将非线性规范变换视为同伦，并提出扭4-余同伦量子化及耦合体-边场空间的同伦拉回描述。

Conclusion: 该工作同时提供了M理论对称性下降的局域动力学机制（由12维拓扑母体诱导边界广义Maxwell–Chern–Simons动力学与引力修正）以及其场内容的候选整体刻画（Sullivan模型、同伦变换、扭4-余同伦量子化与同伦拉回）。这表明M理论的电磁配对与非线性规范结构可由高维拓扑模型统一导出，并暗示其场空间具有同伦论意义下的整体结构。

Abstract: We initiate a symmetry descent procedure for M-theory engineered quantum field theories. Starting from a higher form BF theory supplemented by a cubic bulk topological interaction, we construct a gauge-invariant bulk-boundary system whose edge modes acquire generalized Maxwell--Chern--Simons dynamics after the introduction of a metric-dependent boundary action. The nonlinear contribution to the boundary equation is induced entirely by the cubic bulk interaction. In the case of twelve dimensional bulk parent, the resulting boundary conditions reproduce the local flux equations of the eleven-dimensional supergravity $C$-field, including the gravitational $I_8$ correction. Thus, the electric--magnetic pairing and the nonlinear Maxwell--Chern--Simons dynamics descend from a single 12D topological model. Using Hypothesis H, we identify the bulk equations with the Sullivan model of $S^4$ and interpret their nonlinear gauge transformations as homotopies. We then propose a twisted 4-cohomotopy quantization of the bulk fields and a homotopy pullback description of the coupled bulk--boundary field space. The construction provides both a local dynamical mechanism for M-theory symmetry descent and a candidate global characterization of its fields.

</details>


### [203] [Persistent Spontaneous Time-Reversal Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16162)
*Michael Smolkin,Lev Yung*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在2+1维局部、幺正、紫外完备的量子场论中，通过相关Yukawa形变实现了无需热恢复的自发宇称与时间反演破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究能否在有限场数、紫外完备的局域幺正量子场论中实现自发破缺宇称与时间反演对称性，且该破缺不随升温而被热涨落恢复。

Method: 构造基于临界双锥矢量模型与无质量Dirac费米子乘积的相关Yukawa形变，诱导重整化群流，其红外端点为临界矢量模型与共形Gross-Neveu-Yukawa模型的退耦乘积；通过有限温分析研究其相变行为。

Result: 零温下真空保持两种对称性；升温后体系在有限临界温度发生反热转变，高于该温度时动力学生成费米子质量（在宇称与时间反演下为奇），破缺相一直持续到任意高温。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了自发宇称与时间反演破缺的一种反常热行为，即不存在热恢复，破缺相在高温下依然稳定存在。

Abstract: We provide an analytic realization of spontaneous parity and time-reversal breaking without thermal restoration in a local, unitary, ultraviolet-complete quantum field theory in $2+1$ dimensions with finitely many fields. The theory is defined by a relevant Yukawa deformation of a product of the critical biconical vector model and free massless Dirac fermions. This deformation induces a renormalization group flow whose infrared endpoint is a decoupled product of the critical vector model and the conformal Gross-Neveu-Yukawa model. At zero temperature, the vacuum preserves both symmetries, whereas upon heating, the theory undergoes an inverse thermal transition at a finite critical temperature. Above this temperature, a fermion mass, odd under parity and time reversal, is dynamically generated, and the broken phase persists to arbitrarily high temperatures.

</details>


### [204] [Schwinger-Keldysh effective actions for non-hydrodynamic poles and branch cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16164)
*Andrea Amoretti,Matteo Anselmi,Daniel K. Brattan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过构造高斯Schwinger-Keldysh有效作用量，揭示延迟热关联函数中的分支割线可由弛豫极点连续谱产生，并系统研究了其谱结构与微观实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延迟热关联函数解析延拓到复频率后出现的非流体力学极点与分支割线对流体力学构成约束，但分支割线的有效场论起源及其谱结构尚缺乏系统描述，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构造高斯Schwinger-Keldysh有效作用量，使分支割线源于弛豫极点的连续谱，响应函数由弛豫时间密度的Stieltjes变换支配；利用动力学KMS对称性、正性与稳定性约束谱，并由端点行为控制局部分支结构及近边缘极点标度。

Result: 以均匀密度和幂律密度为例，分别展示了超越全阶的极点-端点分离，以及极点-极点终止与极点-分支点终止之间的连续插值；并证明动能理论中能量依赖的弛豫时间近似在零动量下提供了分支割线的微观实现。

Conclusion: 分支割线可自然地由弛豫极点连续谱在有效场论框架下涌现，其谱结构受对称性与稳定性严格约束，并可在动能理论中获得微观对应。

Abstract: Retarded thermal correlators, analytically continued to complex frequency, exhibit non-hydrodynamic poles and branch cuts that constrain hydrodynamics. We construct Gaussian Schwinger-Keldysh effective actions in which branch cuts arise from continua of relaxation poles and response functions are governed by Stieltjes transforms of relaxation-time densities. Dynamical KMS symmetry, positivity and stability constrain the spectra, while endpoint behaviour controls the local branch structure and near-edge pole scaling. The illustrative examples of uniform and power-law densities exhibit respectively beyond-all-orders pole-endpoint separation and a continuous interpolation between pole-pole and pole-branch-point termination. Finally, we show that an energy-dependent relaxation-time approximation in kinetic theory provides a microscopic realization of our branch cuts at zero momentum.

</details>


### [205] [Making the Great Desert Bloom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16154)
*Jacques Distler,Andreas Karch*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 文章指出"大荒漠"假设并非灾难，反而预言标准模型存在半经典 AdS₄ 真空及其 CFT₃ 对偶，且至少伴随一个显著大于普朗克长度的额外维度，并给出一个候选 CFT₃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱电标度与普朗克标度之间缺乏新物理（大荒漠）通常被视为粒子物理的噩梦场景，作者希望重新审视这一假设，论证其实际具有深远且积极的物理含义。

Method: 基于弱电标度到普朗克标度之间不存在新物理的假设，结合全息对偶（AdS/CFT）框架进行理论论证，在合理假设下推导标准模型的 AdS₄ 真空结构、额外维度存在性及候选对偶 CFT₃。

Result: 大荒漠假设预言标准模型具有带 CFT₃ 对偶的半经典 AdS₄ 真空；在合理假设下必须至少存在一个显著大于普朗克长度的额外维度；并提出一个可能的候选 CFT₃ 作为标准模型的对偶。

Conclusion: 大荒漠并非单纯的噩梦，反而使标准模型的全息性质变得可预测，为标准模型的对偶描述与额外维度物理提供了新的理论动机与研究方向。

Abstract: The absence of new physics beyond neutrino masses between the weak scale and the Planck scale is referred to as the great desert. It is often seen as a nightmare scenario for particle physics. Here we argue, by contrast, that the great desert assumption in fact has dramatic implications: it predicts that the Standard Model has a semi-classical AdS$_4$ vacuum with CFT$_3$ dual and, under reasonable assumptions, must be accompanied by at least one extra dimension significantly larger than Planck length. We also present one plausible candidate CFT$_3$ to be ``the" dual to the Standard Model.

</details>


### [206] [Instanton Interactions and the Axion Weak Gravity Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16165)
*Jarod Hattab,Eran Palti*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将弱引力猜想（WGC）表述为长程带电粒子自相互作用为排斥性，并将其推广到轴子弱引力猜想（Axionic WGC），通过长程瞬子相互作用提出"排斥性相互作用"的轴子弱引力猜想，该猜想对复欧几里得瞬子作用的标量场依赖给出界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱引力猜想可以重新表述为长程带电粒子自相互作用具有排斥性这一物理图像。作者希望将这一排斥性视角推广到轴子情形，从而为轴子弱引力猜想提供一个更物理、更基础的表述，并探讨无质量标量场存在与否对轴子衰变常数界的影响。

Method: 作者考察了瞬子之间的长程相互作用，在瞬子间距的最低阶，这类相互作用完全由无质量标量场中介；引力与规范（规范场）相互作用为次领头阶。据此提出排斥性相互作用版本的轴子弱引力猜想，即对复欧几里得瞬子作用中标量场依赖给出一个界限。

Result: 作者证明在标准的紫外完备（ultraviolet complete）例子中，该新界限能够蕴含通常形式的轴子弱引力猜想，并且在一般情况下更强。由于该表述本质上只涉及无质量标量场，它意味着在缺少无质量标量场时，关于轴子衰变常数的通常界限可能被修正。

Conclusion: 通过将弱引力猜想的排斥性表述推广到瞬子相互作用，作者给出了一个新的轴子弱引力猜想界限，其在紫外完备例子中比通常表述更强，并指出无质量标量场的缺失可能导致轴子衰变常数界限的修正。

Abstract: The Weak Gravity Conjecture admits a formulation in terms of long-range charged particle self-interactions being repulsive. We adopt a similar approach to the Axionic Weak Gravity Conjecture by considering long-range instanton interactions. At leading order, in the instanton separation, such interactions are mediated purely by massless scalar fields. Gravitational and gauge interactions are sub-leading. We formulate a repulsive interaction Axionic Weak Gravity Conjecture, which forms a bound on the scalar field dependence of the complex Euclidean instanton action. We show that in standard ultraviolet complete examples, this bound implies the usual formulation of the Axionic Weak Gravity Conjecture, while generally being stronger. Because this formulation is purely a statement about massless scalar fields, it implies that in the absence of massless scalars the usual bound on the axion decay constant could be modified.

</details>


### [207] [A $dS_4$ maximum in M-theory from Casimir energies on Riemann-flat manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16169)
*Miquel Aparici,Bruno Valeixo Bento,Miguel Montero*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文报道了首个通过M理论在Riemann-flat流形$T^7/\mathbb{Z}_8$上紧化构建的完全显式四维top-down de Sitter鞍点解，但其现象学可行性仍不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弦论/M理论的Landscape中构建受控的四维de Sitter真空是长期难题，现有构造多依赖自下而上或近似方法，缺少完全显式的top-down dS解。作者希望填补这一空白并探索Riemann-flat Landscape中的dS鞍点。

Method: 通过将M理论紧化在Riemann-flat流形$T^7/\mathbb{Z}_8$上，构造一个完全显式的、四维的、top-down的de Sitter鞍点。并检验其对高阶圈图与高阶导数修正的可控性。

Result: 所得解的真空能为$5.02\times10^{-10}\,M_\text{Pl}^4$，层级$m_{KK}/H\sim10^{2}$，且在高圈和高导数修正下保持受控。

Conclusion: 该解是四维top-down dS极大值的首个例子，但远不具现象学可行性；它同时指出了在Riemann-flat Landscape中寻找更高质量dS鞍点的若干有前景方向。

Abstract: We describe a fully explicit, four-dimensional, top-down de Sitter saddle, constructed via compactification of M-theory on the Riemann-flat manifold $T^7/\mathbb{Z}_8$. The solution has a vacuum energy of $5.02\cdot 10^{-10}\,M_\text{Pl}^4$, a hierarchy $m_{KK}/H\sim 10^{2}$, and is under control with respect to higher-loop and higher-derivative corrections. While the solution is the first example of a top-down dS maximum in four dimensions, it is far from being phenomenologically viable. The solution also suggests several promising directions in the search for higher-quality dS saddles in the Riemann-flat Landscape, which we describe.

</details>


### [208] [Zero- and Finite-Temperature Casimir Effect in a Fracton Gauge Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16263)
*Celio R. Muniz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究自由标量荷fracton规范场在两平行反射边界间的Casimir相互作用，通过变分分析建立了能约束全部五个物理极化的边界值问题，并导出了零温与有限温下的精确热Casimir效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶秩（rank-two）fracton规范场具有多分支传播模式和受限的极化结构，其在边界约束下的真空与热涨落行为尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示这种多分支结构如何通过Casimir效应留下可直接观测的光谱特征。

Method: 采用变分分析确定能同时约束五个物理极化的自洽边界值问题；对谱进行分解，识别出三个快传播模式与两个慢传播模式；分别计算零温与有限温下的Casimir自由能和压力，考虑各模式的传播速度加权与热权重差异。

Result: 零温下真空相互作用按模式传播速度加权，当快分支速度取为光速时，Casimir压力约为理想电磁值的2.21倍。有限温下两分支获得不同的热权重，产生分裂的交叉行为；归一化自由能与压力呈现不同的交叉曲线，但在高温经典极限下均趋近理想电磁的2.5倍比值。Matsubara零模独立于传播速度地计入五个受限极化。

Conclusion: 研究给出了精确的热相互作用及其低温与高温极限，并揭示交叉尺度依赖有效规范场速度。从速度加权的量子涨落到经典模式计数的转变，为高阶秩规范场的多分支结构提供了直接的光谱证据。

Abstract: We investigate the zero- and finite-temperature Casimir interaction of the free trace-full scalar-charge fracton gauge field between two parallel reflecting boundaries. A variational analysis determines a consistent boundary-value problem that confines all five physical polarizations of the rank-two field. The spectrum separates into three faster and two slower propagating modes. At zero temperature, the vacuum interaction weights the modes by their propagation speeds; when the fast-branch speed is set equal to the speed of light, the resulting Casimir pressure is approximately 2.21 times the ideal electromagnetic value. At finite temperature, the two branches acquire different thermal weights and generate a split crossover. The normalized free energy and pressure follow distinct crossover profiles but approach the same classical high-temperature ratio of 2.5 relative to ideal electromagnetism, because the Matsubara zero mode counts the five confined polarizations independently of their propagation speeds. We derive the exact thermal interaction, its low- and high-temperature limits, and the dependence of the crossover scale on the effective gauge-field velocity. The transition from velocity-weighted quantum fluctuations to classical mode counting provides a direct spectral signature of the multi-branch structure of the higher-rank gauge field.

</details>


### [209] [Edge observables in Maxwell theory on null boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16400)
*Dušan Đorđević,Olivera Miskovic,Antonia Montecinos,Tatjana Vukašinac*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在零叶层化的哈密顿框架下研究三维Maxwell理论中零边界（类光边界）上的边缘可观测量，涉及Minkowski时空、BTZ黑洞与de Sitter空间三类背景。除通常的U(1)荷外，还发现一个与零边界内禀对称性相关的第二边缘可观测量；两者均为准局域良定义，构成中心扩展代数，其傅里叶模式为两个相反能级、相互独立的Abelian Kac-Moody代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作多关注渐近无穷远边界的边缘自由度，而对有限距离的零边界（如黑洞视界、宇宙学视界）上的边缘结构缺乏系统刻画；作者希望检验渐近边界与有限距离零边界是否具有统一的中心扩展边缘结构。

Method: 采用适配零叶层化的哈密顿表述，在Minkowski时空（零无穷远）、BTZ黑洞（事件视界）与de Sitter空间（宇宙学视界）三类背景上分别计算零边界边缘可观测量，并分析其荷代数结构（含傅里叶模式展开）。

Result: 三类背景均出现两个边缘可观测量：通常的U(1)荷与一个与零边界内禀对称性相关的附加观测量；两者均可准局域良定义，并构成中心扩展代数，在傅里叶模式下表现为两个能级相反、相互独立的Abelian Kac-Moody代数。

Conclusion: 相同的中心扩展边缘结构同时出现在渐近零边界与有限距离零边界上，表明该边缘结构具有普遍性，不依赖于边界位于无穷远还是有限距离。

Abstract: We study edge observables in three-dimensional Maxwell theory on null boundaries using a Hamiltonian formulation adapted to null foliations. We consider three backgrounds: Minkowski spacetime, the BTZ black hole, and de Sitter space, where the relevant boundaries are, respectively, null infinity, the black hole horizon, and the cosmological horizon. In all three cases we find, besides the usual $U(1)$ charge, a second edge observable associated with an intrinsic symmetry on the null boundary. The two charges are well defined quasilocally. They form a centrally extended algebra which, in Fourier modes, becomes two independent Abelian Kac-Moody algebras with opposite levels. Thus, the same centrally extended edge structure appears at both asymptotic and finite-distance null boundaries.

</details>


### [210] [Stringy Gauge Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16455)
*Gerardo Aldazabal,Eduardo Andrés,Lucía M. Cabrera,Martín Mayo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文完善了一个能纳入异弦环面紧化模空间特定点上规范对称性增强-破缺效应的场论作用量构造，提出使作用量具备潜在对称性的场变换，并考察标量导数结构的协变性，最后与扩展切空间下的gauged DFT建立联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异弦环面紧化中，规范对称性会在模空间的特定点发生增强与破缺。此前虽已借助三点相互作用与对偶对称性构造出场论作用量，但其对称性结构、变换性质及与已知框架（如DFT）的联系尚不清晰，需要进一步阐明。

Method: 采用加倍周期坐标与非对易乘积，将非阿贝尔结构以背景无关形式编码；通过广义模展开使模空间依赖性显式化；提出保留扇区的场变换，并在模空间增强点做模展开，检验其是否包含通常的非阿贝尔规范变换；同时考察标量导数结构的协变性；并借助广义Kaluza-Klein模与额外广义标架方向的对应，将构造与扩展切空间的gauged DFT相关联。

Result: 所提场变换在增强点模展开后包含通常的非阿贝尔规范变换，标量导数结构的协变性得到检验；该构造可编码含至多一个振子激发的无质量与有质量态表示；在增强点其括号复现增强规范代数，远离增强点时通过旋转Cartan基亦可恢复同一代数结构。

Conclusion: 该构造虽仍应视为当前未知完整场论描述的截断版本，但已能一致地编码规范对称性增强-破缺效应及相关态表示，并与扩展切空间的gauged DFT建立对应，为进一步构建完整理论提供了基础。

Abstract: A field theory action, able to incorporate stringy gauge symmetry enhancement-breaking effects, which occur at particular points of moduli space in a heterotic string toroidal compactification framework, was previously constructed from three-point interactions and duality symmetries. Via doubled periodic coordinates and a non-commutative product, it encodes the non-Abelian structure in a background-independent form. Moduli-space dependence becomes manifest upon performing a generalized mode expansion. Here we further elaborate on this construction. In particular, we propose field transformations for the retained sectors that make up the foundations of a potential symmetry of the action and that, when mode expanded at enhancement points in moduli space, contain the usual non-Abelian gauge transformations. We also examine the covariance of the corresponding scalar derivative structures. Although the proposal must be regarded as a truncated version of a full, presently unknown field-theory description, it already encodes representations of massless and massive states containing up to one oscillator excitation. Finally, we relate the construction to gauged DFT with an extended tangent space by associating generalized Kaluza-Klein modes considered here with additional generalized-frame directions. At enhancement, their brackets reproduce the enhanced gauge algebra; away from enhancement, the same algebraic structure is recovered by rotating the Cartan basis.

</details>


### [211] [On the relaxation dynamics of non-equilibrium quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17447)
*Matthias Carosi,Björn Garbrecht,Silvia Pla,Nils Wagner,Edward Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文对Zubarev非平衡统计算符方法进行教学性综述，研究近平衡相互作用量子系统中近似守恒荷的弛豫，给出了弛豫律、扩散-弛豫方程及与动理学描述的对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解近平衡相互作用量子系统中近似守恒荷如何弛豫，是连接非平衡统计力学与动理学描述的核心问题；作者希望厘清导致局域弛豫律的物理假设，并为实际计算提供更简洁的替代方案。

Method: 在单个非守恒荷的最小设定下综述Zubarev非平衡统计算符方法；明确弱荷破坏、微观关联短时间尺度与荷弛豫长时间尺度的分离、微观记忆丢失等假设；给出基于中间时间尺度演化的更简单局域平衡构造；并将荷密度在空间上变化以导出扩散-弛豫方程。

Result: 证明在所述假设下主导弛豫率由破坏荷守恒算符的平衡关联函数决定；两种方法给出相同结果；将弛豫率与同一荷的平衡扩散相联系；引入空间变化后得到扩散-弛豫方程；通过电弱B+L washout与微扰标量模型验证，与线性化Boltzmann方程一致。

Conclusion: Zubarev方法与简化局域平衡构造在明确物理假设下等价，弛豫率可由平衡关联函数计算并联系扩散；该框架在电弱washout和标量模型中证明了平衡关联函数描述与动理学描述之间的直接联系。

Abstract: We investigate the relaxation of an approximately conserved charge in interacting quantum systems close to local equilibrium. To this end, we provide a pedagogical review of Zubarev's non-equilibrium statistical operator approach in the minimal setting of a single non-conserved charge and apply it to the problem at hand. We explicitly highlight the physical assumptions that lead to a local relaxation law: weak charge violation, a separation between the short timescale of microscopic correlations and the much longer timescale of charge relaxation, and the resulting loss of microscopic memory. Under these conditions, the leading relaxation rate is determined by an equilibrium correlation function of the charge-violating operator. We show that the same result follows from a simpler local-equilibrium construction based on the system's evolution over an intermediate timescale, providing a direct alternative for practical calculations and making the common physical ingredients of the two approaches explicit. Beyond the decay law itself, we relate the relaxation rate to the equilibrium diffusion of the same charge. We then allow the charge density to vary in space, which leads to a diffusion-relaxation equation. Finally, we illustrate the formalism through electroweak $\mathrm{B+L}$ washout and a perturbative scalar model, where agreement with the linearized Boltzmann equation establishes a direct connection between equilibrium-correlator and kinetic descriptions.

</details>


### [212] [Unitarity and the Forward Direction in Theories with Long-Range Forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16896)
*Luke Lippstreu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在长程力理论中，前向散射振幅积分及幺正性约束常呈现红外尺度依赖性。本文通过一个非相对论模型，展示了如何用改进的分布结构与畸变波微扰论（DWPT）无红外尺度地微扰推导EFT耦合界限，结果快速收敛至精确界限（四阶达0.002%精度），且DWPT各阶将无穷多费曼图重求和为紧凑表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在含长程力的理论中，对前向散射振幅积分及由此提取的幺正性约束存在模糊性：标准EFT耦合界限技术产生的界限通常依赖于任意红外尺度。作者希望找到一种无红外尺度、能给出确定界限的推导方法，并理解长程振幅的分布结构与微扰展开的关系。

Method: 采用一个简单的非相对论模型，其标准技术会产生模糊界限，但精确界限可非微扰导出且不含红外尺度。为微扰推导无红外尺度的界限，引入两个要素：(1) 处理长程振幅修正后的分布结构；(2) 使用畸变波微扰论（DWPT），将库仑动力学精确处理。二者结合使振幅具有良定义的之分波投影且无虚假红外发散。将模型视为具有紫外截断Λ_EFT的EFT，推导适用于任意紫外完备的截断依赖界限，并在各阶取Λ_EFT→∞。

Result: 取Λ_EFT→∞时，各阶界限快速收敛至精确界限，四阶展开达到0.002%精度。此外证明DWPT展开的每一阶都将短程微扰论中无穷多费曼图（各自对应多重多对数与完全椭圆积分）重求和为紧凑表达式；在计算到的阶数，甚至无需积分。

Conclusion: 结合修正分布结构与DWPT可在无红外尺度下微扰推导EFT耦合界限，界限随阶数快速收敛至精确值。DWPT将无穷多复杂费曼图重求和为紧凑形式，且无需积分，表明DWPT可能比标准微扰论提供更简单的散射振幅表示。

Abstract: Integrating scattering amplitudes over the forward direction, and consequently the unitarity constraints one extracts from such integrals, can appear ambiguous in theories with long-range forces. Standard techniques for bounding EFT couplings then typically produce bounds that depend on an arbitrary infrared scale. We study a non-relativistic model in which the standard techniques produce such an ambiguous bound, but which is simple enough that the exact bound can also be derived non-perturbatively and shown to involve no infrared scale. We then show how to derive bounds perturbatively, with no infrared scale entering at any stage. This requires two ingredients: accounting for the modified distributional structure of long-range amplitudes, and using distorted-wave perturbation theory (DWPT), which treats the Coulomb dynamics exactly. Together they give amplitudes with well-defined partial-wave projections and no spurious infrared divergences. Interpreting the model as an EFT with an ultraviolet cutoff $Λ_{\rm EFT}$, we derive cutoff-dependent bounds valid for arbitrary UV completions. Taking $Λ_{\rm EFT}\to\infty$ at each order yields bounds that rapidly converge to the exact bound, reaching $0.002\%$ accuracy at fourth order in the expansion. Finally, we demonstrate that every order of the DWPT expansion resums infinitely many Feynman diagrams of short-range perturbation theory, which individually evaluate to multiple polylogarithms and complete elliptic integrals, into a compact expression. At the orders we compute, no integration is even required, suggesting that DWPT may offer a simpler representation of scattering amplitudes than standard perturbation theory.

</details>


### [213] [Positivity Bounds in $\mathcal N =1$ Supersymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16924)
*Jingxuan Bu,Jiayin Gu,Runqing Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将正性边界（positivity bounds）自举框架推广至 $\mathcal N=1$ 超对称理论，利用超对称Ward恒等式导出不同 $2\to2$ 散射过程Wilson系数正性边界之间的相互约束关系，并以量纲8算符为例具体展示标量、费米子和规范场四次相互作用的正性边界及其关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低能有效场论（EFT）若要具备自洽的紫外完备，须满足局域性、幺正性、解析性和洛伦兹不变性，由此通过 $2\to2$ 前向弹性振幅的色散关系对Wilson系数施加正性约束，这对理论自洽性与实验唯象均有重要意义。然而，以往的自举分析多针对非超对称理论，尚未系统研究超对称性如何通过超对称Ward恒等式在组分振幅间引入线性关系、进而使不同过程的正性边界相互依赖。本文旨在填补这一空白，将正性边界自举推广到 $\mathcal N=1$ 超对称情形。

Method: 将正性边界自举框架扩展至 $\mathcal N=1$ 超对称理论。基于超庞加莱代数将同一超多重态内的玻色与费米自由度联系起来；利用超对称Ward恒等式（SWIs）在组分散射振幅之间施加严格的线性关系，从而约束不同 $2\to2$ 过程对应的Wilson系数，使其各自的正性边界相互依赖。聚焦量纲8算符，显式推导涉及标量、费米子与规范场的四次相互作用的正性边界，并论证它们如何由SWIs直接关联。

Result: 显式给出了量纲8算符下，涉及标量、费米子和规范场的四次相互作用的正性边界，并证明这些边界之间的关联是超对称Ward恒等式直接导致的结果，即不同 $2\to2$ 过程的Wilson系数正性边界并非独立而是相互依存。

Conclusion: 该框架的代数性质有望自然推广至圈级超振幅与更高点过程，为超对称EFT提供统一的自举视角。这说明超对称性不仅带来新的正性约束，还通过SWIs将原本独立的正性边界组织成一个相互关联的整体结构。

Abstract: For a low-energy Effective Field Theory (EFT) to admit a consistent ultraviolet (UV) completion, it must adhere to the fundamental principles of locality, unitarity, analyticity, and Lorentz invariance. This leads to positivity constraints on certain Wilson coefficients via dispersion relations of $2\rightarrow2$ forward elastic amplitudes, which carry significant implications for both theoretical consistency and experimental phenomenology. In this work, we extend this bootstrap framework to $\mathcal N = 1$ supersymmetric theories, where the super-Poincaré algebra relates the bosonic and fermionic degrees of freedom within a single supermultiplet. Supersymmetric Ward identities (SWIs) enforce exact linear relations among component scattering amplitudes. Consequently, the Wilson coefficients associated with different $2\rightarrow2$ processes are mutually constrained, rendering their respective positivity bounds dependent. Focusing on dimension-8 operators, we explicitly show the positivity bounds on quartic interactions involving scalars, fermions and gauge fields, and demonstrate how they are related as a direct consequence of the SWIs. Furthermore, we expect that the algebraic nature of this framework naturally extends to loop-level superamplitudes and higher-point processes, providing a unified bootstrap perspective on supersymmetric EFTs.

</details>


### [214] [Constraints on Kasner Exponents from Holography and Energy Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16980)
*Zi-Hao Li,Run-Qiu Yang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨平面AdS黑洞内部近奇点Kasner标度能否作为自下而上全息对偶一致性的诊断工具，通过对CV、CA、HM熵和热a函数等内部敏感探针的度规组合分析，导出Kasner指数的代数界，并证明经典能量条件可作为保证指数落入全息允许区域的充分判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自下而上全息的核心问题是判断给定体有效理论是否允许一致的全息对偶。需要寻找能够区分允许与不允许体理论的诊断工具，而黑洞内部近奇点区域的Kasner标度可能提供此类判据。

Method: 考察控制内部敏感可观测量的度规组合，包括Complexity=Volume (CV)、Complexity=Action (CA)的体贡献、Hartman-Maldacena (HM)熵以及热a函数；对CV和HM的有限半径晚期分支、有限CA复杂度、有限近奇点热a函数等半经典区域，推导Kasner指数(p_t,p_s)的代数界，并分析零能量条件与主导能量条件作为充分判据。

Result: 得到刻画Kasner空间区域的代数不等式，界定仅由终端标度即可实现上述行为的区域；并证明零能量条件与主导能量条件可提供简单充分判据，确保Kasner指数落在全息允许区域内。

Conclusion: 建立经典体能量条件与全息探针所选内部几何之间的直接联系，表明Kasner标度可作为自下而上全息中的互补诊断工具。

Abstract: A central question in bottom-up holography is whether a given bulk effective theory admits a consistent holographic dual. In this work, we explore whether the near-singularity Kasner scaling of planar AdS black hole interiors can serve as a useful diagnostic based on interior-sensitive holographic probes. By examining the metric combinations that control interior-sensitive observables, including Complexity=Volume (CV), the bulk contribution to Complexity=Action (CA), Hartman-Maldacena (HM) entropy, and the thermal $a$-function, we derive algebraic bounds on the Kasner exponents $(p_t,p_s)$ for the relevant semi-classical regimes: finite-radius late-time branches of CV and HM, finite CA complexity, and a finite near-singularity thermal $a$-function. The resulting inequalities delineate the corresponding regions of Kasner space in which the terminal scaling alone is sufficient to realize these behaviors. Furthermore, we demonstrate that classical energy conditions, specifically the null and dominant energy conditions, provide simple sufficient criteria ensuring that the Kasner exponents fall within these holographically allowed regions. These results establish a direct connection between classical bulk energy conditions and the interior geometry selected by holographic probes, and suggest that Kasner scaling can provide a complementary diagnostic in bottom-up holography.

</details>


### [215] [Carrollian Conformal Dynamics and Flat-Space Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17086)
*Simon Pekar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在共形Carroll几何框架下发展引力动力学：利用度规-仿射表述与超动量处理无挠Carroll联络的独立自由度，将其应用于四维渐近平坦时空，在BMS规范下由Carroll演化方程导出标准Bondi质量与角动量演化方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近平坦时空的共形边界是类零的，携带Carroll几何而非Lorentz几何。与Lorentz Levi-Civita联络不同，与共形结构相容的无挠Carroll联络含有真正独立的自由度，这从本质上改变了对称性、变分原理与演化方程之间的关系。作者希望建立一个边界内蕴的框架，刻画这些联络自由度并推导引力演化方程。

Method: 提出共形动力学的度规-仿射表述，通过超动量（hypermomenta）追踪联络自由度；要求在局域Carroll助推、旋转、Weyl变换与微分同胚下协变，从而得到一组协变演化方程。随后将框架应用于四维渐近平坦时空中的Einstein引力，借助Penrose共形紧化将类零无穷识别为共形Carroll流形，并把相容联络中未确定的横向对称部分等同于渐近Bondi剪切；在固定BMS规范后，将Carroll动量与超动量对应到Bondi数据。

Result: 在该框架下，一般的Carroll演化方程在BMS规范中重现了质量与角动量方面的标准演化方程，其中未定的横向对称联络部分即Bondi剪切。由此给出了引力演化方程的边界内蕴推导，并在平坦空间全息中实现了辐射自由度的几何刻画。

Conclusion: 渐近平坦时空的共形边界应视为共形Carroll流形，其相容Carroll联络的独立自由度具有物理意义（渐近Bondi剪切）。度规-仿射共形Carroll框架能够从边界内蕴地导出标准Bondi演化方程，为平坦空间全息中的辐射自由度提供几何实现。该文为"Athens Workshop in Theoretical Physics: 10th Anniversary"（2025年12月17-19日，雅典国立卡波季斯特里安大学）会议论文集投稿。

Abstract: The conformal boundary of asymptotically flat spacetime is null and therefore carries a Carrollian rather than Lorentzian geometry. This changes the relation between symmetry, variational principles and evolution equations in an essential way: unlike a Lorentzian Levi-Civita connection, a torsion-free Carrollian connection compatible with the conformal structure contains genuine independent data. We develop a metric-affine formulation of conformal dynamics that keeps track of these connection degrees of freedom through hypermomenta. Local Carroll boosts, rotations, Weyl transformations and diffeomorphisms then imply a set of covariant evolution equations. We apply this framework to Einstein gravity in four-dimensional asymptotically flat spacetimes. Penrose's conformal compactification identifies null infinity as a conformal Carrollian manifold; the undetermined transverse symmetric part of its compatible connection is the asymptotic Bondi shear. After fixing a Bondi-van der Burg-Metzner-Sachs (BMS) frame and identifying the Carrollian momenta and hypermomenta with the Bondi data, the general Carrollian evolution equations reproduce the standard evolution equations for the mass and angular-momentum aspects. The result provides a boundary-intrinsic derivation of the gravitational evolution equations and a geometric realization of radiative degrees of freedom in flat-space holography. This is a proceedings contribution to the "Athens Workshop in Theoretical Physics: 10th Anniversary", held at the National and Kapodistrian University of Athens on December 17-19 2025.

</details>


### [216] [Supersymmetric extension of the Hull--Lambert--Sen chiral boson on the 2-Torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17161)
*Amine El Amri,Pietro Antonio Grassi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将Hull-Lambert-Sen手征玻色子模型推广至超对称情形，基于Hull的双度规框架在双环面上显式构造理论，并分析其与外源耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手征玻色子量化存在困难，Hull的双度规表述提供了有力且可处理的框架，作者希望检验该框架能否自然容纳超对称推广。

Method: 采用Hull提出的双度规形式，作为手征玻色子量化的框架，并将其超对称化；在双环面（two-torus）上显式构造该理论，同时引入并分析其与外部源的耦合。

Result: 成功给出Hull-Lambert-Sen手征玻色子模型的超对称扩展，表明双度规框架允许自然的超对称推广，并得到双环面上的显式理论构造及外源耦合行为。

Conclusion: 双度规表述是手征玻色子超对称推广的合适且可处理的出发点，所构造模型为进一步研究手征玻色子量化及其相互作用提供了基础。

Abstract: We present a supersymmetric extension of the Hull-Lambert-Sen chiral boson model. The two-metric formulation developed by Hull provides a powerful and tractable framework for the quantization of chiral bosons, and we demonstrate that it admits a natural supersymmetric generalization. We construct the theory explicitly on the two-torus and analyze its coupling to external sources.

</details>


### [217] [Generalized complexity and dynamical response in holographic Vaidya spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17182)
*Mojtaba Shahbazi,Monireh Emami*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究光滑Vaidya几何下的"Complexity=Anything"，以Weyl平方泛函为主要对象，发现复杂度候选量在4维、5维RN及5维GB时空中同时依赖r和v（"doubled complexity"）；通过Fefferman-Graham展开证明前四个FG系数在静态与动态解之间相消，首个非零贡献出现在第五个系数，由边界应力张量及其导数控制；线性响应下该响应由推迟应力张量关联函数主导，并可通过广义Kramers-Kronig关系用谱密度表示，从而建立广义全息复杂度与动力学应力张量响应之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态解中复杂度候选量通常仅依赖径向坐标r，难以刻画动态时空中的演化。作者希望将"Complexity=Anything"推广到光滑Vaidya等动态几何，明确复杂度在动态背景下的结构，并揭示其与边界场论动力学响应（应力张量关联函数、谱密度）之间的对应关系。

Method: 对4维、5维Reissner-Nordström及5维Gauss-Bonnet时空中的复杂度候选进行数值分析，考察其(r,v)依赖；在渐近AdS区域使用Fefferman-Graham展开，对bulk度规、极值嵌入、诱导度规、法向量和外曲率同步展开，比较静态解与Vaidya动态解的各阶FG系数；在线性响应框架下将响应与推迟应力张量关联函数及广义Kramers-Kronig关系相联系。

Result: 数值分析显示复杂度候选在4维、5维RN和5维GB时空中均随r和v共同演化，即出现"doubled complexity"，不同于静态解仅依赖r。FG展开表明静态解与动态解之间前四个FG系数相消，首个非零贡献出现在第五个系数，由边界应力张量及其导数控制。Vaidya淬火期间，导数项编码边界态的含时响应；在线性响应下，该响应由推迟应力张量关联函数主导，并可通过广义Kramers-Kronig关系用谱密度表示。

Conclusion: 研究确立了广义全息复杂度与边界动力学应力张量响应之间的联系；在线性响应区，该联系可由相应的应力张量谱密度表示，为动态时空中的全息复杂度提供了一种可计算的边界刻画。

Abstract: We investigate "Complexity=Anything" for smooth Vaidya geometries, using the Weyl squared functional as the most common candidate for our study. Our numerical analysis of candidates in 4- and 5-dimensional Reissner-Nordström (RN) and 5-dimensional Gauss-Bonnet (GB) shows evolution in both $r$ and $v$ ("doubled complexity"), unlike static solutions that depend only on $r$. We then study the difference between the complexity of static and dynamical solutions in the asymptotically AdS regime using a Fefferman-Graham expansion. By expanding the bulk metric, extremal embedding, induced metric, normal vector, and extrinsic curvature simultaneously, we show that the first four FG coefficients cancel between the two geometries. In contrast, the first nonvanishing contribution occurs at the fifth coefficient and is controlled by the boundary stress tensor and its derivatives. During the Vaidya quench, the derivative contribution encodes the time-dependent response of the boundary state. In linear response, this response is governed by the retarded stress-tensor correlator and, through generalized Kramers-Kronig relations, can be represented in terms of its spectral density. We thus identify a connection between generalized holographic complexity and the dynamical stress-tensor response, which in the linear-response regime can be represented in terms of the corresponding stress-tensor spectral density.

</details>


### [218] [Cohomological Aspects of Entanglement Entropy: From Information Theory to Noncommutative Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17195)
*Radoslav C. Rashkov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了纠缠熵的上同调框架，将信息论、算子代数与非交换几何统一起来，并以Connes-Radon-Nikodym余圈作为编码相对纠缠的基本对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纠缠熵描述多基于密度矩阵，难以处理不存在局部密度矩阵的Type III von Neumann代数情形（如量子场论与全息），需要一个更普适的代数与几何框架来严格刻画纠缠。

Method: 从熵作为1-余圈的信息论刻画出发，借助Hochschild与循环上同调推广至量子情形；通过条件期望将纠缠复形嵌入Connes循环双复形，把纠缠上同调识别为von Neumann代数到其子代数限制映射的核，并利用Tomita-Takesaki模理论与Connes-Radon-Nikodym余圈提供动力学结构和相对纠缠的编码。

Result: 建立了纠缠复形到Connes循环双复形的嵌入，证明纠缠上同调等于限制映射的核，明确Connes-Radon-Nikodym余圈为相对纠缠的基本对象，且框架自然容纳无局部密度矩阵的Type III von Neumann代数。

Conclusion: 该上同调框架为量子场论与全息中的纠缠提供了严格基础，统一了信息论、算子代数与非交换几何视角。

Abstract: We develop a cohomological framework for entanglement entropy that unifies perspectives from information theory, operator algebras, and noncommutative geometry. Starting from the information-theoretic characterization of entropy as a 1-cocycle, we show how this structure generalizes to the quantum setting through Hochschild and cyclic cohomology. A central result is the embedding of an entanglement complex into the Connes cyclic bicomplex via a conditional expectation, identifying entanglement cohomology as the kernel of the restriction map from a von Neumann algebra to its subalgebra. The Tomita-Takesaki modular theory provides the dynamical structure, with the Connes-Radon-Nikodym cocycle serving as the fundamental object encoding relative entanglement. This framework naturally accommodates Type III von Neumann algebras, where no local density matrix exists, offering a rigorous foundation for entanglement in quantum field theory and holography.

</details>


### [219] [Low-energy brane decoupling in AdS flux vacua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17342)
*Fien Apers,Noelia Sánchez González*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 通过标量波吸收概率研究膜与AdS通量真空渐近bulk的解耦，发现在任何陡度和维度大于2时低能极限下吸收概率消失，膜与bulk解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AdS通量真空中sourcing膜与渐近bulk是否解耦，该问题关系到膜的内禀性质能否用低维有效理论描述。

Method: 采用标量波吸收概率方法，在单模截断下计算，结果仅依赖于AdS真空维度和标量势的渐近陡度。

Result: 对任意陡度和维度大于2，低能极限下吸收概率消失，表明膜解耦；DGKT中P_abs~ω^{27/7}，有效横向维度d_eff=20/7，且沿膜诱导模轨迹的标量势与将假想(4+13/7)维引力理论紧化在带通量的13/7维球面上所得一致；分析也应用于尺度分离AdS_3例子及最简单模KKLT模型。

Conclusion: 膜在所有维度大于2的AdS通量真空中普遍与bulk解耦，DGKT等尺度分离真空展现特殊维度结构，支持低维有效描述的自洽性。

Abstract: We study the decoupling of branes sourcing AdS flux vacua from the asymptotic bulk using scalar-wave absorption probabilities. In a one-modulus truncation, the result depends only on the dimension of the AdS vacuum and on the asymptotic steepness of the scalar potential. We find that for any steepness and for any dimension greater than two the absorption probability vanishes in the low-energy limit, showing that the branes decouple from the bulk. We illustrate the general analysis in several scale-separated AdS vacua. For DGKT we find $P_{\rm abs}\simω^{27/7}$ and an effective transverse dimension $d_{\rm eff}=20/7$. Interestingly, the scalar potential along the brane-induced moduli trajectory agrees with that obtained by compactifying a hypothetical $(4+13/7)$-dimensional gravity theory on a $13/7$-dimensional sphere threaded by flux. We also apply the analysis to scale-separated $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ examples and to the simplest one-modulus KKLT model.

</details>


### [220] [Topological Modular Forms Constrain Threshold States in Extremal $\mathcal{N}=1$ AdS$_3$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17359)
*Yutaka Yoshida*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文揭示极端 N=1 AdS₃ 谱的一个拓扑约束：该约束不由三个偶自旋结构配分函数的亏格一模协变性所隐含。在假定 Ramond Witten 指数的拓扑模形式可除性下，证明对无穷多个奇数 n（中心荷 c=12n），极端全纯 N=1 超共形场论在 BTZ 阈值处需要奇数个 Neveu–Schwarz 原初场，从而排除了无阈值原初场的严格 ansatz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端全纯超共形场论的谱通常受模协变性约束，且常假设其谱中可缺失阈值（BTZ 阈值）处的原初场。作者希望检验这一"严格 ansatz"是否在所有中心荷下都自洽，或是否存在仅靠亏格一模协变性无法捕捉的更强拓扑约束。

Method: 以三个偶自旋结构配分函数的亏格一模协变性为出发点，引入 Ramond Witten 指数的拓扑模形式可除性假设，对中心荷 c=12n（n 为奇数）的极端全纯 N=1 超共形场论进行分析，考察 BTZ 阈值处 Neveu–Schwarz 原初场的计数奇偶性。

Result: 发现一个不被亏格一模协变性所隐含的拓扑约束。对无穷多个奇数 n、c=12n 的情形，极端全纯 N=1 超共形场论要求 BTZ 阈值处存在奇数个 Neveu–Schwarz 原初场。

Conclusion: 无阈值原初场的严格 ansatz 对这一无穷族被排除；模协变性不足以保证该 ansatz 自洽，需额外的拓扑约束。

Abstract: We identify a topological constraint on extremal $\mathcal{N}=1$ AdS$_3$ spectra that is not implied by genus-one modular covariance of the three even spin structure partition functions. Assuming the expected topological modular forms divisibility of the Ramond Witten index, we show that for infinitely many odd $n$ at central charge $c=12n$, an extremal holomorphic $\mathcal{N}=1$ superconformal field theory requires an odd number of Neveu--Schwarz primaries at the BTZ threshold. Thus the strict ansatz with no threshold primaries is excluded for this infinite family.

</details>


### [221] [Channel-Resolved High-Overtone Quasinormal Modes Decode Kasner Scaling inside Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17426)
*Zi-Qing Xiao,Jun Nian,Li Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 高泛音准正规模谱编码了黑洞内部近奇点的Kasner标度，可独立重建时间与空间Kasner指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞内部近奇点几何难以直接观测，需寻找可观测谱特征来探测视界后的局部几何。

Method: 分析高泛音准正规模谱结构，将其分解为全局谱数据与局部分数幂代数修正，在五维渐近平直Schwarzschild、平面Schwarzschild-AdS5及连续变化的Gibbons-Maeda黑洞族中重建Kasner指数。

Result: 时间与空间Kasner等级可由谱中不同结构独立确定，无需施加Kasner约束或借助全息；重建指数事后满足Kasner关系，提供几何一致性检验。

Conclusion: 高泛音谱中分数幂修正为视界后局部几何提供直接谱探针。

Abstract: The high-overtone quasinormal-mode spectrum encodes the near-singularity Kasner scaling of black-hole interiors. We show that distinct structures in this spectrum independently determine the temporal and spatial Kasner grades, allowing the corresponding metric exponents to be reconstructed without imposing Kasner constraints or invoking holography. We demonstrate this independent reconstruction at the fixed Kasner point of five-dimensional asymptotically flat Schwarzschild and planar Schwarzschild--AdS$_5$, and then extend it to a continuously varying Gibbons--Maeda black-hole family. The reconstructed exponents satisfy the Kasner relation a posteriori, providing a nontrivial geometric consistency check. More generally, the high-overtone spectrum separates into global spectral data and local algebraic corrections whose fractional powers are fixed by the near-singularity Kasner geometry. These fractional corrections therefore provide a direct spectral probe of the local geometry behind the horizon.

</details>


### [222] [Exact Black Branes in General Dimensions in Higher-Curvature Scalar-Tensor Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17528)
*Tianhao Wu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在无势dilaton耦合的Lovelock–Horndeski理论中构造了两族中性平面黑膜，发现固定标量零模时Wald熵恒定且为正，并给出了几何解释与耦合关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质紧化将高维曲率与标量动力学联系起来，促使研究具有特定相互作用结构的dilaton耦合Lovelock–Horndeski理论中的中性平面黑膜解。

Method: 采用无势dilaton耦合的Lovelock–Horndeski理论，通过精确系数分析与闭合耦合关系，确定四维分支及其向所有高维的延拓，并考察线性与分数两族解及标量零模下的熵行为。

Result: 得到两族中性平面黑膜；固定标量零模时视界半径与温度变化而完整静态Wald熵保持恒定且为正，指数标量因子在视界处恰好补偿面积增长。线性族共形平坦且固定耦合下AdS标度自由；分数族具有Weyl曲率并固定该标度。共同动能关系排除其由度量–dilaton母体的实单标量平环紧化实现。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了两族黑膜的精确耦合结构，说明Wald熵恒定的几何机制，并区分了两个四维分支的共形性质及其可延拓性。

Abstract: We obtain two families of neutral planar black branes in a potential-free dilaton-coupled Lovelock--Horndeski theory. Heterotic compactification relates higher-dimensional curvature to scalar dynamics and motivates the interaction structure. At fixed scalar zero mode, the horizon radius and temperature vary while the complete stationary Wald entropy remains constant and positive. The exponential scalar factor at the horizon exactly compensates the area growth, giving this behavior a direct geometric explanation. An exact coefficient analysis determines the two four-dimensional branches and their continuation to every higher dimension through closed coupling relations. The linear family is conformally flat and leaves the AdS scale free at fixed couplings; the fractional family is Weyl curved and fixes that scale. The common kinetic relation also excludes their realization through a real one-scalar flat-torus reduction of a metric--dilaton parent.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [223] [Gauged AKSZ sigma models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16043)
*Noriaki Ikeda*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的sigma模型——gauged AKSZ sigma模型，利用QP-流形构造，并研究了其与规范化的兼容性及高维Courant sigma模型的规范化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展AKSZ sigma模型，构造规范化的AKSZ sigma模型，以统一处理规范化结构与AKSZ形式体系。

Method: 采用QP-流形（Q-manifold配Poisson结构）框架，通过目标分次流形上的几何条件刻画AKSZ结构与规范化的兼容性，并具体分析高维高次Courant sigma模型的规范化。

Result: 建立了gauged AKSZ sigma模型的构造框架；明确了兼容性的几何条件；发现当引入通量与边界时，动量映射和Hamiltonian G-空间的推广作为模型的一致性条件自然出现。

Conclusion: gauged AKSZ sigma模型为规范化AKSZ理论提供了统一框架，其一致性条件推广了动量映射与Hamiltonian G-空间的概念。

Abstract: We propose a new class of sigma models that extend AKSZ sigma models, which we refer to as gauged AKSZ sigma models. These models are constructed using QP-manifolds. The compatibility between the AKSZ structure and gauging is characterized by geometric conditions on the target graded manifold. As an example, we explicitly analyze the gauging of higher Courant sigma models in higher dimensions. When fluxes and boundaries are introduced into gauged higher Courant sigma models, generalizations of momentum maps and Hamiltonian $G$-spaces arise as consistency conditions for the models.

</details>


### [224] [Reflectionless edge states in Dirac models of topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16949)
*Alexis Drouot*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究沿弯曲界面拼接的拓扑绝缘体有效模型，构造出无反射边缘态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释物理文献中关于弯曲界面拓扑绝缘体边缘态无损耗输运的实验观察，并提供数值模拟支持。

Method: 分析 $L^2(\mathbb{R}^2,\mathbb{C}^2)$ 上的 Dirac 算子 $\mathcal{D}=D_1\sigma_1+D_2\sigma_2+\kappa(x)\sigma_3$，其中 $\kappa(x)$ 描述带拐角的域壁，在谱窗口内的大部分能量处构造无反射边缘态（模为 1 的透射系数的畸变平面波）。

Result: 证明了 $\mathcal{D}$ 存在局域在 $\kappa^{-1}(\{0\})$ 附近的模式，这些模式穿过弯折处既不泄漏到体态也不发生反射。

Conclusion: 理论结果解释了物理实验观察，并与作者的数值模拟一致，表明弯曲界面拓扑边缘态具有无损耗、无反射的输运特性。

Abstract: We study an effective model of topological insulators joined along a bent interface. The Hamiltonian is a Dirac operator $\mathcal{D} = D_1σ_1 + D_2σ_2 + κ(x) σ_3$ on $L^2(\mathbb{R}^2,\mathbb{C}^2)$, where $κ(x)$ represents a domain wall with a corner: while initially straight, it undergoes a bend when passing from one side of $\mathbb{R}^2$ to the other. For most energies in a spectral window centered at $0$, we construct a reflectionless edge state of $\mathcal{D}$: a distorted plane wave with transmission coefficient of modulus $1$. This implies that $\mathcal{D}$ admits modes confined near $κ^{-1}(\{0\})$, that travel through the bend with no loss neither to the bulk nor to reflection. Our result explains experimental observations from the physics literature, as well as our own numerical simulations.

</details>


### [225] [Operator-theoretic and analytic properties of a driven single-mode Kerr cavity in Bargmann space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16969)
*Maciej Janowicz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究相干驱动单模Kerr腔在Bargmann–Fock空间中的定态谱：正Kerr耦合下哈密顿量自伴、下有界且具紧预解；非零驱动时用极小二解连分式给出精确标量谱条件，谱简单；经Liouville变换联系于退化双合流Heun方程并分类奇点；Olver型Volterra构造给出无穷远处归一化扇形解及误差界；强驱动下证明领头转折点能量系数，次领头系数依赖全局连接猜想；弱驱动展开由Bargmann递推验证并闭式求和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究相干驱动单模Kerr腔的定态谱结构，建立严格的谱条件与奇异微分方程联系，厘清强驱动与弱驱动区域的可证结果与仍需猜想的边界。

Method: 在Bargmann–Fock空间表述哈密顿量；利用极小二解连分式导出精确标量谱条件；通过Liouville变换将本征值方程化为退化双合流Heun问题并分类奇点；采用Olver型Volterra构造无穷远处归一化扇形解并给出显式误差界；结合Bogoliubov与复WKB计算作一致性检验；由Bargmann递推验证弱驱动展开并闭式求和上边缘三对角级数。

Result: 正Kerr耦合哈密顿量自伴、下有界、具紧预解；非零驱动下谱条件精确且所有本征值简单；退化双合流Heun奇点分类完成；无穷远处得到带误差界的归一化扇形解；强驱动领头转折点能量系数得证，次领头系数在全局连接猜想下成立；弱驱动展开获验证且上边缘三对角级数闭式求和。

Conclusion: 该工作为驱动Kerr腔定态谱提供了严格基础与可证结构；强驱动次领头系数仍是条件性结果，探索性WKB迭代图像仅作可视化辅助，不构成对全局连接猜想的证明。

Abstract: We study the stationary spectrum of a coherently driven single-mode Kerr cavity in Bargmann--Fock space. For positive Kerr coupling the Hamiltonian is self-adjoint, bounded below, and has compact resolvent. For nonzero drive, a minimal-solution continued fraction yields an exact scalar spectral condition, with all eigenvalues simple. A Liouville transformation identifies the eigenvalue equation with a degenerate double-confluent Heun problem and classifies its singularities. An Olver-type Volterra construction gives normalized sectorial solutions at infinity with explicit error bounds. In the strong-drive regime, the leading turning-point energy coefficient is proved, whereas the next coefficient remains conditional on a stated global-connection conjecture; independent Bogoliubov and complex-WKB calculations provide consistency checks. A formal weak-drive expansion is verified from the Bargmann recurrence, and its upper-edge tridiagonal series is summed in closed form. Exploratory WKB iteration portraits are included only as visual aids and do not establish the conjectural global connection.

</details>


### [226] [Diassociative conformal algebras and their deformation cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17441)
*Anupam Sahoo,Apurba Das*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文引入双结合共形代数，统一了结合共形代数与双结合代数，建立其范畴等价、上同调与Gerstenhaber代数结构，并应用于形变、扩张及平均算子研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合共形代数与双结合代数分属不同研究框架，缺乏统一结构；为统一二者并系统研究其上同调、形变与扩张理论，文章提出双结合共形代数。

Method: 定义双结合共形代数；证明其范畴与形式分布双结合代数的等价类范畴等价；利用Maurer-Cartan刻画定义上同调；证明其上具Gerstenhaber代数结构；定义结合共形代数上的平均算子并建立其与双结合共形代数的联系。

Result: 双结合共形代数范畴与形式分布双结合代数的等价类范畴等价；其上同调具Gerstenhaber代数结构，可用于研究该结构的形变与扩张；结合共形代数上的平均算子与双结合共形代数密切相关。

Conclusion: 双结合共形代数为结合共形代数与双结合代数提供了统一框架，其丰富代数结构（Gerstenhaber、形变、扩张、平均算子）为进一步研究提供了有力工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce diassociative conformal algebras that unify both associative conformal algebras and diassociative algebras. We show that the category of diassociative conformal algebras is equivalent to the category of equivalence classes of formal distribution diassociative algebras. By employing its Maurer-Cartan characterization, we then define the cohomology of a diassociative conformal algebra and show that it carries a Gerstenhaber algebra structure. Our cohomology can be used to study deformations and extensions of the structure. Finally, we define averaging operators on associative conformal algebras and find their close relations with diassociative conformal algebras.

</details>


### [227] [Higher abelian gauge theory in BV formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17518)
*Shuhan Jiang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二次椭圆高阶阿贝尔规范理论的零模空间上构造了BV结构，证明相应BV积分在温和假设下不依赖于零模拉格朗日量的选择，并对若干p-形式阿贝尔规范理论给出显式零模配分函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶阿贝尔规范理论的零模处理是量子化中的关键难点；零模空间上缺乏系统化的BV（Batalin-Vilkovisky）框架，导致零模配分函数的定义依赖人为选择，亟需建立不依赖于具体选取的构造。

Method: 在零模空间上构造BV结构（包括反括号与BRST算子），定义相应的BV积分，并在温和假设下论证该积分与零模拉格朗日量选取无关；随后将框架应用于若干p-形式阿贝尔规范理论。

Result: 证明了BV积分对零模拉格朗日量选择的独立性，并给出了多个p-形式阿贝尔规范理论的显式零模配分函数。

Conclusion: 该工作为高阶阿贝尔规范理论的零模量子化提供了统一且良定义的BV框架，所构造的零模配分函数具有选择无关性，可推广至更一般的椭圆规范理论。

Abstract: A BV structure is constructed on the space of zero modes of a quadratic elliptic higher abelian gauge theory. The associated BV integral is shown to be independent of the choice of zero-mode Lagrangian under mild hypotheses. The construction yields explicit zero-mode partition functions for several $p$-form abelian gauge theories.

</details>


### [228] [Arithmetic triangular structures in the transfer-matrix of finite Kronig-Penney models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08523)
*Lidia Aceto,Pietro Antonio Grassi,Helmuth Robert Malonek*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该工作对有限Kronig-Penney模型（一维Dirac δ势阵列）的转移矩阵结构给出完整的解析刻画，利用第二类Chebyshev多项式表示单胞转移矩阵的N次幂，获得全局透射与反射振幅的显式闭式表达，并揭示量子散射过程、离散卷积模式与超复数组合结构之间的结构对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Figueroa等（2025）虽已识别出特定转移矩阵元素及相关组合系数，但缺乏相应的严格闭式表达式推导。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立完整的解析理论。

Method: 将单胞转移矩阵的N次幂用第二类Chebyshev多项式展开，从而推导全局透射和反射振幅的显式闭式表达式。

Result: 得到全局透射与反射振幅的显式闭式表示，并发现多重量子散射过程、离散卷积模式与超复数组合结构之间存在此前未被认识的结构对应关系。

Conclusion: 本文为有限Kronig-Penney模型的转移矩阵结构提供了严格的解析刻画，扩展了Figueroa等的结论，并揭示了散射、卷积与超复数组合之间的深层联系。

Abstract: This work provides a complete analytical characterization of the transfer- matrix structure associated with the finite Kronig-Penney model consisting of one-dimensional arrays of Dirac delta potentials recently introduced by Figueroa et al. (2025). Although their study identified the emergence of specific transfer-matrix entries and related combinatorial coefficients, a rig- orous derivation of the corresponding closed-form expressions has not yet been established. By expressing the N th power of the unit-cell transfer ma- trix in terms of Chebyshev polynomials of the second kind, we obtain explicit closed-form representations for the global transmission and reflection ampli- tudes. The proposed formulation reveals a previously unrecognized structural correspondence between multiple quantum scattering processes, discrete con- volutional patterns, and hypercomplex combinatorial structures.
